# Developer Program and Training Sessions Plan

Source specs: `docs/specs/developer-program.md` ("DP") and `docs/specs/training-plan.md` (Training Plan, "TS"; rewritten for curricula on 2026-10-28) · Merged from the former plans `docs/plans/developer-program.md` and `docs/plans/training-plan.md` (in git history) · Last updated: 2026-10-28

**Superseded in part (2026-10-28):** `docs/plans/curriculum-sessions-merge.md` replaced training sessions with curricula: a curriculum (`Program`) holds its participants, mentors, mentor assignments and resources, and each week carries its own dates. Design › Seeders, Data model, Session rules, Roles and permissions, Routes and Main services and screens strike the session parts with "Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge X.Y". The batch notes of Phases 8 to 12 and the Open decisions are history: they describe the code as each batch left it and are not updated.

**Status legend:** `[x]` done · `[ ]` to do · `[~]` in progress · `[-]` deferred

Each batch heading ends with its former ID, e.g. "(was TS 1.1)" or "(was DP 4.3)", so earlier discussion stays traceable.

## Goal

Turn the platform from a single QA course into a developer training platform. Remove the QA program, its data and its QA-only tools; make what stays work for any program and week; seed the 4-week *L4 Accelerator Training* with GitHub-link deliverables and a weekly mentor checklist. Then make trainings first-class: admins schedule **training sessions** (dated runs of a program) with participants, mentors and resources; participants register with their employee ID, join open sessions, and work through weeks that open by date and lock only after an *incomplete* mentor review, with submissions, scores and mentor scope kept per session. **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge:** the dated run is the curriculum itself (its weeks carry the dates), and work, scores and mentor scope are kept per curriculum.

## Rules for every batch

- Read `CLAUDE.md` **as it is on disk** before starting. It is newer than older copies you may have in context: enums in `app/Enums` are the convention for stored statuses and roles (code quality Phase 7 is done), Preflight is on, the legacy CSS classes are gone, and type discriminators such as `gate_criteria.criterion_type` stay plain strings (decided 2026-10-22 in `docs/CODE_QUALITY_PLAN.md`). Follow its "Code Quality Rules" for every new or touched line.
- Keep each batch small and independently shippable: one concern, its tests, green suite. Behavior changes only where the item says so.
- Do only the batch you were given. Record new problems under "Open decisions" instead of fixing them on the spot.
- Definition of done: `vendor/bin/pint --dirty --format agent`, the full suite passing (`php artisan test --compact`), `npm run build` if CSS, JS or Tailwind classes changed, and the item ticked here (`[x]`, or `[~]` with a short note).
- `php` is not on the default PATH in agent shells: use `~/.config/herd-lite/bin/php`.
- **Order.** The phases are in cross-spec dependency order. "Continue the plan" (or `/implement` with no scope) means the first unchecked item in the lowest unfinished phase; no batch depends on a later one.
- **Gate A — QA code and data are gone.** Cleared when Phases 1 to 4 are done (4.4 ticked). Phase 5 onward relies on it: those batches would otherwise rewrite QA code that Phases 2 to 4 delete (Validation Lab, Week 2 to 4 tools, daily logs, hardcoded week checks), and MIG-1 relies on the data deletion in 2.1.
- **Gate B — open questions.** An item marked "Blocked by Qn" waits for that answer: skip it, take the next unblocked item in the same phase, and come back when the answer is recorded under Assumptions.
- **Phase 13 — "When ready".** Its two batches have no fixed place in the order; they come last so the default "next" never stalls on them. Run them by name when their gate opens; if "next" reaches them while the gate is closed, stop and say so.
  - **Gate D — 13.1 (migration squash)** runs right after a production deploy that has run every migration, and before the next batch that adds a migration is merged. Its earliest point is after Phase 4.
  - **Gate C — 13.2 (real curriculum)** runs when the training team delivers the curriculum (Q4).
- **Wording phase.** Phase 6 (developer and mentor wording) is the only phase not needed for the first session on Mon 9 Nov 2026. If the deadline gets tight it can move to the end, but then it has more screens to reword.
- **Removal batches delete, they do not refactor** (Phase 2). Delete the feature's routes, controllers, Form Requests, policies, services, presenters, models, enums, factories, views, components, mail, config keys, sidebar links and tests, and drop its tables in a new migration. In code that stays, remove only the branches that served the feature. Never edit an old migration except to stop it referencing a deleted class (see 4.2).
- When a deleted feature leaves a kept test without purpose, delete the test case; when asserting the feature's absence is useful (route gone → 404), keep one such case.
- New migrations must run on MySQL (production) and SQLite (tests). See `database/migrations/2026_09_16_160000_fix_google_drive_evidence_export_sqlite_foreign_key.php` (in git history once 13.1 deletes it) for the SQLite foreign-key trap this codebase has already hit.
- 13.1 replaces all existing migrations with one hand-written file per table. It squashes whatever migrations exist when it runs. Migrations written after it are ordinary new files; never edit or delete the squashed files.
- New admin actions on sessions, participants, mentors and resources write an `AdminAuditLog` row through `AdminAuditService` (created in 8.2).
- New user-facing copy says "training", ~~"training session"~~, "participant" and "mentor"; never "QA", "tester" or "coach". **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge:** "curriculum" / "curricula" replaces the session term, except "Week N session" where a week is named as a session.

## Current state

### Codebase

- **Working tree:** `database/seeders/L4TrainingProgramSeeder.php` exists, untracked and **empty** (0 bytes); there are no other uncommitted code changes. Phases 1 and 2 are done.
- **One global program.** `Program` (`app/Models/Program.php`, `is_active`) has `TrainingWeek`s (`week_number`, `position`), each with `Activity`s and `TrainingGate`s. About 15 places load "the" active program with `Program::query()->where('is_active', true)` (e.g. `ParticipantDashboardController`, `AdminDashboardPageService`, `CoachDashboardPageService`, `CoachReportService`, `MissingSubmissionReportService`, `ScoringPageService`, `ScoringReportController`, `ExportParticipantScoreSummaryRequest`, `ParticipantNotificationService`). Three used `firstOrFail()` (`ParticipantDashboardController`, `ScoringReportController::participantSummary`, `ScoringPageService`); since 3.3 every reader handles no active program. **Since 12.3** none of the services named here reads the active program any more (sessions replaced it in 9.5 to 12.3); the only lookup left is `TrainingWeek::inActiveProgram()`, which feeds the week and activity filters of the review queue, Gate board, scoring and missing submissions pages and the scoring criteria page (see Open decisions).
- **QA program content** (seeder deleted in 4.2) lived in `database/seeders/QaTrainingProgramSeeder.php` (weeks 2 to 4, gates with `weekly_video` criteria). It is run by `DatabaseSeeder`, by `app:ensure-development-baseline` (`EnsureDevelopmentBaselineCommand`, used by `scripts/dev.ps1`) and by about 25 tests. The old migration `database/migrations/2026_09_10_160000_add_week3_activity_fields.php` instantiates it (`new QaTrainingProgramSeeder` for `fieldGuidance()`), so deleting the seeder breaks fresh migrations unless that line is removed.
- **QA features to remove**, with their footprint (non-migration files referencing them):
  - Validation Lab (removed in 2.2): `ValidationLabController`, `ValidationLabService`, `ValidationSubmission` (+ policy, 3 enums, 2 Form Requests), views `home`, `artifact`, `checklist`, `result`, `lab/*`, routes `/`, `/lab/*`, `/start`, `/checklist`, `/result/*`, `/lab/records/*`; `submission_versions.validation_submission_id`; `Activity::isValidationChecklist()`, the Lab prerequisite in `ActivitySubmissionService` / `TrainingActivityController` / `ActivitySubmissionController`; Lab files in `SyncTrainingDataJob` and workbook headers; the QA practice app card on the participant dashboard (`services.qa_application.url`, `$qaMembershipFeatures`).
  - `WeekTwoGateService` (replaced in 2.10 by `GateReadinessService`: activities handed in, evidence, weekly video only with a `weekly_video` criterion; daily logs removed in 2.3, `weekly_loom` in 2.10) was used by 6 services (`CoachGateOverviewService`, `CoachGateReviewPageService`, `ParticipantDashboardService`, `ParticipantGatePageService`, `ParticipantNotificationService`, `TrainingWeekPageService`) and `GateReviewController`.
  - Week 3 workspace (removed in 2.5): 4 models, `WeekThreeWorkspaceService`, `WeekThreeWorkspacePresenter`, controller, request, `submissions/week-three` view, branches in submission, scoring, review and workbook services; `WeekProgressionService::ensurePriorWeekThreeActivities()`.
  - Week 4 mock interviews: peer interviews (pairs, partner feedback, waivers, questionnaire) removed in 2.6; mentor booking removed in 2.7: 2 models (`WeekFourMentorInterviewSchedule`, `MentorInterviewAvailability`), `MentorInterviewSchedulingService`, `MentorBookingCalendarPageService`, 2 controllers, 2 Form Requests, `MentorMockInterviewScheduled` mail, the `coach/mentor-availability` view, 2 enums, `config/training.php` `week_four_mentor_interview_*` keys, `GoogleCalendarService` (only other users: `AdminSettingsStatusService`, `GoogleDriveConfigurationService`), and the mentor branches in `SubmissionWorkflowService`, `ActivitySubmissionPageService` / `submissions/edit`, `ParticipantDashboardController` / `participant/dashboard` and `AdminDashboardPageService` / `admin/dashboard`.
  - Reusable asset library (removed in 2.8): controller, 2 services, `FilterReusableAssetsRequest`, views, `EvidenceFilePolicy::reusableAsset*`, sidebar links. It had no table, column or config key of its own (it read accepted Week 4 Activity 1 submissions with `asset` evidence).
  - Guides (removed in 2.9): `TrainingGuideController`, `training-guides/*.pdf`, dashboard "Training Guide" card.
  - Daily logs (removed in 2.3 and 2.4): `DailyLog` (+ policy, factory, 5 Form Requests, `ParticipantNotificationType::DailyLogs`), 3 controllers, `DailyLogPageService`, `DailyLogReportPageService`, `CoachParticipantDailyLogPageService`, views `daily_logs/*`, `coach/daily-log-report`, `coach/daily-logs-details`, `coach/participant-daily-logs`, the participant sidebar link, `TrainingExportCoordinator::queueDailyLogs()` and `CoachReportService::participantDailyLogs()` (both used only by the daily-log pages); `config/training.php` `daily_logs_per_week`, `general_training_daily_logs_expected` (the latter unused since 2.3). The counts in dashboards, gate board/review, coach participants, missing-submission report, notifications, exports and workbooks were removed in 2.3, and so was `ParticipantNotificationType::DailyLogs`.
- **Hardcoded weeks** (about 53 matches): most disappear with the removals; the rest are `CoachGateBoardPageService::WEEK_OPTIONS`, `CoachGateOverviewService`, `CoachReviewQueuePageService::REVIEWED_WEEKS`, `ScoringPageService::SCORED_WEEKS`, `ScoringService::summaries()`, `MissingSubmissionReportService`, `ParticipantNotificationService` (`[2, 3, 4]`), `GateReviewController::guard()`, `ActivitySubmissionPageService` (week 2 check), `TrainingWorkbookService` (the participant dashboard view's week 2 to 4 guide-card check went in 2.9).
- **Scoring** uses the fixed `ScoringService::DIMENSIONS` (D1 to D5 with QA labels, max 100 total) in `StoreActivityScoreRequest`, `ScoringPageService`, `ScoringSuggestionService`, `ActivitySubmissionPageService`, `ParticipantDashboardService`; `activity_score_dimensions.dimension` is `string(2)`.
- **Gate review** (`GateReviewController`, `StoreGateReviewRequest`, `CoachGateReviewPageService`, `coach/gate_review` view): decisions `GateEvaluationStatus::decisions()` = Passed, NeedsRevision, Failed; Passed requires every required criterion Met and empty readiness; criteria saved as `GateCriterionStatus` Met / NotMet.
- **Gate-chain unlocking.** `WeekProgressionService::canAccessWeek()` opens a week only when every earlier week's gates are passed. It also drives "current week" and the attendance requirement (`requiresAttendanceForActivity`). (Replaced in 11.1: weeks open by date and lock after an incomplete review.)
- **Submission fields** come from `activity_requirements` with `requirement_type` `submission_field` and `configuration.type` (`text`, `textarea`, `url`, `select`, `date`; `participant_selector` handling removed in 2.6); drafts are validated in `StoreSubmissionDraftRequest`, submissions in `SubmissionWorkflowService`.
- **Per-user, not per-session, progress.** `activity_submissions` (`activity_id`, `user_id`, `attempt_number`), `gate_evaluations` (unique `training_gate_id` + `user_id`) and `weekly_video_recordings` (unique `user_id` + `training_week_id`) have no session. Versions, evidence, reviews, scores and recovery requests hang off `activity_submissions`. Attendance (`attendance_sessions`) is a global check-in/check-out per user.
- **Participant dashboard = today's "session page".** `ParticipantDashboardController` + `ParticipantDashboardService` + `resources/views/participant/dashboard.blade.php` render one tab per week (`x-ui.tab-list` / `x-ui.tab-panel`, `initializePageTabs` in `resources/js/app.js`) with activities, gate and video cards.
- **Routes are week-scoped, not session-scoped**: `training/weeks/{trainingWeek}/…` for weeks, activities, submissions, evidence upload, gate page, video; `training/coach/weeks/{trainingWeek}/participants/{participant}/gates/{trainingGate}` for gate review. `bootstrap/app.php` matches `training/weeks/*/video-recording` for its `PostTooLargeException` handler.
- **Global coach assignments** (retired in 10.4: mentors are assigned per session in `mentor_assignments` and checked with `User::mentorsParticipantInSession()`; the table, model, relations, route and form are gone). `coach_participant_assignments` (`CoachParticipantAssignment`, `User::assignedCoaches()` / `assignedParticipants()`), edited on the admin user form (`AdminUserController@assignments`, `UpdateParticipantAssignmentsRequest`). `User::canManageParticipant()` gates every coach write (policies `SubmissionVersionPolicy`, `GateEvaluationPolicy`; Form Requests `StoreSubmissionReviewRequest`, `StoreActivityScoreRequest`, `StoreSecondReaderScoreRequest`, `StoreGateReviewRequest`, `DecideRecoveryRequestRequest`, `ImproveScoringFeedbackRequest`; `GateReviewController`, `ScoringController`, `WeeklyVideoRecordingController`). Coach list pages show **every** participant, marking "Assigned to me".
- **Users.** `users` has `name`, `email`, `role` (`UserRole`), `training_path` (`TrainingPath`), `drive_link`, `is_active`. No employee ID, first/last name or job role. Registration (`RegisteredUserController`, `StoreRegisteredUserRequest`, `auth/register`) asks name, email, password, training path. Admin user form maps an "access type" (`admin`, `coach`, `qa_manual`, `qa_automation`, `qa_automation_manual`) to role + path. Login (`AuthenticatedSessionController`) redirects by role.
- **Training paths** are referenced in ~60 app, view, factory and seeder files and ~50 tests (`grep -rln "training_path\|TrainingPath\|participant_path" app resources database tests`), many inside QA features that Phase 2 deletes.
- **Demo accounts**: `DevelopmentAccountProvisioner` reads `config/development_accounts.php` (one dev coach, one dev admin) and refuses outside `local` / `testing` (since 4.3: three participants, three mentors and one admin; `local`, `staging` and `testing`).
- **Audit log.** `admin_audit_logs` (`actor_id`, nullable `target_user_id`, `action`, JSON `metadata`). Written inline by `AdminUserController::audit()`, `AdminAiSettingsController`, `AdminGoogleDriveController`, `BootstrapAdminCommand`.
- **Files.** Private `local` disk; size and type limits in `config/training.php`; evidence/video serving in `SubmissionEvidenceController` and `WeeklyVideoRecordingController`; rules in `App\Services\EvidenceFilePolicy`.
- **Drive sync.** `SyncTrainingDataJob` and `TrainingWorkbookService` build one workbook per `week_number` across all activities with that number, so two sessions would mix.
- **Sidebar** links come from `App\Services\SidebarNavigationService`.
- **QA wording** appears in about 52 app and view files (many deleted by Phase 2).
- **Deploys** (`scripts/atomic-release.sh`, `docs/ATOMIC_DEPLOYMENT.md`) never run migrations: `prepare` extracts the release, links the shared `.env` and storage, installs Composer dependencies, then `health_check` runs `php artisan migrate:status` against the live database and dies on any `Pending` line ("Pending migrations block activation"). Migrations are applied by hand, expand/migrate/contract. Seeders are run by hand.
- **Migrations:** 89 files in `database/migrations` (Laravel's three `0001_01_01_*` files, 78 dated 2026-09-03 to 2026-10-06, and eight from this plan: 1.1's identity fields, 2.1's QA data deletion, 2.2's Validation Lab drop, 2.3's daily-log notification cleanup, 2.4's `daily_logs` drop, 2.5's Week 3 workspace drop, 2.6's Week 4 peer interview drop and 2.7's mentor interview booking drop). 15 of them are data migrations that rewrite QA activities, requirements and gate criteria by title (e.g. `2026_09_07_030000_align_week2_working_template_fields.php`, `2026_09_18_160000_align_current_activity_submission_definitions.php`); they match no rows on a fresh database. `2026_09_10_160000_add_week3_activity_fields.php` instantiates `QaTrainingProgramSeeder` (handled in 4.2). Two migrations exist only to fix SQLite foreign keys (`2026_09_14_121000_…`, `2026_09_16_160000_…`). None inserts rows on a fresh database.

### How the two specs fit together

- The DP spec's order of work is (1) removal and generalization, (2) the TS spec, (3) first session and demo enrollment. This plan keeps that order, with user identity (Phase 1) first because DMO-1 demo users need employee IDs, names and job roles, and 2.2 uses the role landing mapping from 1.5.
- Training-path removal (TS MIG-2, which the DP spec delegates to TS) is Phase 5. Replacing active-program lookups with the session being viewed (DP GEN-4, second sentence) happens in 9.5, 10.5 to 10.9 and 12.3.
- The DP spec's order of work does not place LOG-1 to LOG-4 or DEV-5. They are 7.1 to 7.3 (before the week-locking rules in Phase 11 that read their outcomes) and 9.11 (LOG-2, after the session page exists).
- **Every week needs a checklist.** WK-2 / WK-3 locking, the WK-4 "missing deliverables" list and early resource unlocking (Q3) all read the week's checklist review, so every program week must have at least one checklist item (one active gate with at least one required criterion). Neither spec states it; 4.1 (seeder guard) and 7.2 (check) enforce it. A week without a checklist can never be marked incomplete or completed, so it never locks later weeks and never unlocks the next week's resources early.

### Overlaps with `docs/CODE_QUALITY_PLAN.md`

- Its Phase 7 "Production values" check is still open and blocks deploying `main`; this plan's work ships behind it too. That check lists `mentor_interview_availabilities` and `week_four_mentor_interview_schedules` queries; those tables are dropped in 2.7. Note it in that plan when 2.7 lands; the check still applies to the remaining columns. (Noted in 2.7.)
- Many open decisions there concern removed features (Validation Checklist lock, Mentor Booking Calendar, Week 4 guide badges, daily-log report filters and week filter, unused `TrainingExportService::dailyLogs()`, weekly-video literals in `WeekTwoGateService`, training-path text with a " QA" suffix). Close them with "removed by developer-program-and-training-sessions n.n" when the batch lands; do not fix them first.
- "Weekly Video Recording treated as required whatever the Gate criteria" (batch 8t) is resolved by 2.10 for the Missing Submissions report and the notifications (video required only when the week's active Gate has a `weekly_video` item); the week page and dashboard video cards are left (see Open decisions). Noted in that plan.
- "Coach who is not assigned sees the Gate review read only" and "COMPLETED tile unlinked" are changed by 10.4, 10.7 and 10.9 (unassigned mentors get 403). Note the outcome in that plan when those batches land.
- "Review queue participant filter is a select of every participant" also applies to the add-participant control in 8.5; keep the select for now (see Out of scope).

## Design

### Gate readiness (2.10)

`GateReadinessService` replaces `WeekTwoGateService` with only generic rules, keyed by gate and criterion type: every active activity of the week has a handed-in attempt; its required evidence is present; a weekly video exists when the gate has a `weekly_video` criterion. Same public shape as today (`readiness()`, `readinessForGates()`, `readinessForParticipants()`, `weeklyVideo()`, `weeklyVideosForParticipants()`), so callers only change the type they inject. `weekly_loom` legacy handling goes (DAT-2 deletes the data it served).

### Weekly checklist (4.1, 7.2, 7.3)

- Each program week has exactly one active gate, the week's checklist. Its items are `gate_criteria` rows: one `deliverable` item per required deliverable, one `session_logs` item titled "Session logs committed for this week", and optionally a `weekly_video` item. Items belong to the **week** (the gate), not to an activity (Q2).
- Every active week of an active program must have at least one required checklist item. The L4 seeder throws if it would leave a week without one, and a test asserts it for the seeded program.
- Criterion type strings are listed once as constants on `GateCriterion` (e.g. `GateCriterion::TYPE_DELIVERABLE`, `TYPE_SESSION_LOGS`, `TYPE_WEEKLY_VIDEO`, `VIDEO_TYPES`), replacing the scattered literals.
- Outcomes keep their stored values: **Completed** = `GateEvaluationStatus::Passed`, **Incomplete** = `GateEvaluationStatus::NeedsRevision`. `label()` becomes "Completed" / "Incomplete"; `decisions()` becomes `[Passed, NeedsRevision]` (no new Failed reviews). The missing items of an incomplete review are its criterion evaluations with `NotMet`. 11.1 reads exactly these.

### Scoring dimensions (3.2)

Per activity in `activities.configuration['scoring_dimensions']`: an ordered list of `{key, label, max}`, keys `D1`..`D9` (fits `string(2)`), maxima summing to 100 so the existing bands (`ScoringService::band()`) still apply. `Activity::scoringDimensions()` returns them. AI prompts take the program name, week title, activity title, description and these dimensions; no QA text in code.

### GitHub links (7.1, 9.11)

- `App\Rules\GitHubUrl`: `https://github.com/` followed by at least an owner segment; used for submission fields with `configuration.type` `github_url` (draft save and submit).
- `App\Rules\GitHubRepositoryUrl`: exactly `https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>` (optional trailing slash; `.git` rejected); used for `training_session_participants.repository_url` (nullable string, 255).

### Seeders

- `L4TrainingProgramSeeder` (DEV-1, DEV-2, DEV-6): `updateOrCreate` keyed by program name, (`program_id`, `week_number`), (`training_week_id`, `position`), requirement `configuration.key`, (`training_gate_id`, `position`). Records that the seeder no longer defines are set `is_active = false`, never deleted (deleting would cascade into submissions). Sets L4 active.
- ~~`L4FirstSessionSeeder` (DEV-4): `firstOrCreate` the November 2026 session through `TrainingSessionService`.~~ **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge 2.1:** deleted; `L4TrainingProgramSeeder` dates the L4 weeks from Mon 9 Nov 2026.
- Demo users live in `config/development_accounts.php`, provisioned by `DevelopmentAccountProvisioner` (local, staging, testing; throws in production). Demo enrollment (DMO-2) is a second provisioner step.

"Today" is `today()` in the app timezone (`Asia/Manila`).

### Session rules

- ~~`ends_on = starts_on + weeks_count × 7 − 1 day` (4 weeks from Mon 2 Nov 2026 → Sun 29 Nov 2026; 4 weeks from Mon 9 Nov → Sun 6 Dec).~~ **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge 1.3:** an admin dates the weeks from one start date (week *N* runs start + (*N* − 1) × 7 days to 6 days later; 4 weeks from Mon 9 Nov → Sun 6 Dec) or edits one week's dates; the curriculum runs from its first active week's start to its last active week's end.
- ~~Status: *Upcoming* if `starts_on > today`, *Completed* if `ends_on < today`, else *Active*.~~ **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge 1.2:** *Unscheduled* if no active week has dates, *Upcoming* before the first start, *Completed* after the last end, else *Active*.
- ~~Week *N* is open from `weekOpensOn(N)`. It is locked for a participant when an earlier week of the same session has an *incomplete* review that has not since become *completed* (Phase 11).~~ **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge 2.1:** week *N* is open from its own `starts_on` and never while it has no dates. It is locked for a participant when an earlier week of the same curriculum has an *incomplete* review that has not since become *completed* (Phase 11, unchanged).

Who can open a Drive file is decided by its Drive sharing, not by the app.

Records carry no curriculum column, so every coach check resolves the curriculum from the record's activity → week or Gate → week.

Actions: `training_session_created`, `training_session_updated`, `session_participant_added`, `session_participant_removed`, `session_mentor_added`, `session_mentor_removed`, `session_mentor_assigned`, `session_resource_added`, `session_resource_renamed`, `session_resource_removed`.~~ **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge 2.1:** curriculum actions put `program_id` in `metadata`: `program_participant_added|removed`, `program_mentor_added|removed|assigned`, `program_resource_added|renamed|removed`, and `curriculum_scheduled` (1.3).

Use `x-ui.*` components and theme tokens; tabs via `x-ui.tab-list` / `x-ui.tab-panel` inside a `data-page-tabs` container; redirects after tab actions return to the tab's hash (e.g. `#resources`).

### Session resources (8.9)

- A resource is a title and an http(s) URL, for the whole session or one week. There are no uploads, stored files, size limits or file-type lists.
- `App\Support\GoogleDriveLink::fromUrl(string $url): ?self` (next to `EvidencePreview`) recognizes Google Drive files (`drive.google.com/file/d/{id}/…`, `drive.google.com/open?id={id}`) and Google Docs, Slides and Sheets (`docs.google.com/document|presentation|spreadsheets/d/{id}/…`, also with a `/u/{n}/` segment), on exactly those two hosts, and gives the embed URL (`https://drive.google.com/file/d/{id}/preview`, `https://docs.google.com/{document|presentation|spreadsheets}/d/{id}/preview`) and a label ("Google Drive", "Google Docs", "Google Slides", "Google Sheets"). Anything else (folders, Forms, YouTube, other hosts) is a plain link.
- Display (both Resources tabs): a list grouped "Whole session", "Week 1", …; each row shows the title, a badge (the Drive label or "Link"), the host, and an "Open in Drive" or "Open" link to the URL as entered (new tab). A Drive row also has a native `<details>` "Show preview" whose `<iframe>` carries the embed URL in `data-preview-src` and no `src`; the `initializeResourcePreviews` initializer in `resources/js/app.js` sets `src` the first time the row is expanded, so collapsed rows load nothing. Shared Blade component `x-session-resource` (`resources/views/components/session-resource.blade.php`), with a slot for the admin's Rename and Remove actions.
- Access: the viewer needs access to the Drive file itself (shared "Anyone with the link" or with the company domain). The app's Google scope (`drive.file`) cannot check this, so the add form reminds admins to share the file, and each preview carries a fixed line telling viewers what to do when it does not load.

### Weekly videos in Google Drive (8.10 to 8.17)

- **Flow.** The participant uploads the video to their own Drive, then, on `training.video-recording.edit`, presses "Choose Video from Google Drive". `initializeGoogleDrivePicker` (kind `video`) fetches a token from `profile.google-drive.picker-token` and opens a `DocsView(ViewId.DOCS_VIDEOS)` limited to files they own. The chosen file's ID and resource key go into the page's form, which posts to `training.video-recording.store`. `WeeklyVideoRecordingService::choose()` reads the file through `GoogleDriveService::videoFile()` (`files.get` with the participant's `google_drive_connections` token). It refuses a non-video, trashed or not-owned file and otherwise saves the row. Choosing the file in the Picker (with the project number as app ID) gives the app `drive.file` access to that one file. 8.16 then shares it.
- **Table** `weekly_video_recordings` (recreated in 8.12; the table and model names stay): `id`, `user_id` FK (cascade), `training_week_id` FK (cascade), `drive_file_id` string not null, `drive_resource_key` string nullable, `name` string, `mime_type` string(120), `size_bytes` unsigned big int nullable, `drive_created_at`, `drive_modified_at` timestamps nullable, `notes` text nullable, `chosen_at` timestamp nullable, timestamps; unique (`user_id`, `training_week_id`), which 9.1 swaps for the session key. 8.16 adds `drive_permission_id` and `shared_at`. `disk`, `path`, `original_filename`, `uploaded_at` and the sync copy's `drive_destination_folder_id` go.
- **Model.** `WeeklyVideoRecording::driveViewUrl()` (`https://drive.google.com/file/d/{id}/view`) and `drivePreviewUrl()` (`…/preview`), each with `?resourcekey={key}` when the row has one. Mentors and participants open the video in Drive or in a lazily loaded preview that uses 8.9's `data-resource-preview` markup and `initializeResourcePreviews`. The app has no video player and no stream or download route.
- **Routes.** `training.video-recording.edit|store` stay (participant only; 404 for a week without a video item, 403 for a week the participant cannot open, 422 once the week's gate is completed). `training.video-recording.view|download` go. 9.4 nests the two that stay under the session.
- **Drive calls** happen only when the participant chooses a file: one metadata read, plus the share in 8.16. Pages never read Drive (Q6). Later Drive checks are started by a mentor or admin and queued, and they read `drive_file_id` (Open decisions: AI scoring and submission checks).
- **Video evidence (8.17, Q7).** An activity's `video` evidence requirement takes a link and nothing else. The link is validated `url:http,https`, max 2048, so Drive, Loom and YouTube links all work, and saved as URL evidence as today. A posted file is refused. The upload rules (`EvidenceFilePolicy::videoRules()` and its helpers) and the `training.weekly_video_*` keys go. `file`, `screenshot` and `asset` evidence keep their uploads under the general rules.

## Assumptions

### QA removal, generalization and the L4 program

- **QA data scope.** DAT-2 deletes every row in the training tables (not only rows linked to the QA program), since the QA program is the only one with data; it deletes the QA program by name (*AI-Assisted QA Level 3*). Files on Google Drive are not deleted; only local storage files are. — Simplest correct reading of "all QA training data".
- **DAT-1 coverage.** The existing exports (CSV reports, attendance CSV, per-participant Drive sync) do not bundle uploaded files, so the release checklist adds a database dump and an archive of `storage/app/private` taken by whoever runs the deploy, next to the CSV downloads. — Confirm with the user; building a bulk export UI is out of scope.
- **QA participant accounts** = every user with role `participant`, including the seeded `test@example.com`. Admin and coach accounts stay, with their audit rows.
- **Google Calendar.** With mentor interview booking gone, `GoogleCalendarService` and the Google Calendar status and verification on the admin settings pages are removed too, if no other caller remains when 2.7 runs (public interview scheduling uses external booking links). — Confirm; it is part of the mentor booking feature. Applied in 2.7: no other caller remained.
- **The QA practice app card** on the participant dashboard (`services.qa_application.url`, membership features list) belongs to the Validation Lab and is removed in 2.2.
- **Ordered Week 3 activities** (`ensurePriorWeekThreeActivities`) were part of the Week 3 workspace and go with it; no generic "complete activities in order" setting replaces it. — GEN-1 does not ask for one.
- **Readiness.** Until 7.3, gate readiness still blocks a *Passed* review as today; from 7.3, only the checklist decides (LOG-4), and readiness is shown to the mentor as information.
- **Weekly video in the checklist.** DEV-3 keeps weekly videos, but LOG-3 lists only deliverables and session logs. The placeholder checklists include a required `weekly_video` item so the flow stays "same as today"; the real curriculum can drop it per week. **Confirmed 2026-10-23:** whether a week asks for the weekly video is decided only by that week's checklist (a `weekly_video` item on its gate), never by a hardcoded week; sessions reuse the program's weeks, so a session week follows its program week's checklist (3.4).
- **Incomplete needs a missing item.** Marking a week *incomplete* with every required item ticked fails validation ("Leave at least one item unticked to mark the week incomplete."). — Otherwise WK-4 would show an empty missing list.
- **Deliverables are submission fields** of type `github_url` (one per placeholder activity), not evidence links.
- **Placeholder content:** 2 activities per week, each with a description ("Placeholder — objective and instructions to come"), a required `github_url` deliverable field, an optional notes field, no required evidence, and dimensions D1 to D4 (40/30/20/10). Program description is a placeholder sentence.
- **"Coach" becomes "Mentor" in user-facing text** (REM-5 "generic text says … mentor"): sidebar, headings, emails, exports, role label. Route names, URLs (`/coach`), the `UserRole::Coach` case and its stored value `coach` stay. — Isolated in 6.3 so it can be dropped if the user disagrees.
- **Root-level QA artefacts** (`QA_LAB_MASTER_PLAN.md`, `QA_Training_Working_Template.xlsx`, `training-plans/*.pptx`, `training-apps/membership-app`) are not app code and are left alone; listed under follow-ups.
- **Seeding in production.** The L4 program and first session are seeded by running their seeders during the release (`php artisan db:seed --class=… --force`), recorded in the release checklist; demo users never are.

### Users and sessions

- **First and last name** are new columns; `name` stays as their combination, kept in sync on save. Existing users are backfilled by splitting `name` at the first space (one-word names → last name empty). — REG-1 asks for both; keeping `name` avoids touching every screen and sort.
- **Employee ID** is free text, trimmed, max 50 characters, unique, no format rule. Users cannot change it themselves once set; admins can. — The spec gives no format; tighten later if the company format is known.
- **Admin-created users** must have an employee ID; on admin edit it stays optional (so legacy users can still be edited) but unique. — REG-2 "every user has one"; MIG-3 covers legacy accounts.
- **The employee ID prompt (MIG-3) applies to every role**, including admins and coaches. — REG-2 says every user.
- **Job role** is required at registration for participants, optional and admin-editable for coaches and admins, and read-only on the profile. — REG-1 says it is informational.
- **End date** is inclusive: start + weeks × 7 − 1 day. — Matches the spec's example; the literal "start plus weeks" would end on a Monday.
- **No weekday rule** on the start date, and **past dates are allowed** when creating or editing. — The spec sets none; admins may record past runs.
- **Weeks** count the program's active weeks; session week *N* is the program week with `week_number` N. Lowering a session's weeks hides later weeks but keeps their data. — GEN-2 numbers weeks from 1.
- **Programs offered** in the create form are active programs only.
- **Mentors are coaches only**; admins act on every session without being mentors. — TS spec Terms.
- **Removing a mentor** from a session ends that mentor's current assignments there; those participants become unassigned. **Removing a participant** ends their current assignment.
- **Admins can add participants to any session**, including completed ones; only self-joining is limited to non-completed sessions. — PAR-2 restricts participants, not admins.
- **Attendance stays global** (one check-in per user, not per session), and the attendance card stays on the participant landing page with its heading renamed from "TRAINING SESSION" to "ATTENDANCE" to avoid a clash with the new term. (Attendance check-ins are `AttendanceSession`, not the model curriculum-sessions-merge superseded; whether attendance copy should stop saying "training session" is an open decision there.)
- **Review outcomes.** *Completed* = `GateEvaluationStatus::Passed`, *incomplete* = `NeedsRevision` (and, before 7.3, `Failed`); an admin marks a week completed through the same gate review form (WK-3). 7.3 keeps these stored values, relabels them "Completed" / "Incomplete" and stops offering `Failed`; 11.1 reads them.
- **"Marks an activity as incomplete" (PAR-5)** is the existing review decision *Needs revision* (`SubmissionStatus::NeedsRevision`). In a completed session only attempts in that status can be revised and resubmitted.
- **Activities tab** lists the activities of the session's weeks with per-activity counts for the session's participants (not started, awaiting review, needs revision, approved). Its tab count is the number of activities. — The spec names the tab but not its content.
- **Overview tab** shows the training and review counts the admin dashboard had before 12.3, scoped to the session (participants, mentors, participants with work per week, awaiting review, needs revision, pending gates). Week 4 interview and daily-log counts are gone after Phase 2. Since 12.3 the admin dashboard keeps only the global review counts and no per-week training counts.
- **Mentors see their sessions** as a "My sessions" card on the coach dashboard plus a session filter on coach pages; there is no separate mentor session page. — SA-2 asks only that they see them.
- **Exports** add an "Employee ID" column next to the name; session-scoped exports (Phase 10) also add a "Training session" column. **Superseded by curriculum-sessions-merge 2.1:** the column is "Curriculum".
- **No session deletion.** Not in the spec; sessions are only created and edited.
- **Repository URL (LOG-2)** is asked on the session page after a one-click Join (Q1); a member without one sees a prompt there, but it does not block submissions. Mentors see "No repository yet". — The spec does not say it is required before submitting.

### Session resources (decided 2026-10-23)

- **Resources are links only** (user decision 2026-10-23, replacing 8.6's uploads; scheduled as 8.9). Admins add a title and a URL; Google Drive, Docs, Slides and Sheets links preview in the app from Drive, other http(s) links (e.g. YouTube) get an "Open" link only. Each resource expands to its preview; the iframe loads only when expanded. The TS spec (SES-4, ADM-5, the "Resource" term, the resources assumption, decision 10) and the DP spec's REM-4 now describe links (checked in 8.9).
- **A new migration, not an edit of 8.6's.** The app has not been deployed, but the rules say old migrations are only edited to stop them referencing a deleted class, and developers' databases may already have run 8.6's. 13.1 later folds both into one file per table.
- **Existing file resources are deleted** by 8.9's migration, with their stored files. Only development databases can hold any (no production database exists), and a file cannot become a link.
- **The resource route goes.** With no files to stream, `training.sessions.resources.show` and `TrainingSessionResourceController` only redirected links; rows now link straight to the URL, and the session page's `view` is the only access check. Week resources that have not opened (11.6) are listed without their URL, so nothing leaks. — Simpler than keeping a redirect that reveals the same URL.
- **The Drive kind is derived from the URL**, so the `type` column and `TrainingSessionResourceType` go. — Nothing else needs a stored type, and a derived value cannot drift from the URL.
- **`uploaded_by` becomes `added_by`** in the same migration, matching `training_session_participants.added_by`; nothing is uploaded any more.
- **Recognized Drive URLs** are those in the Design; Drive folder links and Google Forms are plain links (their embeds differ and the spec names only files, Docs, Slides and Sheets). "Open in Drive" uses the URL exactly as the admin entered it. An http Drive link still previews (the embed URL is always https).
- **No `sandbox` on the preview iframe.** Drive's viewer needs scripts, its own cookies and pop-ups ("Open in new window"); the iframe gets `title`, `allow="autoplay; fullscreen"` and `allowfullscreen`.
- **No Content-Security-Policy today.** The app sends none (no `Content-Security-Policy` or `X-Frame-Options` in `app`, `bootstrap`, `config`, `routes` or `public/.htaccess`), so Drive iframes load. If one is added later, it must allow `frame-src https://drive.google.com https://docs.google.com`.
- **Without JavaScript** the preview stays empty and the "Open in Drive" link still works. The lazy loading is checked by hand in a browser (no browser tests in this project); feature tests assert the markup contract (`data-preview-src`, no `src`).
- **Editing a resource's link** is not offered; the spec asks for add, rename and remove. An admin removes and re-adds a resource to change its URL.

### Weekly videos in Google Drive (decided 2026-10-23)

The user's decision is recorded under Open decisions ("Weekly videos live in Google Drive …"). These follow from it and the codebase:

- **The batches sit at the end of Phase 8, before 9.1.** This way 9.1 adds `training_session_id` to the Drive-backed table and 9.4 nests the choose route, instead of both reworking upload code that 8.12 deletes. The first L4 session's week 1 (9 to 15 Nov 2026) asks for a video.
- **Existing rows and files are deleted** by 8.12's migration, which drops the table and creates it again. No production database exists, so only development databases hold any rows, and an uploaded file cannot become a Drive file. No table references `weekly_video_recordings`, so dropping it is safe on MySQL and SQLite; 13.1 later folds it into one file per table.
- **2.1's migration gets a column guard.** `2026_10_23_060944_delete_qa_training_data.php`'s `localFiles()` reads `path` from `weekly_video_recordings`, and `DeleteQaTrainingDataMigrationTest` runs it on the current schema. 8.12 makes it skip that table when the column is missing. This is a second allowed reason to edit an old migration: its behavior on the schema it ran against is unchanged.
- **Names stay.** The table, the `WeeklyVideoRecording` model, `User::weeklyVideoRecordings()` and the route names `training.video-recording.edit|store` keep their names, so readers do not need renaming. The record is still the participant's weekly video recording.
- **Own Drive only.** The Picker view uses `setOwnedByMe(true)`, and the server refuses a file whose `ownedByMe` is false. That covers shared drives and files shared with the participant, which they could not share on (8.16).
- **A Drive connection is required.** The video page asks the participant to connect first, and 8.13 brings them back to the page. The connect route stays participant-only. The Picker token comes from the existing `profile.google-drive.picker-token`.
- **Company accounts.** With the OAuth consent screen *Internal* (DP spec Assumptions), only company Google accounts can connect. The release checklist (8.15) records the Google Cloud setup.
- **Notes stay.** The optional notes are posted with the choice, as they were with the upload.
- **The lock stays.** Once the week's gate is completed, the choice cannot change (the existing 422).

### Migration squash (13.1)

- **Scope.** It replaces every migration file present when it runs: the 81 existing ones plus every migration added by batches merged before it. The databases that must have run them are production, staging and any shared database; developers' local databases are rebuilt with `migrate:fresh --seed` or adopt the new names with the same command.
- **Adopting the new migrations** is done by a one-time artisan command shipped in the squash release (not hand-run SQL), so it can check the database before changing it and is testable on SQLite. It only rewrites rows in the `migrations` table; it never runs migration code.
- **Framework tables** (`password_reset_tokens`, `sessions`, `cache`, `cache_locks`, `jobs`, `job_batches`, `failed_jobs`) also get one file each, written by hand like the rest.
- **Rolling back across the squash.** After adoption, the previous release's `migrate:status` shows its old files as pending. Activating it again with `atomic-release.sh rollback` still works (rollback runs no migration check), but nobody may run `php artisan migrate` from a pre-squash release afterwards.

### Answered questions (2026-10-23)

- **Q1 — Join stays one click.** PAR-2 and REG-1 work as written: Join and registration add the membership immediately, with no extra form. The GitHub repository URL (LOG-2) is asked later, on the session page (9.11). Affects 9.7, 9.8, 9.11.
- **Q2 — Checklist items belong to their week, not to an activity.** "Missing deliverables" are the week's required checklist items marked not met in the *incomplete* review, listed by title and linking to that week (its tab and activities). "Submitted after an incomplete review" means **at least one** activity version in that week, in that session, submitted after the review's `evaluated_at`. A new incomplete review resets the signal. **Trade-off:** resubmitting *any* activity in the week counts, even one unrelated to the missing item, so "Waiting for your mentor" and the mentor's waiting list can appear before the real gap is fixed; the mentor still verifies by hand before marking the week completed. This relies on every session week having at least one activity (the user confirmed it does) and at least one checklist item (4.1, 7.2). Affects 11.3, 11.5.
- **Q3 — Week resources unlock by date or by the previous week's checklist.** In a session that is not completed (active or upcoming), a participant sees a week's resources once that week has opened by date (WK-1) **or** once their review of the previous week is *completed*; the second case can unlock them before the week's start date. Week 1 follows its open date only. Session-wide resources (no week) are always visible. In a completed session every resource is visible (PAR-5). Mentors and admins always see every resource. Affects 9.6, 11.6.
  - **Interim:** 9.6 ships "show every resource to members" and 11.6 adds the week rule on top. This is the simpler order: 9.6 needs no week-state logic, and 11.6 only tightens one policy method and the tab rendering once 11.1 provides the week states. The deploy block means participants should not see the gap. If the app does reach participants between 9.6 and 11.6, week resources are visible early.
  - **Assumption from this answer:** the resource rule is date-or-checklist only, so a week whose date has passed keeps its resources visible even while an *incomplete* review of an earlier week locks its activities (WK-2). Locking hides the week's activities and deliverables, not its resources. Confirm with the user if locked weeks should also hide resources.
  - **Assumption from this answer:** unlocking resources early does not unlock the week's activities early; activities still follow WK-1 dates.
- **Q5 — The chosen weekly video is shared with the company domain.** It is shared view-only with the company's Google Workspace domain (`type=domain`, `role=reader`, `allowFileDiscovery=false`), once, when the participant chooses it. Mentor changes (10.3) need no new sharing; admins can open it too. A previous mentor or any employee who has the link can still open the file, but the app shows the link only to the participant, their current mentor (from 10.4) and admins. Per-mentor email grants were rejected: they would be built twice (before and after Phase 10), need a re-grant job that fails once the participant disconnects Drive, and leave admins out. Affects 8.16 (now unblocked) and VID-3.
- **Q7 — Video evidence becomes link-only.** Activity evidence of type `video` no longer uploads a file; it takes a link. The video upload rules, `training.weekly_video_max_kb` and the other video-only evidence config go entirely (8.17). **Default chosen from the codebase:** the link must be an `http` / `https` URL (`url:http,https`, max 2048). It is not limited to Drive (`App\Support\GoogleDriveLink`), because recordings for activities may live on Loom or YouTube, and the app cannot check a link's sharing either way. URL evidence already shows the link to the mentor (`SubmissionAnswerPresenter`). Existing video files stored as evidence (development databases only) stay as they are; no migration. Affects 8.15 and 8.17.

## Open questions

- **Q4 (blocking 13.2):** The training team has not supplied the developer curriculum (week themes, activities with objective and deliverable, mentor review, checklist items, scoring dimensions, program description, resources). 4.1 ships placeholders; 13.2 swaps in the real content. Needed: the filled "Developer curriculum" table from the DP spec. No recommendation; it is an input.
- **Q6 (not blocking; 8.12 and 8.14 apply the recommendation): What do pages show when the participant disconnects Drive, revokes the app's access, or deletes or trashes the file? Does readiness treat the video as missing?**
  - **Recommendation:** the app never reads Drive on a page view (VID-5), so the choice stays recorded and readiness, Missing Submissions and notifications keep counting it.
  - On the participant's video page, a participant who is no longer connected sees the chosen video with "Connect Google Drive to choose a different video." and no Picker.
  - Every video link, for mentors and participants, carries a fixed line: "Video not opening? It may have been deleted, moved to the trash or unshared in the participant's Google Drive. Ask them to choose it again." The participant's version says "in your Google Drive. Choose it again."
  - The mentor opens the video before ticking the checklist item (LOG-3), and the future manual Drive check reports files it cannot read.
  - Sharing is a property of the file, so a disconnect does not remove the mentor's access.
  - **Alternative:** re-read the file's metadata (one Drive call) each time the participant opens their own video page, and show "This video is no longer in your Google Drive" there.

## Phase 2 — Remove QA data and tools

- [x] **2.1 Delete the QA training data and its files** (DAT-2, DAT-3, DAT-4, REM-1 data, MIG-1) (was DP 1.1) — Done. `database/migrations/2026_10_23_060944_delete_qa_training_data.php` empties 29 training tables (the list above plus `mentor_interview_availabilities`, the coach-owned Week 4 booking slots, so 2.7 drops an empty table), each guarded by `Schema::hasTable` so the migration and its test survive the Phase 2 table drops. It uses stored strings (`'participant'`, the program name), not `UserRole` or models, so later class removals cannot break it. Before deleting participants it removes their `sessions` (no foreign key), `password_reset_tokens` (by email), `password_setup_tokens`, `google_drive_connections` and coach assignments; admin and coach Drive connections stay. Audit rows that target a participant are kept with `target_user_id` set to null by its `nullOnDelete` key (the same applies to `password_setup_tokens.created_by` and `interview_schedulers.user_id` / `approved_by`); `interview_schedulers` (public interview booking) is not training data and stays. The program's weeks, activities, requirements and gates go by cascade; the restricting keys (`activity_scores.scorer_id`, Week 4 `mentor_id` / `assigned_by_id` / `authorized_by_id`, `week_four_mentor_interview_schedules.activity_id`) are cleared first. Deletes run in one transaction; files are deleted after it from each row's disk (default `local`). The restricting keys were checked against the dev MySQL schema (`information_schema`) and match SQLite; the migration itself was only run on SQLite (see Open decisions).
  - **Do:** One migration that, in FK-safe order with the query builder (no models): collects the local storage paths of `submission_evidence`, `weekly_video_recordings` and Validation Lab records; deletes rows from Drive export/sync tables, notifications, recovery requests, scores (and dimensions, history, suggestions), reviews, interactions, evidence, versions, submissions, gate criterion evaluations, gate evaluations, daily logs, attendance, weekly videos, Validation Lab records, Week 3 and Week 4 tables, `coach_participant_assignments` of deleted participants; deletes training sessions of the QA program if `training_sessions` exists (defensive; in this order the table is created later, in 8.1); deletes the QA program (weeks, activities, requirements, gates cascade); deletes every `participant` user; then deletes the collected files. `down()` is a no-op with a comment. Works on MySQL and SQLite.
  - **Acceptance:** On a database seeded with the QA seeder plus participants, submissions with files, videos, logs, gate evaluations, notifications and Drive records, running the migration leaves: no participant users, no QA program, empty training tables, no referenced files on the fake disk, and admin/coach users and their audit rows intact. Running it on an empty database does nothing.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/DeleteQaTrainingDataMigrationTest.php` (`Storage::fake('local')`, require the migration file and call `up()`): full cleanup, accounts kept, files deleted, idempotent on empty data.

- [x] **2.10 Replace the Week 2 gate rules with generic gate readiness** (REM-3, GEN-1) (was DP 1.10) — Done. `GateReadinessService` keeps the public shape except what only served the legacy Loom: `weeklyVideo()` results lost their always-null `activity` key, and the unused `weeklyLoom()` alias and `canPass()` went. The rules are unchanged otherwise (latest attempt handed in, required non-`gate_evidence` evidence on the latest handed-in version, video only with a video criterion); readiness no longer loads the weeks (one query fewer) and the video URL uses the Gate's week id. Removed with `weekly_loom`: a `weekly_loom` criterion no longer counts as a video criterion, and a legacy Loom link (the `gate_evidence` URL on the week's activity at position 3, the only week- or position-specific rule) no longer satisfies the video. `GateCriterion` gained `TYPE_DELIVERABLE`, `TYPE_SESSION_LOGS`, `TYPE_WEEKLY_VIDEO`, `VIDEO_TYPES`, the `weeklyVideo()` scope and `isWeeklyVideo()`; they replace the literals in `GateReviewController`, `CoachGateReviewPageService` and the QA seeder (which also stopped matching `weekly_loom`). The Gate board row key `loom` is now `weekly_video`. Behavior change (the 8t open decision in `docs/CODE_QUALITY_PLAN.md`): the Missing Submissions report (page and CSV) lists a Weekly Video Recording row, and `ParticipantNotificationService` raises the video reminder, only for a week with an active Gate that has a `weekly_video` criterion (before: every accessible Week 2–4, with or without a Gate). Program "L4 Accelerator Training" (placeholder description, `is_active` true, keyed by name); weeks 1 to 4 ("Placeholder topic", `position` = `week_number`); per week "Placeholder Activity N.1" and "N.2" (description, objective and instructions "Placeholder — objective and instructions to come", `requires_coach_review`, `configuration.scoring_dimensions` D1–D4 Deliverable 40 / Code quality 30 / Testing 20 / Reflection 10) with two `submission_field` requirements keyed by `configuration.key`: required `deliverable_url` (type `url`, label "Deliverable link") and optional `notes` (textarea); no evidence requirements. One active Gate per week, "Week N checklist", with four required items by position: a `deliverable` item per activity ("Placeholder Activity N.M deliverable submitted"), `session_logs` ("Session logs committed for this week") and `weekly_video` ("Weekly video recording uploaded"), typed with the existing `GateCriterion::TYPE_*` constants. ## Phase 5 — Remove training paths

- [x] **5.1 Apply every activity requirement to every participant** (REG-4, MIG-2) (was TS 3.1) — Done. No code reads `participant_path` any more; only the `ActivityRequirement` fillable/cast and the `TrainingPath` docblock name it (5.4 drops them). `SubmissionWorkflowService::fieldDefinitions()` and `evidenceRequirements()` lost the path filter and their now-unused `User` parameter (as did the private `validateSubmissionRequirements()` / `firstUnmetEvidenceRequirement()`), so participants and coaches get the same fields and evidence; `ActivitySubmissionPageService` calls them without the participant. `TrainingActivityPageService::requirements()` shows every requirement (participants still do not see deprecated ones, as before). `SubmissionEvidenceService::requirementFor()` accepts any evidence requirement of the activity (no participant parameter). `TrainingWorkbookService` puts every submission field in the workbook. `MissingSubmissionReportService` lost `activityApplies()`, so every active activity is expected from every participant, and no longer eager-loads `requirements`. `ActivityRequirementFactory` and `L4TrainingProgramSeeder` stop writing `participant_path`. `SubmissionAnswerPresenter` never filtered by path. `User::eligibleTrainingPaths()` now has no caller in `app` (only `HomeNavigationTest`); it stays for 5.4. - [x] **5.4 Drop the path columns and the TrainingPath enum** (MIG-2) (was TS 3.4) — Done. `database/migrations/2026_10_23_121319_drop_training_path_columns.php` adds `users_role_index` (`role`), drops `users_role_training_path_index` and then `users.training_path`; on `activity_requirements` it adds `activity_req_activity_pos_idx` (`activity_id`, `position`) before dropping `activity_req_activity_path_pos_idx` (on the dev MySQL schema that index is the only one starting with `activity_id`, so it backs the `activity_id` foreign key and MySQL would refuse to drop it first), then drops `participant_path`. Indexes go before columns because SQLite refuses to drop an indexed column; SQLite 3.35+ drops columns in place, so no table is rebuilt and the foreign keys that point at both tables stay put. `down()` restores both columns (nullable, empty) and the original indexes, creating the old activity index before dropping the new one. Verified on a throwaway SQLite file: `migrate:fresh`, `migrate:rollback --step=1` (columns and original indexes back), `migrate` again; `PRAGMA foreign_key_check` clean. On MySQL only `migrate --pretend --path=…` (the six `alter table` statements) and the dev schema's indexes were checked; it was not run there (see Open decisions). Deleted `App\Enums\TrainingPath`, `User::trainingPathLabel()` and `eligibleTrainingPaths()`, the `training_path` fillable/cast on `User`, the `participant_path` fillable/cast on `ActivityRequirement` and the `UserFactory` field. Old migrations that write `participant_path => null` through `ActivityRequirement::create()` still run (the key is silently dropped; the column exists at that point). The grep for `training_path|participant_path|TrainingPath` outside `docs/` and the old migrations now finds only the new migration, its schema test (`DropTrainingPathColumnsMigrationTest`, which must name the columns), `CLAUDE.md` and the root `google-sheets-integration-audit.md` (see Open decisions). Tests: new `DropTrainingPathColumnsMigrationTest` (columns gone; `role` and (`activity_id`, `position`) indexed); deleted the cases that had nothing left to check: `AuthenticationAuthorizationTest`'s "a participant without a training path can save their profile" and "registration and profile updates ignore a posted training path", `HomeNavigationTest`'s `eligibleTrainingPaths()` case, `SubmissionVersionPageTest`'s and `CoachParticipantPageTest`'s "does not show a training path"; reworked `ParticipantDashboardControllerTest` (header case keeps the name and start week), `ParticipantFieldGuidanceTest` (fields and evidence include every requirement), `MissingSubmissionReportPageTest` (path data set gone), `AdminUserFormPageTest` (data set lost the path parameter and the former-path row), `AttendanceTest` (check-in case uses one participant instead of a manual/automation loop), `TrainingDomainTest` (cast assertion gone), `ParticipantWorkflowTest` (two "formerly tied to a path" cases renamed), `AuthPagesTest` / `ProfilePageTest` (no `name="training_path"` assertion; "Training path" text check kept), `AdminUserManagementTest` (renamed), `DevelopmentAccountProvisioningTest` (path assertion gone); fixtures in the other test files stop writing a path. The "an old path filter is ignored" cases keep posting `?path=`. `docs/ATOMIC_DEPLOYMENT.md` names the migration.
  - **Do:** Migration dropping `users.training_path` (and its composite index with `role`; keep an index on `role`) and `activity_requirements.participant_path` (rebuild `activity_req_activity_path_pos_idx` as (`activity_id`, `position`)). Delete `App\Enums\TrainingPath`, `User::trainingPathLabel()`, `eligibleTrainingPaths()`, the `fillable`/cast entries and factory fields. `grep` must find no `training_path`, `participant_path` or `TrainingPath` outside old migrations.
  - **Acceptance:** Migrations run up and down on SQLite and MySQL; the suite passes with no path references.

  - **Depends on:** 5.3.

## Phase 7 — GitHub deliverables and the weekly checklist

- [x] **7.1 Accept only GitHub links as deliverables** (DEV-5) (was DP 4.1) — Done. New `App\Rules\GitHubUrl` (`https://github.com/` plus an owner segment of letters, digits and hyphens not starting with a hyphen, then an optional path, query or fragment without whitespace, quotes or angle brackets; the scheme and host match case-insensitively, `www.github.com` and look-alike hosts fail) with `GitHubUrl::MESSAGE` and a static `accepts()` shared by the rule, the submit check and the presenter. `StoreSubmissionDraftRequest` adds the rule to `github_url` fields (blank still saves; `FieldLimits::forType('github_url')` is the URL limit, 2,048); `SubmissionWorkflowService::validateSubmissionRequirements()` fails a filled `github_url` value the rule rejects with the same message on `field_values.{key}`, after the required check. The form renders the field as `type="url"` with `placeholder="https://github.com/"` (`fieldControl()` maps `github_url` to `url`; other inputs get no placeholder attribute). `SubmissionAnswerPresenter::fieldLinks()` returns key → URL for `github_url` fields whose value the rule accepts (a stored non-GitHub value, e.g. from before the switch, stays plain text, so no unsafe `href`); `CoachSubmissionReviewPageService::submittedFields()` and `ScoringPageService::answerRows()` add `link`, and `coach/review` and `scoring/edit` print it as a `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"` link. `fields()` is unchanged, so the AI scoring payload is the same. `L4TrainingProgramSeeder`'s `deliverable_url` fields are `github_url` (help: "Paste the GitHub link to your deliverable for this activity."). Tests: new `tests/Unit/GitHubUrlRuleTest.php` (7 accepted, 15 rejected values); `SubmissionFormPageTest` (input and placeholder, a plain `url` field without one, draft save, the three rejected values on draft save, a plain `url` field still saving a GitLab link, submit failing on a stored non-GitHub value then passing); `CoachSubmissionReviewPageTest` (link in a new tab, a plain `url` field and a non-GitHub `github_url` value not linked); `ScoringEditPageTest` (link in a new tab); `L4TrainingProgramSeederTest` expects `github_url`.
  - **Do:** `App\Rules\GitHubUrl`; submission field type `github_url`: `StoreSubmissionDraftRequest` applies the rule when filled, `SubmissionWorkflowService` applies it on submit; the field renders as `type="url"` with a `https://github.com/` placeholder; `SubmissionAnswerPresenter` and the review/scoring views render the value as a link opening in a new tab. Switch the L4 seeder's deliverable fields to `github_url`.

- [x] **7.3 Mark weeks completed or incomplete from the checklist** (LOG-4) (was DP 4.3) — Done. `GateEvaluationStatus::label()` is "Completed" / "Incomplete" for `Passed` / `NeedsRevision` (stored values unchanged; `Failed` keeps its case and "Failed" label for older reviews) and `decisions()` is `[Passed, NeedsRevision]`, so `StoreGateReviewRequest`'s `Rule::enum(...)->only(decisions())` now rejects `failed` and the form offers only Completed / Incomplete (the review page's "Needs Revision" special case is gone; options use `label()`). The checklist checks moved from the controller into `StoreGateReviewRequest::after()` (runs only when the fields are valid): the weekly video item ticked before a video is uploaded fails on `criteria.{id}` with the existing message, now for **both** decisions (before 7.3 readiness covered it for Passed; without that, a mentor could complete a week with an unuploaded video ticked); *Completed* with any required item unticked or left out fails on `criteria` with "Tick every checklist item to mark the week completed."; *Incomplete* with every required item ticked fails on `criteria` with "Leave at least one item unticked to mark the week incomplete." (the Assumptions text). Readiness no longer blocks (the old `gate` error "The Week N Gate cannot pass until …" is gone); `coach/gate_review` says under "Gate readiness" "For information only. The weekly checklist decides whether the week is completed." and explains both decisions in a hint linked to the select with `aria-describedby`. New `GateReviewService::record()` saves the evaluation and every required item's status (unticked as `NotMet`) in one transaction, then queues the summary reports and the Drive sync; `GateReviewController::update` only guards, authorizes and calls it with `StoreGateReviewRequest::criterionStatuses()` (required items keyed by id, loaded once per request). Labels: the Gate board stat "COMPLETED" and filter options "Completed" / "Incomplete" ("Failed" kept as a filter for older reviews); badges on the board, coach participant page, participant Gate, week and dashboard pages follow `label()`; the participant dashboard says "Completed" on a completed week, "Program complete — every week completed.", "Your mentor must mark the previous week completed before you can continue." (locked reason) and "A Mentor must review the weekly checklist and mark the week completed …"; the week tab's screen-reader state is "(Completed)"; the readiness video reason and the participant Gate page's video message say "before this week can be marked completed". Notifications: `GatePassed` is "Week N completed" / "Your mentor marked Week N completed." and `GateIncomplete` "Week N requirements incomplete" / "You still have requirements to complete before your mentor can mark Week N completed." (triggers unchanged). Tests: `CoachGateReviewPageTest` (Completed / Incomplete options, no `failed` option, readiness and decision hints; the rejected-decision case now completes with one item unticked; completing with an unticked or left-out item fails; every item ticked completes while readiness has blockers; the video ticked before upload fails on completion; incomplete with every item ticked fails; an admin completes a week; `failed` added to the non-decision statuses), `CoachGateBoardPageTest` (stat, badges and the filter option texts), `ParticipantGatePageTest`, `TrainingWeekPageTest`, `CoachParticipantPageTest`, `ParticipantDashboardControllerTest`, `HomeNavigationTest`, `CoachOperationsTest`, `GateReadinessTest` (label and copy assertions), `ParticipantNotificationsTest` (new: the requirements reminder text, then the completed event text once the week is completed), `ParticipantNotificationsPageTest` (fixture texts). `ParticipantNotificationTriggersTest` needed no change: its Gate case (incomplete with the only item unticked) still passes through the new service and queues one sync.
  - **Do:** `GateEvaluationStatus`: labels "Completed" / "Incomplete" for `Passed` / `NeedsRevision`, `decisions()` = `[Passed, NeedsRevision]`. `GateReviewController::update` / `StoreGateReviewRequest`: *Completed* requires every required item ticked (readiness no longer blocks; it is shown as information); *Incomplete* requires at least one required item unticked and saves the unticked items as `NotMet`. Admins can do both. Update labels on the gate board, participant pages and notifications (`GatePassed` / `GateIncomplete` texts).
  - **Acceptance:** Completing with one item unticked fails with "Tick every checklist item to mark the week completed."; incomplete with all ticked fails (Assumptions); an incomplete review stores the unticked items as not met; an admin can mark a week completed; `Failed` is no longer offered.
  - **Tests:** Update `CoachGateReviewPageTest`, `CoachGateBoardPageTest`, `ParticipantGatePageTest`, `ParticipantNotificationTriggersTest`; add the validation cases.
  - **Depends on:** 7.2. **Needed by:** 11.1, 11.3, 11.5, 11.6.

## Phase 8 — Training sessions: admin setup and the first session; weekly videos from Google Drive

> **History.** This phase built the training-session model that `docs/plans/curriculum-sessions-merge.md` superseded (2026-10-28). Its batch notes describe the code as each batch left it; for the current design see that plan and Design above.

- [x] **8.1 Add the TrainingSession model and session participants** (SES-1, SES-3, SA-1; prerequisite for the rest) (was TS 2.1)
  - **Do:** Migrations for `training_sessions` and `training_session_participants` (see Design). Models `TrainingSession` (relations, `status()`, `isCompleted()`, scopes, `weeks()`, `includesWeek()`, `weekOpensOn()`) and `TrainingSessionParticipant` pivot if useful; `User::trainingSessions()`; `Program::trainingSessions()`. Enum `TrainingSessionStatus`. `TrainingSessionFactory` with `upcoming()`, `active()`, `completed()` states (dates relative to `today()`).
  - **Acceptance:** Status is Upcoming/Active/Completed exactly at the boundaries (start today → Active; end today → Active; end yesterday → Completed). `weeks()` returns program weeks 1..N only. `weekOpensOn(2)` for a 9 Nov start is 16 Nov.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/TrainingSessionModelTest.php` (uses `travelTo`): status boundaries, `weeks()`, `includesWeek()` for a week of another program, `weekOpensOn()`.
  - **Depends on:** Gate A (4.4).

- [x] **8.2 Add the admin session list and create form** (SES-1, ADM-1 "View all", SA-2, SA-4) (was TS 2.2)
  - **Do:** `TrainingSessionPolicy` (admin abilities; see Design). `AdminTrainingSessionController@index|create|store`, `StoreTrainingSessionRequest` (`program_id` exists and active; `title` required max 255; `weeks_count` integer 1..number of the program's active weeks with message "The program has only N weeks."; `starts_on` date; `ends_on` date equal to `starts_on + weeks × 7 − 1` with message "The end date must be N weeks after the start date (DD Mon YYYY)."). `TrainingSessionService::create()`. `AdminAuditService` (new) records `training_session_created`. `AdminTrainingSessionPageService` index rows: title, program, date range ("9 Nov – 6 Dec 2026"), status badge, participant count (`withCount`), paginated (20), newest start first. Views `admin/sessions/index` and `admin/sessions/form`. JS initializer `initializeSessionEndDate` in `resources/js/app.js` fills `ends_on` from `starts_on` and `weeks_count` (server stays the source of truth). Sidebar link "Training Sessions" for admins in `SidebarNavigationService`.
  - **Acceptance:** An admin creates "L4 Accelerator Training — November 2026", 4 weeks, 9 Nov to 6 Dec 2026, and sees it in the list. End 7 Dec → validation error, nothing saved. 5 weeks for a 4-week program → error. Two overlapping sessions of the same program both save. Participants and coaches get 403 on every route. An audit row exists.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/AdminTrainingSessionTest.php`: create happy path, mismatched end date, too many weeks, inactive program, overlap allowed, authorization (participant, coach, guest), pagination order, audit row.
  - **Depends on:** 8.1.
  - **Done:** The end date rule lives in `TrainingSessionService::endsOn()` (used by `StoreTrainingSessionRequest` and `TrainingSessionFactory`). The end date message prints the date as "6 Dec 2026" (`j M Y`, like the list's date range) rather than a zero-padded "06 Dec 2026". The policy has `viewAny`, `create`, `update` and `manage`; `view` and `join` come with the participant and mentor screens. The admin routes keep `role:admin` in front of the policy, as the other admin routes do.

  - **Do:** `AdminTrainingSessionController@edit|update`, `UpdateTrainingSessionRequest` (same rules as store; `program_id` must equal the current one once the session has participants: "The program cannot change after participants have joined."). Shared form view with the program select disabled (and explained) when locked. Audit `training_session_updated` with the changed field names.
  - **Acceptance:** Title, weeks and dates update under SES-1 rules. With one participant, a changed `program_id` fails and nothing is saved; without participants it saves.
  - **Tests:** Extend `AdminTrainingSessionTest`: update happy path, mismatched dates, program change with and without participants, authorization.

The locked form shows the program in a disabled select with the explanation and submits the current program from a hidden `program_id` input. `TrainingSessionService::update()` recomputes `ends_on`, saves and records `training_session_updated` with `metadata.changed_fields` (column names, e.g. `["title", "weeks_count", "starts_on"]`); details that change nothing are neither saved nor audited. Each list row has an Edit link; saving returns to the list (no show page until 8.4).

Overview shows the header facts only (filled in 12.1); Mentors and Resources show their empty states until 10.2 and 8.6; Activities lists the session weeks' activities (week, title) until 12.2. Participants tab: name, employee ID, job role label, joined date (current mentor column added in 10.3), one eager-loaded query.

Mentors and Resources count 0 through `countedTab()` until 10.2 and 8.6 pass real counts. The Overview tab shows program, dates, weeks and status. Each list row's title now links to the dashboard, `update` and the edit form's Cancel return to the dashboard, and `store` still returns to the list. Authorization for `show` (participant, coach, guest) is in `AdminTrainingSessionTest`'s every-route tests; the new test file has the unknown-session 404 and checks the hash contract (`data-page-tabs`, each tab's `data-tab-key` and its hidden panel).

Participants tab: multi-select labelled "Name (Employee ID)" and a Remove button with the existing confirmation initializer. Audit `session_participant_added` / `session_participant_removed` per user. Return to `#participants`.
