- [x] **8.6 Manage session resources** (SES-4, ADM-5, SA-3, SA-4) (was TS 2.6)
  - **Do:** Migration + model `TrainingSessionResource`, enum `TrainingSessionResourceType`, factory. Config in `config/training.php`: `session_resource_max_kb` (102400) and allowed extensions/MIME types (pdf, doc, docx, xls, xlsx, ppt, pptx, png, jpg, jpeg, gif, webp, mp4). `StoreTrainingSessionResourceRequest` (`title` required; `type`; `file` required for files with those rules; `url` required http(s) for links; `week_number` nullable 1..`weeks_count`), `UpdateTrainingSessionResourceRequest` (title only). `TrainingSessionResourceService` stores files on the private `local` disk under `training-sessions/{id}/resources`, deletes them on removal. `AdminTrainingSessionResourceController@store|update|destroy`; `TrainingSessionResourceController@show` (admin-only for now; opened to members in 9.6) streams files inline or redirects to links. Resources tab grouped "Whole session", "Week 1", …, each row with type, size, Rename (inline form) and Remove. Add `admin/sessions/*/resources` to the `PostTooLargeException` handler in `bootstrap/app.php` with "The file must not exceed 100 MB." Audit add/rename/remove.
  - **Acceptance:** An admin uploads a 2 MB PDF to Week 1 and adds a YouTube link to the whole session; both appear grouped. A `.exe` or a 101 MB file fails validation. Week 5 on a 4-week session fails. Rename changes only the title. Remove deletes the row and the stored file. Non-admins get 403 on all write routes.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/AdminTrainingSessionResourceTest.php` with `Storage::fake('local')`: upload, link, invalid type, too large, week out of range, rename, remove (file gone), grouping on the tab, authorization, audit rows.
  - **Depends on:** 8.4.
  - **Done:** Migration `2026_10_23_143612_create_training_session_resources_table` (Design columns; `type` string(20), `url` text, `size_bytes` unsigned big int), model `TrainingSessionResource` (`type` cast to `TrainingSessionResourceType` with `label()`, `isFile()`), `TrainingSession::resources()`, `TrainingSessionResourceFactory` (a whole-session link by default; `file()`, `forWeek()`). Config `training.session_resource_max_kb`, `session_resource_extensions` and `session_resource_mimes`. Routes `admin.sessions.resources.store` (`POST admin/sessions/{trainingSession}/resources`), `admin.sessions.resources.update|destroy` (`PUT|DELETE …/resources/{resource}`) and `training.sessions.resources.show` (`GET training/sessions/{trainingSession}/resources/{resource}`), all with `scopeBindings()` through `TrainingSession::resources()`, so a resource of another session 404s. `StoreTrainingSessionResourceRequest` authorizes `manage`, uses the `resources` error bag and returns to `#resources`; `file` and `url` are `exclude_unless` their type, so the other type's field is ignored; messages "The file must not exceed 100 MB." (`maxFileSizeMessage()`, from the config), "The file must be a PDF, Word, Excel or PowerPoint document, an image or an MP4 video.", "The link must be a full web address starting with http:// or https://." and "This session has only N weeks.". `UpdateTrainingSessionResourceRequest` validates the title only and puts its errors in the resource's own bag (`renameResource{id}`, `errorBagFor()`), so the tab shows them beside the right row. `TrainingSessionResourceService::add()` stores the file first and deletes it again if the row or audit insert fails; `rename()` saves and audits only a changed title (`previous_title` in the metadata); `remove()` deletes the stored file only after the row's delete and audit commit. Audit rows `session_resource_added|renamed|removed` carry `training_session_id`, `training_session_resource_id` and `title` (no target user). `TrainingSessionResourceController@show` authorizes `manage` (admin-only until 9.6 adds `TrainingSessionResourcePolicy::view`), streams files inline with their stored MIME type and `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` (404 when the file is missing), and redirects links with `redirect()->away()`. The Resources tab has two add forms ("Upload a file", "Add a link"; title, file or link, and "For": Whole session or Week 1..N) and the groups "Whole session", "Week 1", … (weeks without resources are left out), each row with the linked title, type badge, file name and size or the link's host, a Rename disclosure (`<details>`, opens on its own error) and a Remove button through `form[data-confirm]`. The `PostTooLargeException` handler redirects `POST admin/sessions/*/resources` to the dashboard's `#resources` with the size message in the `resources` bag (see Open decisions: the message does not survive a real request). No seeder: 8.8 seeds the first session and the spec seeds no resources.
  - **Note (2026-10-23):** 8.9 replaces this batch's file uploads: resources become links only, with Google Drive previews (user decision 2026-10-23). The grouping, rename, remove, audit and the store / update / destroy routes stay.

New `x-session-resource` (`resources/views/components/session-resource.blade.php`): title, badge, host, an `x-ui.button` "Open in Drive" / "Open" (`target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`, screen-reader suffix ": {title} (opens in a new tab)"), the actions slot, and for Drive rows a `<details data-resource-preview>` whose summary reads "Show preview" / "Hide preview" (screen-reader suffix "of {title}") with an iframe without `src` (`aspect-4/3 max-h-[75vh] w-full`, `title="Preview of {title}"`) and the fixed help line. `initializeResourcePreviews()` in `resources/js/app.js` sets `src` from `data-preview-src` on the first `toggle` to open. New `tests/Unit/GoogleDriveLinkTest.php` (25 cases) and `tests/Feature/TurnTrainingSessionResourcesIntoLinksMigrationTest.php` (file row and its file gone, an unreferenced file kept, link row kept with `added_by`, old columns gone; `url` not null and `added_by` nulled when its admin is deleted). The migration ran up, down and up on a throwaway SQLite file; MySQL was checked with `migrate --pretend` only (see Open decisions). The acceptance grep for `session_resource_` still matches the kept audit actions (`session_resource_added|renamed|removed`) and `training_session_resource_id`; the enum, config keys, show route and controller are gone. **Browser check not done in the real app:** a headless Chrome run (DevTools protocol, 375 px and 1280 px) on a static copy of the component markup with the built CSS and JS showed no Google request and no iframe `src` while collapsed, the embed URL loading once on expand (kept on collapse and re-open), visible focus outlines on the summary and the open link, and no horizontal overflow. A manual check on the admin page, signed in to Google with a real shared file, is still to do.

- [x] **8.11 Read a chosen Drive video's details through the participant's connection** (VID-2)
  - **Do:** Add `GoogleDriveService::videoFile(GoogleDriveConnection $connection, string $fileId, ?string $resourceKey = null): array`, returning `array{id: string, name: string, mime_type: string, size_bytes: ?int, created_at: ?CarbonImmutable, modified_at: ?CarbonImmutable, resource_key: ?string}`.
    - Check the ID against `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$` (as `GoogleDriveConnectionService::selectDestination()` does) before any request.
    - Make one `GET https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/files/{id}` with the token from `GoogleDriveConnectionService::accessToken()`, which refreshes it when expired. Use `fields=id,name,mimeType,size,createdTime,modifiedTime,trashed,ownedByMe,resourceKey` and `supportsAllDrives=true`, send the resource key in the `X-Goog-Drive-Resource-Keys: {id}/{key}` header when given, and set a 15-second timeout.
    - Throw a `RuntimeException` with the participant-facing message, following the existing convention in `GoogleDriveConnectionService`:
      - not `video/*`: "The file you chose is not a video. Choose a video file."
      - trashed: "That video is in your Google Drive trash. Choose another video."
      - not owned: "Choose a video from your own Google Drive."
      - malformed ID, 403, 404, token failure or other errors: "The app could not open that file in your Google Drive. Choose it again, or reconnect Google Drive on your profile."
    - Log failures as `verifyFolderAccess()` does (file ID suffix, status and Google reason only; never tokens).
  - **Acceptance:** With `Http::fake`:
    - An owned, untrashed `video/mp4` returns the shape, with `size_bytes` cast from Drive's string and the dates parsed.
    - `application/pdf`, a trashed file, `ownedByMe: false`, and 403 and 404 responses each throw their message.
    - A malformed ID throws without sending any request.
    - A resource key is sent in the header.
    - An expired token is refreshed first.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/GoogleDriveVideoFileTest.php` (`Http::fake`, `Http::assertSent` / `assertNothingSent`; create the connection as `GoogleDriveConnectionTest` does).
  - **Depends on:** none.
  - **Done:** `GoogleDriveService::videoFile()` uses the `GoogleDriveConnectionService` the service already receives through its constructor, so no new dependency and no cycle. It checks the ID before any call (a malformed ID throws without logging), then makes one `GET files/{id}` (`acceptJson()`, `timeout(15)`, the fields and `supportsAllDrives=true` from the item, `X-Goog-Drive-Resource-Keys: {id}/{key}` only when a key is given). Any failure, including `accessToken()` throwing (no refresh token, refresh rejected), is logged as "Google Drive video lookup failed." with only `operation`, `file_id_suffix`, `http_status` and `google_reason`, and rethrown as a `RuntimeException` with the generic message (private `VIDEO_UNAVAILABLE_MESSAGE`; the Google exception is kept as `previous`). The video, trash and ownership checks run in that order on the response and are not logged, like `verifyFolderAccess()`'s. `size_bytes` is `(int)` when Drive's `size` is numeric, else null; the dates are `CarbonImmutable::parse()` of Drive's RFC 3339 times; `resource_key` is the posted key, else Drive's. Tests: new `GoogleDriveVideoFileTest` (13 cases with providers): the returned shape with the exact request URL and bearer token and one request; PDF, trashed and not-owned messages; 403 and 404 give the generic message and log the exact context without the token; four malformed IDs send nothing; the resource key header; an expired token is refreshed before the file read; an expired connection without a refresh token sends nothing. The 15-second timeout is not asserted (`Http::fake` does not expose request options).

- [x] **8.13 Return to the weekly video page after connecting Google Drive** (VID-2)
  - **Do:**
    - New `ConnectGoogleDriveRequest` for `GoogleDriveController::connect()`. It has `return_to` (nullable, string, max 2048) and a `returnTo(): ?string` helper that keeps the value only when it is a relative path starting with `/training/`: no scheme, no `//`, no backslash. Anything else falls back to the profile and is not an error.
    - `connect()` stores it in the `google_drive_oauth` session payload. `callback()` redirects there instead of `profile.show`, with the same status or error flash, for success, cancel and failure. Without it, behavior is unchanged.
    - The video page's connect link passes `return_to` as the page's relative URL (`route(…, absolute: false)`).
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Connecting from a week's video page and approving lands back on that page with "Google Drive connected successfully.". Cancelling lands there with the cancel message.
    - `return_to` values `https://evil.example`, `//evil.example`, `/\evil.example` and `/profile` all fall back to the profile.
    - Connecting from the profile works as today.
  - **Tests:** Extend `GoogleDriveConnectionTest` (fake the token and userinfo calls as it does): the return after success, cancel and failure; the open-redirect data set; no `return_to`.
  - **Depends on:** 8.12.
  - **Done:** New `ConnectGoogleDriveRequest` (`return_to` nullable, string, max 2048). Its `failedValidation()` is a no-op, so a failed rule never bounces the GET back with errors; `returnTo()` then returns null for a failed rule, and otherwise keeps the value only when, both as given and once percent-decoded, it starts with `/training/` and has no `//`, no backslash, no `.` or `..` segment and no whitespace or control character (so `/training/../profile`, `/training/%2F%2Fevil`, `/training/%5C…`, `/training/%2e%2e/…` and a CRLF all fall back). `connect()` stores `return_to` (the kept path or null) in the `google_drive_oauth` payload; `callback()` redirects every outcome after reading the payload (verification failure, cancel, error, exchange failure, success) to it with the same flash, or to `profile.show` without it; the non-participant redirect to login is unchanged. `WeeklyVideoRecordingPageService` passes `return_to` = `route('training.video-recording.edit', $week, absolute: false)`. The video page now also shows a `google_drive` error (an error alert under the status alert), so a failed connection is visible where it lands. Tests: `GoogleDriveConnectionTest` gained the return after approval, cancel and a failed token exchange (a data set; each lands on the video page, which shows the message; one connection only when approved) and 15 fallback cases (no `return_to`, empty, the four acceptance values, `//` and `\` under `/training/`, dot segments, encoded slashes, backslash and dots, a line break, 2049 characters, an array), each starting the connection without errors and returning to the profile. `WeeklyVideoRecordingPageTest`'s two connect href assertions now include `return_to`. Full suite 1086 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. Not checked against real Google: the round trip from the video page through the consent screen.

- [x] **8.16 Share the chosen video with the company's Google Workspace domain** (VID-3; Q5 answered 2026-10-23)
  - **Do:**
    - **Config.** `services.google_drive.share_domain` from `GOOGLE_DRIVE_SHARE_DOMAIN`, read through a new `GoogleDriveConfigurationService::getShareDomain()`.
    - **Migration.** Add `drive_permission_id` (string, nullable) and `shared_at` (timestamp, nullable) to `weekly_video_recordings`.
    - **Drive service.** `GoogleDriveService::shareWithDomain(GoogleDriveConnection $connection, string $fileId, ?string $resourceKey, string $domain): string` sends `POST files/{id}/permissions?supportsAllDrives=true` with `{type: domain, role: reader, domain, allowFileDiscovery: false}` and returns the permission ID. `removePermission(GoogleDriveConnection $connection, string $fileId, string $permissionId): void` does the reverse.
    - **Choosing.** `WeeklyVideoRecordingService::choose()` shares the file after saving it and stores `drive_permission_id` and `shared_at`. When the participant replaces a video, it removes the old file's permission (best effort). If sharing fails, or no domain is configured, the choice is kept with `shared_at` null, the failure is logged, and the participant gets a warning flash: "Your video is saved, but it could not be shared. In Google Drive, share it with everyone at {domain} as Viewer."
    - **Pages.** The video page hint adds "Choosing a video shares it, view only, with everyone at {domain} who has the link, so your mentor can watch it." The mentor version of `x-weekly-video-actions` shows "Shared with {domain}", or "Not shared yet. Ask the participant to share it in Google Drive."
    - **Release checklist.** Add `GOOGLE_DRIVE_SHARE_DOMAIN` to the 8.15 step.
  - **Acceptance:**
    - With Drive faked, choosing a video sends one permission request with that body and stores its ID and `shared_at`.
    - Replacing a video deletes the old permission and creates the new one.
    - A failing permission request keeps the choice and shows the warning.
    - Without a domain, no permission request is sent.
    - Mentors see the shared state.
  - **Tests:** Extend `GoogleDriveVideoFileTest` (share and remove requests), `WeeklyVideoRecordingTest` (the share, replace, failure and no-domain cases) and `WeeklyVideoActionsComponentTest` (the shared state).
  - **Depends on:** 8.15.
  - **Done:** Config `services.google_drive.share_domain` (`GOOGLE_DRIVE_SHARE_DOMAIN`, also added to `.env.example`), read by `GoogleDriveConfigurationService::getShareDomain()`, which trims and lowercases it and returns null unless it matches the new `SHARE_DOMAIN_PATTERN` (a plain hostname with at least two labels: no scheme, port, path or `@`), so an invalid value counts as unset. Config only, as the item says (Open decisions: the share domain is not on the admin page). Migration `2026_10_23_200206_add_sharing_to_weekly_video_recordings_table` adds `drive_permission_id` (string, nullable, after `drive_resource_key`) and `shared_at` (timestamp, nullable, after `chosen_at`); the model gets the fillable fields, the `shared_at` cast and `isShared()`, the factory writes both as null and has a `shared()` state. `GoogleDriveService::shareWithDomain()` and `removePermission()` follow `videoFile()`: IDs checked against the shared `DRIVE_ID_PATTERN` (and the domain against `SHARE_DOMAIN_PATTERN`) before any request, the token from `accessToken()`, `acceptJson()`, `timeout(15)`, the resource key header when given (share only; the remove signature has none and the owner does not need it); the share is `POST files/{id}/permissions?supportsAllDrives=true` with exactly `{type: domain, role: reader, domain, allowFileDiscovery: false}`, and an answer whose `id` is missing or not `DRIVE_ID_PATTERN` counts as a failure, since the ID later goes into the delete URL; the remove is `DELETE files/{id}/permissions/{permissionId}?supportsAllDrives=true`. Failures log "Google Drive video share failed." / "Google Drive video permission removal failed." with the same four keys as `videoFile()` (`operation` `permissions.create` / `permissions.delete`; never the token) and throw a `RuntimeException` ("The app could not share that video in your Google Drive." / "The app could not stop sharing the earlier video in your Google Drive."; not shown to participants). `videoFile()`'s request building and logging moved into private `videoRequest()` and `logVideoFailure()`, output unchanged. `WeeklyVideoRecordingService::choose()` now saves the choice with `shared_at` null (and `drive_permission_id` null, or kept when the same file is chosen again), then shares it and saves the permission ID and `shared_at`; only then, when the earlier row had a different file and a permission ID, it removes that permission, best effort (a failure is logged by the Drive service and ignored). So re-choosing the same file shares it again and never deletes its permission (Drive answers with the same domain permission). If the share fails the earlier permission of the same file stays recorded (so a later replacement still removes it) but `shared_at` stays null. Without a usable domain no permission request is sent and "Weekly video not shared: no Google Workspace share domain is configured." is logged with the recording ID. New `sharingWarning()` gives the flash, null once shared: "Your video is saved, but it could not be shared. In Google Drive, share it with everyone at {domain} as Viewer.", or without a domain "… share it with your mentor as Viewer." (so no empty domain is printed). The controller flashes it as `warning` next to the unchanged `status`, and the video page shows it as a warning alert (`role="alert"`) under the status alert. Pages: `WeeklyVideoRecordingPageService` adds `shareHint` ("Choosing a video shares it, view only, with everyone at {domain} who has the link, so your mentor can watch it.", null without a domain, when nothing is shared), appended to the intro wherever it shows (the connected form and the not-connected card); `x-weekly-video-actions` takes a `share-domain` prop and, with `for-mentor` only, shows above the help line "Shared with {domain}" (`shared_at` set; "Shared with the company's Google Workspace" when no domain is passed) or "Not shared yet. Ask the participant to share it in Google Drive." (`shared_at` null, whatever the config); `CoachParticipantPageService` passes `shareDomain` and the coach participant page hands it to the component. The state follows the row's `shared_at`; the domain named is the one configured now, because the row does not store the domain it was shared with (Open decisions). Release checklist step 1 lists `GOOGLE_DRIVE_SHARE_DOMAIN` (`.env` only, a plain hostname, what happens without it) and its final check now also expects no sharing warning and the domain as Viewer in Drive. Tests: `GoogleDriveVideoFileTest` +18 cases (share: the exact URL, body, token and resource key header in one request; a 403 logged and thrown; two unusable permission answers; eight malformed file ID or domain targets with nothing sent; remove: the exact DELETE; a 404 logged and thrown; four malformed targets with nothing sent); `WeeklyVideoRecordingTest` +9 (the share with its body, stored ID and `shared_at`, no warning and the page hint; the domain trimmed and lowercased; a replacement shares the new file, then deletes the old permission, three requests in order; a failed old-permission removal keeps the new shared choice without a warning; the same file again sends no delete and keeps its permission ID; a refused share keeps the choice unshared, still removes the replaced file's permission and shows the warning on the page; unset, empty and invalid domains send no permission request, log once, warn with "your mentor" and show no hint); `WeeklyVideoActionsComponentTest` +4 (shared with the domain; shared without one; not shared despite a domain; the participant version shows no state); `CoachParticipantPageTest` +1 (the configured domain reaches the page, shared and not shared rows). Existing tests were not changed: without a domain they send no permission request. Full suite 1125 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. The migration ran up, down and up on a throwaway SQLite file; its MySQL SQL was checked with `migrate --pretend --path=…` only (two `alter table … add … null after …`); its `down()` was not checked on MySQL and it has not run on the dev MySQL database (Open decisions). Not checked against real Google: that Drive accepts the domain permission on a participant's file with `drive.file` access, that re-sharing the same file returns the same permission ID, and the warning and mentor state at phone width in a browser.

## Phase 9 — Session-scoped training for participants, and repository links

> **History.** This phase built the training-session model that `docs/plans/curriculum-sessions-merge.md` superseded (2026-10-28). Its batch notes describe the code as each batch left it; for the current design see that plan and Design above.

- [x] **9.1 Tie submissions, gate evaluations and weekly videos to a session** (PAR-3, MIG-1) (was TS 4.1)
  - **Note:** `training_session_id` ships nullable for now (Open decisions, option (a)); 9.4 makes it not null once every write path passes the session.
  - **Do:** Migration adding `training_session_id` (not null, FK restrict) to `activity_submissions`, `gate_evaluations`, `weekly_video_recordings`, swapping the unique keys and index (see Design). The migration first checks the three tables are empty and throws a clear exception otherwise (MIG-1 guarantees they are after 2.1, DAT-2). Models: fillable, `trainingSession()` relations, `TrainingSession::activitySubmissions()` etc. Factories (including 8.10's `WeeklyVideoRecordingFactory`): default `training_session_id` from a `TrainingSession` factory whose program owns the activity's/gate's/week's program where practical. `weekly_video_recordings` already holds Drive file references (8.12).
  - **Acceptance:** Migrates on SQLite and MySQL; refuses to run with existing rows. The same participant can have a gate evaluation for the same gate in two sessions.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/SessionScopedRecordsTest.php`: two sessions, same gate/user → two evaluations; same user/week → two videos; existing factories still work across the suite.
  - **Depends on:** Gate A (4.4), 8.1.

- [x] **9.2 Add session access control and nest the week and activity pages** (PAR-4, PAR-6, SES-3) (was TS 4.2)
  - **Do:** `TrainingSessionPolicy::view` (admin, member participant, any coach until 10.4). The week-in-session middleware (Design). Move `training.weeks.show` and `training.activities.show` under `training/sessions/{trainingSession}/weeks/{trainingWeek}`; update `TrainingWeekController`, `TrainingActivityController`, `TrainingWeekPageService`, `TrainingActivityPageService` and every `route()` call to pass the session (find them with `grep -rn "training.weeks.show\|training.activities.show" app resources tests`). Sidebar "active" patterns updated.
  - **Acceptance:** A member opens their session's week and activity pages. A participant not in the session gets 403 on both; a participant removed from the session gets 403. A week from another program, or beyond `weeks_count`, returns 404.

Middleware `EnsureWeekBelongsToSession` (alias `session.week`, `bootstrap/app.php`) 404s unless `includesWeek()` (another program, an inactive week, or beyond `weeks_count`). `training.weeks.show` and `training.activities.show` keep their names and moved to `training/sessions/{trainingSession}/weeks/{trainingWeek}` and `…/activities/{activity}` in a group with `can:view,trainingSession` then `session.week`, so a non-member gets 403 before the week check. Both controllers take the session; `TrainingWeekPageService::showData()` links the activities within it and `TrainingActivityPageService::showData()` returns it as `trainingSession` for 9.3's nested submission routes. - [x] **9.9 Generate participant notifications per session** (PAR-3) (was TS 4.9)
  - **Do:** `ParticipantNotificationService::sync()` iterates the participant's non-completed sessions instead of the active program, with keys prefixed `session:{id}:` and action URLs to the nested routes; delete or resolve notifications whose session the participant left. `ParticipantProgressObserver` keeps queuing per participant.
  - **Acceptance:** A participant in two sessions gets separate "missing submission" notifications per session, each linking to the right session's activity. Removal from a session resolves its notifications.
  - **Tests:** Update `ParticipantNotificationsTest`, `ParticipantNotificationTriggersTest`; add a two-session case.
  - **Depends on:** 9.5.
  - **Done:** `ParticipantNotificationService::sync()` no longer reads the active program or `ParticipantWeekSessionService`: it takes the participant's memberships of not-completed sessions (any program) and, per session, every session week they can open or have passed there (`WeekProgressionService::weekStatesForMemberships()`, one call per program), raising the same notifications with the same titles, bodies and conditions from their work in that session only (latest attempt, its versions, reviews and evidence; the passed evaluation; the weekly video; `GateReadinessService::readinessForMemberships()`). Keys are `session:{id}:week:{id}:activity:{id}:{incomplete|draft|revision|review|evidence}`, `session:{id}:week:{id}:{video|gate}` and `session:{id}:event:{review|gate}:{id}`, and every link goes to the nested route of that session. The reads take a fixed number of queries per program (27 for one program in the test, with one or three weeks of one or three activities in two sessions); it loads the session's stored notifications once and writes only those that change, so a sync that changes nothing writes nothing. Each sync first deletes the participant's notifications whose key has no `session:` prefix (the pre-9.9 keys, active or resolved; their links point at pre-9.2 to pre-9.4 URLs) and resolves the active notifications of every session not in the list, i.e. one they left or one that has completed (resolved rows keep their link). Joining, being added and being removed now queue the sync: `TrainingSessionParticipant` is observed by `ParticipantProgressObserver` (created and deleted; updates do not), which covers 9.7's `join()`, 9.8's registration, the admin add and the admin remove (`detach()` with the custom pivot fires `deleted`). Tests: `ParticipantNotificationsTest` (keys carry the session; new: two sessions get separate notifications linking within each and work in one does not count in the other; the admin removal route resolves that session's notifications and keeps the other's; a completed session raises nothing and its active notifications are resolved; pre-9.9 notifications are deleted and replaced; the query count of a repeated sync does not grow with weeks and activities; the no-session case now asserts a participant in no session gets no notifications); `ParticipantNotificationTriggersTest` (joining, an admin adding two participants and an admin removing one queue the sync; registering with a training ticked raises that training's notifications); `HomeNavigationTest` (the disclosure case creates its notification after joining the session, because joining now syncs and deletes a factory notification without a session key). Full suite 1208 tests, exit 0. No markup changed, so no build.

- [x] **9.10 Sync Drive workbooks per session** (PAR-3) (was TS 4.10)
  - **Do:** `SyncTrainingDataJob` and `TrainingWorkbookService::forWeek()` work per (session, week): one managed folder per session (named by session title) containing its week workbooks and evidence (weekly videos stay in the participant's own Drive and are not copied, 8.15); `google_drive_exports.scope_key` includes the session ID. Workbook rows add the employee ID.
  - **Acceptance:** A participant in two sessions gets two folders whose workbooks contain only that session's work. Re-running the sync updates rather than duplicates files.
  - **Tests:** Update `GoogleDriveSyncTest` / `GoogleDriveExportTest` with two sessions.
  - **Depends on:** 9.4.
  - **Done:** The managed folders live in the participant's **own** Drive, under the folder they chose with the Picker (the sync's captured `destination_folder_id`), written through their own `drive.file` connection; nothing goes to an admin's Drive. `SyncTrainingDataJob` now reads the participant's memberships (every session they are in, upcoming, active or completed, earliest start first) and the active weeks of those sessions' programs in two queries, and builds `{destination}/{session folder}/Week N/Week N.xlsx` for each session week (`TrainingSession::includesWeek()`, so weeks beyond `weeks_count` are left out), with the week's evidence under `Week N/Evidences/{activity}`. The session folder is named by the session title; when two of the participant's sessions share a title it gets the date range (`dateRangeLabel()`), and also `#id` when the dates match too, because `GoogleDriveService::ensureFolder()` falls back to a same-name lookup and would otherwise put two sessions in one folder. Tracking keys (`google_drive_exports.scope_key`, no schema change): `session_folder` `session:{id}`; `week_folder`, `weekly_workbook_nested` and `week_evidences_folder` `session:{id}:week:{weekId}`; `evidence_activity_folder` `session:{id}:week:{weekId}:activity:{activityId}`; evidence files keep `google_drive_evidence_exports` per evidence and destination (an evidence row belongs to one session through its submission). Re-running a sync passes every tracked ID, so files are updated in place. Evidence is discovered once per session (one query, grouped by week) instead of once per week number across all sessions, and every session week syncs its evidence, Week 1 included; the empty `Week 1/Evidences` folder the old job always created is gone (Evidences is created only when the week has evidence; Open decision "Drive sync still hardcodes Weeks 1 to 4" resolved). Weekly videos are still not copied (8.15). `component_results` keys are now "{session folder} / Week N.xlsx" so two sessions' workbooks do not overwrite each other's result on the profile's failed-components list. `TrainingWorkbookService::forWeek(User, TrainingSession, TrainingWeek)` takes that week's active activities only (it used to take every program's activities with that week number) and the participant's latest attempt in that session, in four queries whatever the number of activities (it used to run three per activity); each sheet and the header-only sheet gain an "Employee ID" column right after "Participant" (as 1.6 did for the CSVs). Old tracking rows: production has none at the first sync, because 2.1 (same release) empties `google_drive_exports` and deletes every participant; a local or staging database where a participant synced after 2.1 keeps their pre-9.10 rows unused and their old top-level `Week N` folders and workbooks stay in that participant's Drive, no longer updated; the new tree is created beside them, so nothing is duplicated inside it (Open decisions). Tests: `GoogleDriveSyncTest` gained `test_a_participant_in_two_sessions_gets_a_folder_per_session_with_only_that_sessions_work` (two sessions of one program, one of them one week long: the exact folders and uploads with their parents, each session's workbook holding only its own attempt and the employee ID, evidence only under its own session's week, the result keys, the scope keys; a second run passes every tracked folder and file ID and adds no tracking row), `test_sessions_with_the_same_title_get_folders_told_apart_by_dates_then_id`, `test_sync_of_a_participant_in_no_session_uploads_nothing`, `test_week_workbook_shows_the_employee_id_and_only_the_latest_attempt_in_its_session` (column B, latest attempt, the other session's attempt absent) and `test_week_workbook_query_count_does_not_grow_with_activities`; `test_nested_sync_does_not_adopt_legacy_root_workbook_tracking` became `test_sync_does_not_adopt_tracking_from_before_training_sessions` (pre-9.10 `week_folder` / `weekly_workbook_nested` rows and the older `weekly_workbook` row are left as they were and not reused); the workbook cases pass a session and week (one also asserts another program's week-2 activity is left out, the header-only one the new column); the evidence, video and repeated-sync cases put the participant in a session and their expected folder and upload counts follow the session's weeks (the repeated-sync case now checks the session folder and week folders under it, and no Evidences folder). `GoogleDriveExportTest` covers `GoogleDriveService` only, which did not change, so it is unchanged. Full suite 1213 tests, exit 0; no markup, CSS or JS changed, so no build. Not checked against real Google: the folder tree in a real Drive.

- [x] **9.11 Ask for the repository URL on the session page** (LOG-2) (was DP 6.3)
  - **Do:** Migration adding `repository_url` (nullable string 255) to `training_session_participants`; `App\Rules\GitHubRepositoryUrl`; `UpdateSessionRepositoryRequest` (member only); a form on the participant session page (prompt "Add your GitHub repository for this session" until set, then the link with a Change action); show the URL (or "No repository yet") on the mentor's submission review, scoring and gate checklist pages for that participant and session.
  - **Acceptance:** A member saves `https://github.com/ana/l4-accelerator` and sees it; `https://github.com/ana`, `https://github.com/ana/repo/tree/main` and non-GitHub URLs fail; a non-member gets 403; the mentor sees the link on review, scoring and checklist pages; Join stays one click.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Unit/GitHubRepositoryUrlRuleTest.php`, `tests/Feature/SessionRepositoryUrlTest.php`; extend `CoachGateReviewPageTest`.
  - **Depends on:** 7.2, 9.5 (session page; submissions and gate reviews already carry their session from 9.3 and 9.4).
  - **Done:** Migration `2026_10_23_223926_add_repository_url_to_training_session_participants_table` adds `repository_url` (nullable `string` 255, after `added_by`; a plain `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN` on both databases, no table rebuild; `down()` drops it). New `App\Rules\GitHubRepositoryUrl` reuses 7.1's `GitHubUrl::accepts()` (scheme, host, owner, no whitespace or markup) and then requires exactly `/{owner}/{repo}` with an optional trailing slash: the repository segment is letters, digits, `.`, `-` and `_`, not `.` / `..`, not ending in `.git` (any case), and no deeper path, query or fragment; message "Enter the link to your GitHub repository (https://github.com/owner/repository).", with a static `accepts()` like `GitHubUrl`. `PUT training/sessions/{trainingSession}/repository` (`training.sessions.repository.update`, `role:participant`) runs `TrainingSessionController@updateRepository`: `UpdateSessionRepositoryRequest::authorize()` allows only a participant in the route's session (anyone else 403, nothing written); rules `required|string|max:255` plus the new rule (attribute "repository link"); `TrainingSessionService::saveRepositoryUrl()` updates the membership (an update does not queue the notification sync, as before) and the participant returns to the session page with "Your GitHub repository is saved.". Join and registration are unchanged (one click, `repository_url` null). `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageService::showData()` adds `repositoryUrl` (from the membership it already loads, no extra query) and `repositoryFormUrl`; `training-sessions/show` has a card between the header and the tabs: without a repository the heading "Add your GitHub repository for this session", a line on why, and the form; with one, "Your GitHub repository", the link (new tab, `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, screen-reader "(opens in a new tab)") and a native `<details>` "Change" disclosure (styled like the admin Rename) holding the same form, opened when the save failed. The form (new partial `training-sessions/repository-form`, fully styled `type="url"` input with placeholder, hint linked by `aria-describedby`, `aria-invalid` / `aria-errormessage` and the error under it, secondary "Save repository") needs no JS. Mentor pages: new `x-session-repository` component prints "Repository:" and the link (same link attributes) or "No repository yet" in the page header of `coach/review`, `scoring/edit` and `coach/gate_review`; `CoachSubmissionReviewPageService`, `ScoringPageService::editData()` and `CoachGateReviewPageService` read it with one query each through the new `TrainingSessionParticipant::forParticipantInSession()` scope (the submission's session and participant; the Gate review's route session), so the mentor sees the repository of that session only, and "No repository yet" when the participant has none there or is no longer in it. Tests: new `tests/Unit/GitHubRepositoryUrlRuleTest.php` (4 accepted, 19 rejected values); new `tests/Feature/SessionRepositoryUrlTest.php` (Join stays one click and the page then shows the prompt and an empty form; a member saves `https://github.com/ana/l4-accelerator` and sees the link with Change and the filled form; a member changes it; owner only, `/tree/main`, GitLab, blank and too long fail on `repository_url` and keep the saved link; a rejected change reopens the disclosure with the error and the input; another session's participant, a coach and an admin get 403 and nothing is saved; the review and scoring pages show the submission session's repository and not the other session's, or "No repository yet"); `CoachGateReviewPageTest` (the checklist page shows the session's repository, and "No repository yet" in the participant's other session; the existing query-count case still passes). Full suite 1249 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. The migration's MySQL SQL was checked with `migrate --pretend --path=…` only (`alter table training_session_participants add repository_url varchar(255) null after added_by`); it has not run on MySQL, and its `down()` has not been run on either database. Not checked in a browser: the repository card and the Change disclosure at phone width.

## Phase 10 — Mentors, and demo enrollment

> **History.** This phase built the training-session model that `docs/plans/curriculum-sessions-merge.md` superseded (2026-10-28). Its batch notes describe the code as each batch left it; for the current design see that plan and Design above.

- [x] **10.1 Add session mentors and mentor assignments** (MEN-1, MEN-2, MEN-3; prerequisite) (was TS 5.1)
  - **Do:** Migrations and models for `training_session_mentors` and `mentor_assignments` (Design), factories. `MentorAssignmentService`: `addMentors()`, `removeMentor()` (ends that mentor's current assignments in the session), `assign(TrainingSession, User $participant, User $mentor, User $actor)` (ends the current row and opens a new one in a transaction; no-op when unchanged), `currentMentor()`, `history()`. `TrainingSessionService::removeParticipant()` ends the participant's current assignment. Relations on `TrainingSession` and `User` (`mentoredSessions()`, `currentMentorAssignments()`).
  - **Acceptance:** Assigning B after A leaves A's row ended at the change time and B's current; history lists both in order. Assigning a mentor who is not a session mentor, or a participant not in the session, throws a validation error.
  - **Tests:** New `tests/Feature/MentorAssignmentServiceTest.php`: assign, change, unchanged no-op, remove mentor ends assignments, remove participant ends assignment, invalid mentor/participant.
  - **Depends on:** 8.5.
  - **Done:** Migrations `2026_10_23_232343_create_training_session_mentors_table` (`id`, `training_session_id` and `user_id` FKs cascading on delete, `user_id` indexed, timestamps, unique pair) and `2026_10_23_232344_create_mentor_assignments_table` (`training_session_id`, `participant_id` and `mentor_id` FKs cascading on delete like `coach_participant_assignments`, `assigned_by` FK users nullable null on delete, `started_at` datetime, `ended_at` datetime nullable, timestamps; index (`training_session_id`, `participant_id`, `ended_at`) named `mentor_assignments_session_participant_ended_index` because the default name is 68 characters, over MySQL's 64, and (`mentor_id`, `ended_at`)). Both only create tables (no rename or rebuild, so not the SQLite foreign-key trap). New pivot model `TrainingSessionMentor` (shaped like `TrainingSessionParticipant`) and model `MentorAssignment` (`current()` scope = `ended_at` null; `forParticipantInSession()` scope; `trainingSession`, `participant`, `mentor`, `assignedBy` relations). `TrainingSession::mentors()` (belongsToMany through `TrainingSessionMentor`), `mentorAssignments()` and `includesMentor(User)`; `User::mentoredSessions()` and `currentMentorAssignments()` (as mentor, every session). Factories: new `MentorAssignmentFactory` (a coach's current assignment in a new session; `ended(?CarbonInterface)`; it does not create the memberships) and `TrainingSessionFactory::withMentors(User ...)`. No seeder: demo mentors and assignments come with 10.10. `MentorAssignmentService`: `addMentors(TrainingSession, list<int>, User $admin)` attaches and records `session_mentor_added` per mentor; `removeMentor(TrainingSession, User $mentor, User $admin)` ends that mentor's current assignments in that session only (now), detaches them and records `session_mentor_removed`; `assign(TrainingSession, User $participant, User $mentor, User $actor)` locks the participant's membership row (`lockForUpdate`, so two concurrent assignments cannot both open a row), throws a `ValidationException` on `participant` ("The participant is not in this training session.") or `mentor_id` ("The selected mentor is not a mentor of this training session."), returns the current row unchanged (no audit) when the mentor is already current, and otherwise ends the current row and opens the new one at the same instant (`assigned_by` = actor) and records `session_mentor_assigned` on the participant with `training_session_id`, `previous_mentor_id` (null for a first assignment) and `mentor_id`; `currentMentor()`; `history()` (every row for the participant in the session, earliest `started_at` first, with `mentor`); `endCurrentAssignment()`. The audit rows are written in the service (as `TrainingSessionService` does), in the same transaction as the change, so 10.2 and 10.3 only need to assert them over HTTP. `TrainingSessionService` now takes `MentorAssignmentService`, and `removeParticipant()` ends the participant's current assignment in the session before detaching them; the ended row stays as history, and a re-added participant has no mentor until one is assigned. Tests: new `tests/Feature/MentorAssignmentServiceTest.php` (8 cases: add mentors with audit rows; first assignment with `assigned_by`, start time, `currentMentorAssignments()` and audit metadata; change A → B ends A at the change time, B current, history [A, B], audit previous/new IDs; same mentor again is a no-op with one row and one audit; removing a mentor ends their two assignments in the session but not another mentor's or their own in another session; removing a participant ends their assignment, keeps another participant's and keeps the history; a coach who mentors only another session, and a participant only in another session, fail validation with nothing written). Full suite 1257 tests, exit 0; no markup, CSS or JS changed, so no build. The migrations' MySQL SQL was checked with `migrate --pretend --path=…` only; they have not run on MySQL, and their `down()` has not been run on either database.

The 2.1 migration `2026_10_23_060944_delete_qa_training_data` now skips a missing table in its participant-owned rows (it already did so for the training tables), so `DeleteQaTrainingDataMigrationTest` still runs it on the current schema; its behavior on the schema it ran against is unchanged, and the test no longer seeds or checks `coach_participant_assignments`. **Interim readers (session-agnostic until 10.5 to 10.9):** the new `User::currentMentors()` (belongs-to-many through `mentor_assignments` with `ended_at` null, one row per session, names de-duplicated where printed) replaces `assignedCoaches` in the participant landing page's Mentor field (`ParticipantDashboardController` passes `mentorNames`), the coach participants list ("Assigned to me" = the viewer currently mentors them in any session; mentor names), `CoachReportService` (the `mentor` / `assigned_coach` filters and the progress CSV's mentor filter), `CoachProgressReportPageService` (Mentors column), `MissingSubmissionReportService` (mentor filter and column), the scoring overview (`assigned_to_me` filter, Assigned Mentor column), `ScoringReportService` (`assigned_coach` filter), `ScoringReportController`'s two CSVs (mentor columns) and `CoachRecoveryRequestPageService` (a coach lists the pending requests of participants they currently mentor in any session, so a request from another session can be listed but deciding it is a 403). Still to do in later batches: 10.5 takes the session on `coach.participants.show` (its Gate rows link to Gate reviews in sessions the viewer may not mentor, which are now 403) and scopes the list; 10.6 scopes the review queue, the review page (still viewable read only by any coach through `SubmissionVersionPolicy::view`, whose evidence links are now 403 for a non-mentor) and recovery requests by session; 10.7 turns the read-only Gate review for a session mentor who is not the participant's mentor into a 403 and scopes the board; 10.8 scopes the scoring overview, participant page, scoring page (still read only for any coach) and reports by session instead of `currentMentors`; 10.9 scopes the dashboard, progress and missing-submission reports and attendance. Tests: new `tests/Feature/MentorScopeAuthorizationTest.php` (5 cases, on a fixture where an admin assigns Ana and two weeks later Ben in session X, after Ana reviewed an earlier version, and Cy mentors the same participant in session Y of the same program: Ben reviews, records the second reading, scores, completes the week 1 Gate, decides the recovery request and downloads the evidence, and Ana's earlier review still names Ana on the review page; Ana gets 403 on each of those six URLs with nothing changed; Ben gets 403 on the participant's work in Y and Cy on their work in X; only an admin and Ben see the weekly video link on the Gate review, Ana sees it read only without it and Cy gets 403; an admin who is no mentor does all six). Checked that the cases fail without the current-assignment condition, without the session condition and with the video link unconditional. Updated: `AuthenticationAuthorizationTest` (the `canManageParticipant` case now asserts `mentorsParticipantInSession()` for admin, current, previous and other-session mentors; dropped irrelevant attaches in two route-gone cases), `AdminUserManagementTest` (three assignment cases replaced by one: the form shows no mentor section and the old route is a 404), `AdminUserFormPageTest` (no mentor section; its query-count case now varies attendance only), `CoachGateReviewPageTest` (`gateFixture(mentor:)` makes the coach a session mentor and the current mentor; the read-only case now uses a session mentor who is not the participant's), `TrainingSessionAccessTest` (admins and the session's mentors open week and activity pages; new case: a mentor of another session gets 403), `DeleteQaTrainingDataMigrationTest`, and the setups of `ActivityMarkdownRenderingTest`, `AttendanceTest`, `CoachOperationsControllerTest`, `CoachOperationsTest`, `CoachParticipantListPageTest`, `CoachParticipantPageTest`, `CoachProgressReportPageTest`, `CoachRecoveryRequestPageTest`, `CoachReportsTest`, `CoachSubmissionReviewPageTest`, `GateReadinessTest`, `HomeNavigationTest`, `InterviewSchedulingTest`, `MissingSubmissionReportPageTest`, `ParticipantNotificationTriggersTest`, `ParticipantWorkflowTest`, `ScoringEditPageTest`, `ScoringOverviewPageTest`, `ScoringParticipantPageTest`, `ScoringReportControllerTest`, `ScoringTest`, `SessionRepositoryUrlTest` and `TrainingWeekPageTest`, which now create a `MentorAssignment` in the record's session (and add the coach as a session mentor where a session route is used) instead of attaching a global assignment. Full suite 1276 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (markup only removed). The drop migration's MySQL SQL was checked with `migrate --pretend --path=…` (`drop table if exists`); it has not run on MySQL (the local database also still has 9.11's and 10.1's migrations pending), and its `down()` has not been run on either database.

  - **Do:** `CoachParticipantListPageService` lists one row per (participant, session) where the viewer is the current mentor (admins: all), with a session filter (`FilterCoachParticipantsRequest` gains `session`), employee ID and session columns, and the "Assigned to me" label removed. `CoachParticipantPageService` / `coach.participants.show` take the session (route or query) and show only that session's work; 403 for non-mentors.

a previous mentor, a mentor of the participant in another session and a session mentor of other participants get 403. `CoachParticipantPageService::showData(User $viewer, TrainingSession, User $participant)` loads only the session's submissions (with `activity.week` and `versions`) and weekly videos (with `week`), the current mentor in the session (`MentorAssignmentService::currentMentor()`), `isAdmin` and the Gate rows of the session's weeks; attendance stays global. `access`, `canManage`, the per-row `can_manage` and `mentorNames` went (a non-mentor can no longer open the page), so the view drops the read-only notice, the "Read only — this participant is not assigned to you." line and the `@if($canManage)` around the weekly video card; the "Assigned Mentors" card became "Training session" (title, dates, "Mentor: {name}" or "No mentor assigned.", and the admin or "Assigned to you" notice), and every Gate row has its Review Gate link. `CoachGateOverviewService::participantOverview()` became `participantSessionOverview(TrainingSession, User)` (the Gates whose week is in `TrainingSession::weeks()`, readiness through `GateReadinessService::readinessForGates()`, the evaluations in the session), so `ParticipantWeekSessionService` had no caller left and is deleted with `ParticipantWeekSessionServiceTest` (2 cases), as the "Interim week links" decision planned. `CoachParticipantListPageService` no longer uses `CoachReportService::participants()`: it pages `TrainingSessionParticipant` rows (one per participant and session) of users with the Participant role, all for an admin and, for a coach, only those with a current `mentor_assignments` row naming them (a `whereExists` on the row's participant and session), with the current mentor's name and the session's submission and Gate evaluation counts as subselects (not loaded), the participant (`id`, `name`, `email`, `employee_id`, `drive_link`, with attendance sessions and Drive connection) and the session eager loaded, ordered by participant name, then the latest session first, then id. `FilterCoachParticipantsRequest` gains `session` (nullable integer, `Rule::exists(TrainingSession::class, 'id')`), a plain query parameter so 10.9 can link to `coach.participants?session={id}`; the filter offers every session to an admin and the sessions a coach mentors (`mentoredSessions()`), latest start first, labelled "Title (9 Nov – 6 Dec 2026)". Table columns: Participant, Employee ID (or "—"), Training session (title and dates), Mentor (name or "Not assigned"; the "Assigned to me" and "Admin access" badges are gone), Training Drive, Google Drive, Attendance, Submissions, Gates, Action (Inspect links to the row's session). The "Showing … of N participants" line counts rows, so a participant in two sessions counts twice. Participants in no session are no longer listed (Open decisions). Other links now carry the session: the retry redirect in `CoachReviewController::retry()` (the submission's session), the Gate board rows (the row's session; the board is not mentor-scoped until 10.7, so a coach's link to someone they do not mentor is a 403), and the missing-submission report rows (`MissingSubmissionReportService` now maps each participant's open weeks to the earliest membership that opens them and puts that `trainingSession` in the row; the CSV is unchanged until 10.9). Updated: `CoachOperationsTest` (new `mentorInSession()` helper; the weekly video case now expects 403 for an unassigned coach; the three list cases make the coach the participants' mentor in a session; the pagination case names the participants "Participant 01" to "26" because the list is ordered by name; the two retry cases redirect to the session's page), `CoachOperationsControllerTest` (the participant detail query count opens the page as the participant's mentor in the session of their records, `mentoredParticipantUrl()`), `CoachGateBoardPageTest` and `MissingSubmissionReportPageTest` (participant links carry the session; `enrollParticipants()` returns the session), `DriveLinkTest` and `EmptyTrainingDataTest` (open the page in a session; the empty-data case renamed `test_coach_participant_page_opens_without_any_program_weeks_or_training_data`, since a session needs a program). Checked that the query-count cases fail without the session eager load (list) and without the submissions' `activity.week`/`versions` eager load (page), that the reassignment case fails without `current()` in the list scope and that the four 403 runs fail without the controller check. Recovery requests: `CoachRecoveryRequestPageService::indexData(User, array)` lists the pending requests whose attempt the viewer currently mentors in its session (before, any session where they mentored the participant, so a request from another session was listed but deciding it was a 403), filters by `session` through the new `FilterRecoveryRequestsRequest` (coach or admin; same `session` rule), shows each request's session and gains a filter card (Training session select, Filter, Clear while filtered; empty text "No pending recovery requests in this training session." while filtered). **Review page:** the plan names it in the title and 10.4 listed its read-only view by any coach as left for 10.6, and MEN-4 says a mentor *sees* only their current participants, as 10.5 (participant page) and 10.7 (Gate review) do, so `SubmissionVersionPolicy::view` is now `update` (the submitting participant, an admin or the current mentor in the submission's session) and `coach.reviews.show` is a 403 for any other coach, including a previous mentor and a mentor of the participant in another session; `CoachSubmissionReviewPageService::showData()` no longer takes the viewer or returns `canManage` (`canClaim` / `canRetry` follow the submission only), and `coach/review` always shows Save Review and the form (the "Read only — this participant is not assigned to you." notice is gone). Checked that the new cases fail without the scope on the queue or on the recovery page, without `current()` or the session condition in the scope and with the old `view` policy, and that the two query-count cases fail without the new session eager loads. The "Assigned to me" checkbox and the "No training program is active yet." notice are gone; the filter form gains "Training session" (`CoachSessionFilterService::options()`) and, for admins only, "Mentor" (table now `min-w-280`). `FilterScoringParticipantsRequest` drops `assigned_to_me` and gains `session` (`Rule::exists(TrainingSession::class, 'id')`) and `mentor` (`Rule::exists(User::class, 'id')->where('role', UserRole::Coach)`). **Participant page:** route `coach.scoring.participant` (name kept) is now `GET coach/scoring/sessions/{trainingSession}/participants/{participant}` with `scopeBindings()` (404 for someone not in the session), `ScoringController::participant()` answers 403 unless `mentorsParticipantInSession()` (as 10.5's participant page), and `ScoringPageService::participantData(TrainingSession, User)` summarizes only that session's work; the header adds "Training session: Title (dates)" and a session without weeks says "This training session has no weeks to score yet." instead of the active-program notice. `ScoringController::store()` redirects to the submission's session page. **Scoring page:** 10.4 listed the scoring page "(still read only for any coach)" as left for 10.8 and 10.6 / 10.7 made the review and Gate review pages a 403, so `coach.scoring.edit` now runs `Gate::authorize('view', $submissionVersion)` (`SubmissionVersionPolicy::view`: an admin or the current mentor in the submission's session) and a previous mentor, a mentor of the participant in another session and any other coach get 403; ## Phase 11 — Week schedule and unlocking

> **History.** This phase built the training-session model that `docs/plans/curriculum-sessions-merge.md` superseded (2026-10-28). Its batch notes describe the code as each batch left it; for the current design see that plan and Design above.

- [x] **11.1 Open weeks by date and lock them after an incomplete review** (WK-1, WK-2, WK-3, DEV-3) (was TS 6.1)
  - **Do:** Rewrite `WeekProgressionService` access rules for a session: week *N* is accessible to a participant when `today >= weekOpensOn(N)` and no earlier week of the session has a gate evaluation whose status is *incomplete* (see Assumptions); coaches and admins always have access. Return a state per week: `open`, `not_open` (with date), `locked` (with the blocking week number). "Current week" = the latest open, unlocked week of an active session; `requiresAttendanceForActivity()` uses it. Remove the gate-chain rule and `ensurePriorWeekThreeActivities()` if still present. Check the review statuses 7.3 left (Completed = `Passed`, Incomplete = `NeedsRevision`) and adjust the mapping if they changed.
  - **Acceptance:** In a session starting Mon 9 Nov, on 15 Nov week 1 is open and week 2 shows "not open"; on 16 Nov week 2 is open without week 1's gate being passed. After a mentor records week 1 *incomplete*, weeks 2 to 4 are locked even if open; submitting the missing work alone does not unlock; marking week 1 *completed* (by mentor or admin) unlocks the weeks whose date has come, and later weeks stay date-locked. Earlier weeks are never locked.
  - **Tests:** Rewrite the progression parts of `TrainingDomainTest` / `ParticipantWorkflowTest` with `travelTo`: date opening, lock after incomplete, no unlock on resubmission, unlock on completed (mentor and admin), date lock kept after unlock, earlier weeks open.
  - **Depends on:** Phase 10, 7.3.
  - **Done:** **Week states.** New `App\Enums\WeekAccessState` (`Open`, `NotOpen`, `Locked`; not stored) and value object `App\Support\SessionWeekState` (`weekNumber`, `access`, `opensOn`, `lockedByWeekNumber`, `passed`; `isOpen()`, `isNotOpen()`, `isLocked()`), for 11.2, 11.3, 11.6 to reuse. `WeekProgressionService` no longer has the Gate chain (`ensurePriorWeekThreeActivities()` was already gone with 2.5): `weekStates(User, TrainingSession)` (the session's weeks, keyed by week id in week order) and `weekStatesForMemberships()` (same signature, now `SessionWeekState` per **session** week instead of `array{passed, accessible}` per program week) share one rule computed with two queries for any number of memberships (the Gates of the weeks with the matching reviews eager loaded). Walking the session's weeks by `week_number`, the first week with an incomplete review on an active Gate locks every later week (`lockedByWeekNumber` = that earliest week); an unlocked week is `Open` from `weekOpensOn(N)` (`<= today()`, app timezone) and `NotOpen` before it. **A week that is both before its date and after an incomplete review is `Locked`** (the acceptance's "weeks 2 to 4 are locked"); its `opensOn` is still set, so 11.2 can say both. `passed` keeps the old rule (every Gate of the week has a `Passed` review in the session). **Review mapping** checked against 7.3: completed = `Passed`, incomplete = `NeedsRevision`, plus the older `Failed` reviews (new `GateEvaluationStatus::incompleteReviews()`, see Open decisions "Older `Failed` Gate reviews"). `canAccessWeek()`: coaches and admins always; a participant while the week is `Open`. `ensureCanAccessWeek()` 403s with "Week 2 opens on Mon 16 Nov." or "Complete your Week 1 deliverables to unlock Week 2." (11.2 owns the final wording). New `currentWeekId(TrainingSession, $weekStates)` = the latest open week of an active session (null otherwise); `isCurrentWeek()` (and so `requiresAttendanceForActivity()` / `requiresAttendanceForNewActivity()`) and the session page's `is_current` / `attendance_required` (`ParticipantDashboardService::weeks()`) use it, so the page and the server ask for a check-in on the same week. `hasCompletedSession()` and `completedMemberships()` are unchanged in behavior (the latter now reads the shared query). **Callers:** `ParticipantDashboardService`, `ParticipantNotificationService::openWeeksBySession()` and `MissingSubmissionReportService` read the value object (`isOpen()`, `passed`) with unchanged conditions (`passed || open` on the page and in notifications, open only in the report); every controller and `StoreWeeklyVideoRecordingRequest` keep calling `ensureCanAccessWeek()` / `ensureCanAccessActivity()`. **UI copy (minimal, 11.2 owns it):** a closed week's panel says "Locked — Week 2 opens on Mon 16 Nov." or "Locked — Your mentor must mark Week 1 completed before you can continue." (was "Your mentor must mark the previous week completed …"), and the Overview's "Gates & progression" line says "Each week includes required activities and evidence, and opens on its date. If your mentor marks a week incomplete, the later weeks stay locked until they mark it completed." **Factory:** `TrainingSessionFactory::forWeek()` now starts the session `week_number − 1` weeks ago, so the given week (and every earlier one) has opened today and the session is still active (before: started today, which with date opening closed every week after Week 1; most fixtures use the factory's Week 2). **Tests:** `ParticipantWorkflowTest` (session from Mon 9 Nov 2026, four weeks with one activity and a one-item checklist each, a mentor; reviews posted through `training.coach.gates.update`): weeks open on their dates without earlier reviews (8 Nov Week 1 closed with its date, 15 Nov Week 2 closed and its draft refused, 16 Nov open with no review), an incomplete Week 2 review locks Weeks 3 and 4 although open (page and draft 403 with the message) while Weeks 1 and 2 stay open and the mentor still opens Week 4, submitting the missing Week 1 work leaves Week 2 locked, completing the week (mentor and admin, data provider) unlocks Week 2 while Week 3 keeps its date until 23 Nov, an older `failed` review locks, and the current week (latest open unlocked week) is the only one asking for a check-in, moving back to Week 1 after its incomplete review; the gate-chain tests (`test_week_three_and_four_are_server_locked_until_prior_gate_evaluations_pass` and the two `incompleteStatuses` data-provider tests) are replaced, and the Week 3 / Week 4 submission tests travel to their week's date instead of passing Week 2's Gate. `TrainingDomainTest` had no progression cases. `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageTest`: the locked-panel test now covers the date reason (15 Nov) and a new test the review lock (16 Nov), and the tab test travels to Week 2's date to keep Week 2 current. Fixtures that relied on the Gate chain or an upcoming session's open Week 1 were updated to the new rule without changing what they assert: `MissingSubmissionReportPageTest::enrollParticipants()` and `ParticipantNotificationsTest::joinSession()` create a session in which every week has opened, `ParticipantNotificationsTest` travels to Week 3's date for the video-reminder case and uses two active sessions in the two-session case, `SessionSubmissionTest`'s second session is active instead of upcoming, `TrainingWeekPageTest`'s video case uses a session in which Week 3 has opened, and `HomeNavigationTest` asserts the new Overview line.

- [x] **11.2 Explain locked and unopened weeks on their tabs** (WK-4) (was TS 6.2)
  - **Do:** On the session details page every session week keeps its tab (title visible, `locked` state icon). An unopened week's panel shows "Opens on Mon 16 Nov" (format `D j M`); a review-locked week shows "Complete your Week 1 deliverables to unlock" instead of its contents. Week, activity and submission URLs of such weeks return 403 for participants with the same message.
  - **Acceptance:** Tabs and messages match the cases in 11.1; the earlier weeks' contents remain reachable.
  - **Tests:** Extend `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageTest` and `TrainingSessionAccessTest` with `travelTo`.
  - **Depends on:** 11.1.
  - **Done:** **Message.** New `SessionWeekState::lockedMessage()` is the single source of the text: "Opens on Mon 16 Nov" (`D j M`) for a `NotOpen` week, "Complete your Week 1 deliverables to unlock" for a `Locked` one (the week named by `lockedByWeekNumber`, even when its date has not come either), null while open. `ensureCanAccessWeek()` throws it as the 403 message (the 11.1 interim "Week 2 opens on Mon 16 Nov." / "… to unlock Week 2." are gone; a role or week without a state keeps "This training week is not available to your account."). `ActivitySubmissionController@show` (`training.submission.show`, a handed-in version) now calls `ensureCanAccessWeek()` after its 404 checks, so every week, activity and submission URL of a closed week 403s with the message (the other submission routes, the export and the video routes already did). **Session page.** `ParticipantDashboardService::weeks()`: `available = isOpen()` (was `passed || open`, resolving the Open decision for the page) and `locked_message` replaces `locked_reason` (its private `lockedReason()` is gone); the tab state is `locked` for every week that is not available, before `passed` and `current`, so each session week keeps its tab with the lock icon (`aria-hidden` emoji plus "(Locked)" screen-reader text, unchanged in `x-ui.tab-list`). A closed week's panel keeps its eyebrow, title and description and shows the new partial `resources/views/training-sessions/locked-week.blade.php` (`x-ui.card` with an `aria-hidden` lock, sr-only "Locked: " and the message; `data-locked-week`; 11.3 adds its list and the waiting variant there) instead of the check-in alert, activities, Gate card and video row; the old "Locked — …" alert is gone and the "Completed" alert shows only on an open week. Earlier open weeks keep their contents and links. **Tests:** `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageTest`: the unopened-week test (15 Nov) asserts the tab's lock icon and "(Locked)", Week 1's link, Week 2's title and "Opens on Mon 16 Nov" in its panel and no Week 2 activity or links; the review-lock test (16 Nov) asserts "Complete your Week 1 deliverables to unlock", no Week 2 contents and Week 1 selected; new `test_a_completed_week_locked_by_an_earlier_incomplete_review_shows_its_lock_instead_of_completed`; the video-row test's session now starts at Week 3 (it relied on completed weeks before their date being available). `TrainingSessionAccessTest`: new data-provider test (not opened yet on 15 Nov; locked by an incomplete Week 1 review on 16 Nov) asserting 403 with the message on Week 2's week, activity, submission edit and handed-in version pages while Week 1's week and activity pages open. `ParticipantWorkflowTest`'s 403 assertions moved to the new messages. Full suite 1320 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. Not checked in a browser (no phone-width visual check of the locked panel).

- [x] **11.3 List the missing deliverables and show when they await verification** (WK-4) (was TS 6.3)
  - **Do:** Under the locked message, list the incomplete review's required checklist items marked not met (titles), each linking to the incomplete week (its tab on the session page, where its activities are). Put the "has new work since the review" check in `WeekProgressionService`, so 11.5 reuses it: at least one activity version of that week, in that session, by that participant, with `submitted_at` after the review's `evaluated_at` (Q2). When it holds, replace the message and list with "Waiting for your mentor to verify Week 1".
  - **Acceptance:** After an incomplete review of week 1 with two unmet items, a later week's tab shows "Complete your Week 1 deliverables to unlock" and both item titles linking to week 1. After the participant submits any week 1 activity, it shows "Waiting for your mentor to verify Week 1" and the later weeks stay locked (WK-3). A submission made *before* the review, or in another week or session, does not change the message. A new incomplete review brings the list back.
  - **Tests:** Extend `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageTest`: missing list, waiting after one submission, earlier/other-week/other-session submissions ignored, re-review resets.
  - **Depends on:** 11.2.
  - **Done:** **New-work check.** New public `WeekProgressionService::latestWorkSinceReviews(Collection $reviews)` (incomplete `GateEvaluation`s with their Gate loaded) returns, keyed by review id, the latest `submitted_at` of an activity version the review's participant handed in after the review's `evaluated_at` in an activity of the review's week, in the review's session (Q2: any activity of the week; drafts have no `submitted_at` and never count; a review without `evaluated_at`, which only factories create, never counts). One grouped query (versions joined to attempts and activities, `max(submitted_at)` per session, participant and week) for any number of reviews, so 11.5 can pass its mentees' incomplete reviews in bulk and show the latest submission time. **Week states.** The locking review (walking the session's weeks in order, the first incomplete review of an active Gate, the same rule as 11.1) is now found by a private `lockingReview()`; `weekStates()` and `weekStatesForMemberships()` run the check for the locking reviews of every membership at once, so they take two queries plus a third only when some week is locked. `SessionWeekState` gains `lockingReviewId` and `awaitingVerification` (both set only on locked weeks), and `lockedMessage()` says "Waiting for your mentor to verify Week 1" for a locked week that awaits verification. **403 decision:** week, activity and submission URLs of a locked week 403 with the same text as the tab, so they also switch to "Waiting for your mentor to verify Week 1" (11.2's "same message" rule; `ensureCanAccessWeek()` already throws `lockedMessage()`). **Session page.** `ParticipantDashboardService::weeks()` rows add `locked_by_week_number` and `missing_items` (titles of the locking review's required checklist items with a `NotMet` criterion evaluation, in position order; one query, run only while a week is locked and not awaiting verification; empty on other weeks); `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageService::showData()` adds `missing_items_url` (`weekTabUrl()` of the locking week, e.g. `/training/sessions/1#week-1`). `training-sessions/locked-week` shows under the message "Not met in your Week 1 review:" and a `list-disc` list of the titles, each a link to that tab (`data-missing-items`); while waiting, only the waiting message shows. `initializePageTabs()` in `resources/js/app.js` now also selects and focuses the tab on `hashchange`, so a same-page link to `#week-1` switches tabs (before, the hash was read only on load). A new incomplete review (same row, `GateReviewService::record()` sets a new `evaluated_at` and rewrites the item statuses) brings the list back with its new missing items. **Tests:** `ParticipantTrainingSessionPageTest`: the missing list (two unmet items linking to `#week-1`, the met item absent), waiting after one Week 1 submission after the review (week still locked, no list), a data-provider test where work handed in one second before the review, a draft saved after it, a submission in Week 2 and one in another session of the same program leave the list, a re-review posted by an admin through `training.coach.gates.update` after new work brings back the list with only its unmet item, and a query-count test for locked weeks (1 vs 3 locked weeks and missing items). `TrainingSessionAccessTest`'s closed-week data provider gains a "waiting for the mentor to verify" case (403 with the waiting message on the four Week 2 URLs). Full suite 1329 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. Not checked in a browser: the hash-link tab switch and the locked panel at phone width.

The message is `TrainingSession::ENDED_MESSAGE` ("This training session has ended."). `WeekProgressionService::ensureSessionAcceptsWork(User, TrainingSession, ?Activity)` throws it as a 403 (`AuthorizationException`, like the week messages), reading the participant's latest attempt in the session only when the session is completed; `ensureCanEditActivity()` calls it before the check-in rule, so every activity write path that already called it refuses the same way. **Viewing.** `SessionWeekState` gains `sessionCompleted` and `isViewable()` (open, or any week once the session has ended); `canAccessWeek()` and `ensureCanAccessWeek()` use it, so week, activity, handed-in version, export and video pages open for every week of a completed session (no date or review lock), while `access` and `lockedMessage()` keep their values, so notifications (completed sessions are already skipped), the missing submissions report (open weeks only) and `currentWeekId()` (null outside an active session) are unchanged. The session page's `available` is `isViewable()`: a review-locked week of a completed session shows its contents, its `locked_message` / `locked_by_week_number` are null and the missing-items query does not run for it. Resources already list every link (9.6 interim), so 11.6 has nothing to add for completed sessions. **Write paths, per path:** draft save (`training.submission.draft`, also the JSON autosave), the submission form (`training.submission.edit`, a GET: it is the work form, so it 403s too unless the attempt is reopened), submit, revise and evidence upload (`training.evidence.store`) go through `ensureCanEditActivity()`; the weekly video choice (`StoreWeeklyVideoRecordingRequest::authorize()`, after the week check and before the 422 Gate lock) and new recovery requests (`StoreRecoveryRequestRequest::authorize()`, for the failed attempt's session: an approved request would open a retry that cannot be worked on) call `ensureSessionAcceptsWork()` without an activity. There is no evidence delete or video remove route. **Left writable on purpose:** attendance check-in and check-out (global, not per session; Assumptions), the repository URL (`training.sessions.repository.update`: membership data, not work, and the mentor needs it to review a reopened activity), the activity export and the participant-wide Google Drive sync (they copy existing work, they do not change it), and reading notifications. Mentors' and admins' review, retry, recovery decision and Gate review are unchanged (see Open decisions). **Buttons:** the session page shows "This training session has ended. (`data-session-ended`), drops Choose Video / Change Video, and labels an activity's button "View" unless its latest attempt was reopened; the week page has no Choose Weekly Video main action and no Change Video (`changeVideoUrl` null); the video page replaces the Picker with "This training session has ended."; the activity page drops its main action (Start / Continue submission) and a draft's Continue Attempt unless the attempt is reopened, says "This training session has ended. No existing test needed a change. Full suite 1334 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. Not checked in a browser: the ended notices and the pages without their write buttons at phone width.
