## Phase 12 — Session overview and admin dashboard

> **History.** This phase built the training-session model that `docs/plans/curriculum-sessions-merge.md` superseded (2026-10-28). Its batch notes describe the code as each batch left it; for the current design see that plan and Design above.

- [x] **12.1 Fill the Overview tab** (ADM-2) (was TS 7.1)
  - **Do:** `AdminTrainingSessionPageService` overview stats for the session using `x-ui.stat`: participants, mentors, unassigned participants, participants with work per week, awaiting review, needs revision, pending gates, participants with locked weeks. Counts in a fixed number of queries.
  - **Acceptance:** Counts include only the session's participants and records; another session's submissions do not change them.
  - **Tests:** Extend `AdminTrainingSessionDashboardTest` with two sessions.
  - **Depends on:** Phase 11.
  - **Done:** **Counts.** `AdminTrainingSessionPageService::showData()` adds `overviewStats` (label, value, note) and `weekWorkStats` (one per session week), each counting the session's **current** participants (the Participants tab's rows) and their records **in this session** only, so another session's work and a removed participant's work (which keeps its session) never count: **Participants** = the memberships (the Participants tab count); **Mentors** = the session's mentors (the Mentors tab count); **Unassigned** = participants without a current mentor assignment in the session (from the assignments the page already reads, no query); **Awaiting review** = their attempts (`activity_submissions` rows) in the session with a status in `SubmissionStatus::awaitingReview()` (submitted, resubmitted, under review), the list the review queue uses; **Needs revision** = their attempts in the session in `NeedsRevision` (both count attempts, as the review queue does; since a retry opens only after a failed attempt, every earlier attempt is `Failed`, so these are their latest attempts); **Pending gates** = their saved Gate reviews in the session whose status is not `Passed` (today `NeedsRevision` and older `Failed`, plus any legacy `NotReady` / `AwaitingReview` rows), the admin dashboard's global PENDING GATES scoped to the session; **Participants with locked weeks** = participants with at least one session week in the `Locked` state of `WeekProgressionService::weekStatesForMemberships()` (11.1's rule: an incomplete review of an earlier week; an incomplete review of the session's last week locks nothing and does not count; the memberships are the participants' pivots with the session set, so no membership query); **Week N** ("Participants who handed in work") = distinct participants with at least one attempt in the session at an activity of that week (any activity, as 11.3) that was handed in at least once (`activity_submissions.submitted_at` set, which submitting sets together with the version's; a draft never handed in does not count). **Queries:** the session's weeks are now read once with their active activities (`sessionWeeks()`, the two queries `activityRows()` already ran, which now takes the weeks) and shared by the Activities tab, the week counts and the week states; the overview adds three count queries, one grouped query for the week counts and two (three when a week is locked) for the week states, none of them without participants. **View:** the Overview tab keeps its facts and adds "Participants and reviews" (an `x-ui.stat variant="field"` grid with notes such as "Activity attempts waiting for a mentor"; `data-overview-stats`) and "Participants who handed in work" (one stat per week, or "This session has no weeks."; `data-week-work-stats`). The stats are not links: the review queue still counts removed participants' work (see Open decisions). **Tests:** `AdminTrainingSessionDashboardTest` (8 cases, was 6): new `test_the_overview_counts_only_the_current_participants_and_their_records_in_this_session` (two sessions of one program on 17 Nov 2026: four members, two mentors, an ended and two current assignments; attempts in each status, a draft never handed in, Gate reviews completed, incomplete on Week 1, older failed on Week 1 and incomplete on the last week; a removed participant's attempts and review in the session and a shared member's and another member's attempts and review in the other session; expects 4, 2, 2, 3, 2, 3, 2 and Week 1: 3, Week 2: 1) and `test_the_overview_of_a_session_without_participants_counts_zero` (another session's work present); the query-count case now also gives each participant an attempt awaiting review, one needing revision, an incomplete Week 1 review that locks Week 2 and an attempt in another session, and asserts the week counts (renamed `test_query_count_does_not_grow_with_participants_mentors_assignments_activities_and_their_work`). Checked that the counts case fails without the session condition on the attempts or the week counts, without the membership condition, without the handed-in condition, without the `Passed` exclusion and when every membership counts as locked. Full suite 1348 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (new stat grids). Not checked in a browser: the Overview tab at phone width.

- [x] **12.2 Fill the Activities tab** (ADM-2) (was TS 7.2)
  - **Do:** Per activity of the session's weeks: week, title, and counts among the session's participants of not started, awaiting review, needs revision, approved; link to the review queue filtered by session.
  - **Acceptance:** Counts match the session's submissions; the tab label shows the number of activities.
  - **Tests:** Extend `AdminTrainingSessionDashboardTest`.
  - **Depends on:** 12.1.
  - **Done:** **Counts.** `AdminTrainingSessionPageService::activityRows()` now takes the session and its participants and gives each active activity of the session's weeks (the weeks 12.1 reads once) a `counts` array over the session's **current** participants (the Participants tab's rows, as in 12.1; a removed participant's work keeps the session but is not counted), each participant placed by their **latest attempt at the activity in this session** (highest `attempt_number`, then highest ID, so a participant falls in exactly one bucket and the six counts add up to the participant count): **Not started** = no attempt in this session (attempts in another session do not count); **In progress** = latest attempt `Draft`, that is a first draft never handed in, a revision of a version sent back (saving a revision returns the attempt to `Draft`, with `submitted_at` still set) or a retry a mentor opened after a failed attempt; **Awaiting review** = `SubmissionStatus::awaitingReview()` (submitted, resubmitted, under review); **Needs revision** = `NeedsRevision`; **Approved** = `SubmissionStatus::accepted()` (approved, legacy completed); **Failed** = `Failed` with no retry opened yet (once a retry exists the participant moves to the retry's bucket). The bucket comes from `attemptCountKey()`, a `match` over those enum lists with no default. Since a retry opens only after a failure, the Awaiting review and Needs revision columns summed over the activities equal the Overview's attempt counts. **Query:** one grouped query (`latestAttemptCountsByActivity()`: the session's attempts by its current participants at the listed activities with no later attempt, via `whereNotExists` on `activity_submissions as later_attempts`, grouped by activity and status), none without participants or activities; the dashboard grows from 12.1's count by that one query. **View:** the Activities table (`min-w-220`, in the existing scroll region) adds the six count columns (labels from `activityCountColumns`, cells `data-activity-count="{key}"`) and a "Reviews" column whose "Review queue" link (aria-label "Open the review queue for {title}") goes to `coach.reviews?session={id}&activity={activityId}`: the queue's `activity` filter (10.6's `FilterReviewQueueRequest`, nullable integer) matches any activity ID, although its select offers only the active program's activities (see Open decisions). A line above the table explains the counts. The tab label keeps the number of activities ("Activities (3)"). The queue still lists a removed participant's work, so the link can show more than the row counts (Open decisions, updated). **Tests:** `AdminTrainingSessionDashboardTest` (9 cases, was 8): new `test_the_activities_tab_counts_each_current_participants_latest_attempt_in_this_session` (five members, a removed participant with attempts in the session and two members plus an outsider with attempts in another session; failed then approved retry → Approved, failed then retry draft → In progress, failed alone → Failed, a revision draft → In progress, legacy completed → Approved, no attempt → Not started; expects 1/1/1/0/1/1, 1/1/1/1/1/0 and 5/0/0/0/0/0, the column headers, the tab label and the review queue links); the zero-participants case asserts all-zero counts; the query-count case also gives each participant a failed attempt and a retry draft and asserts the In progress and Awaiting review totals; the `attempt()` helper takes an optional attempt number. Checked that the new case fails without the later-attempt condition, without the session condition (outer or in the subquery), without the membership condition and when drafts are bucketed elsewhere. Not covered by a test: the same-attempt-number tie-break (it needs duplicate attempt numbers, which only a race on the first draft could create). Full suite 1349 tests, exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (new table columns and link). Not checked in a browser: the Activities table at phone width.

## Phase 13 — When ready (run by name when the gate opens; see Rules)

- [x] **13.1 Replace the old migrations with fresh hand-written ones** (maintenance, user request 2026-10-23) — **Gate D** (was DP 3.5)
  - **When:** right after a production deploy that has run every migration, and before the next batch that adds a migration is merged. Earliest point: after Phase 4.
  - **Precondition (record it here before starting):** every database that will run this code has applied every migration file present on `main` when the batch starts, including 2.1 (QA data deletion) and the table drops in 2.2 to 2.10. That means the release containing them has been deployed and migrated on **production and staging** (deploying `main` is currently blocked by the code quality plan's Phase 7 "Production values" check, so this waits for that too). A reviewer checks it by running, from the currently live release on each environment, `php artisan migrate:status --no-ansi` (no `Pending` line) and comparing its "Ran" list with the files in `database/migrations` at the batch's starting commit (same names, same count). Paste the date, environment, commit and result under this item.
  - **Do:**

Framework tables included (Assumptions). Simple `down()` that drops the table. No data inserts, no model or seeder imports, no `DB::statement` unless a reference index cannot be expressed otherwise (note it).

    - **Add `App\Console\Commands\AdoptSquashedMigrationsCommand`** (`app:adopt-squashed-migrations`, options `--pretend`, `--force` required outside local) holding two constants: the replaced migration names and the new ones. In a transaction it: refuses (exit 1, no change) unless every replaced name is recorded in `migrations` and no row outside both lists exists; does nothing ("Already adopted.") when every new name is recorded and no replaced name is; otherwise deletes the replaced rows and inserts the new names with `batch = max(batch) + 1`. `--pretend` prints the rows it would delete and insert. It never runs a migration.
    - **Document the one-time release step** in `docs/ATOMIC_DEPLOYMENT.md`, section "One-time: adopting the squashed migrations", fitting the existing flow: (1) confirm the Precondition on that environment; (2) run `atomic-release.sh prepare` for the squash release, which stops at "unexpected pending migrations" after extracting and installing the release (expected: the new files are not recorded yet); (3) from that release directory run `php artisan app:adopt-squashed-migrations --pretend`, check the output, then run it with `--force`; (4) re-run `prepare` (it re-extracts and now passes the pending-migration check); (5) `activate` as usual; (6) never run `php artisan migrate` from a pre-squash release afterwards. ## Out of scope / follow-ups

- The AI checker for GitHub deliverables, private repositories, the VS Code session-log hooks and any automatic session-log check (DP spec Out of scope).
- Program, week and activity editing UI; content for other job roles; bringing back mock interviews or an asset library (both specs' Out of scope).
- Leaving a session as a participant, deleting sessions, certificates, per-session reports beyond the scoped exports; showing the participant's current mentor on their session page; mentor change notifications (TS spec Out of scope).
- Root-level QA artefacts not used by the app: `QA_LAB_MASTER_PLAN.md`, `QA_Training_Working_Template.xlsx`, `training-plans/*.pptx`, `training-apps/membership-app`. Delete or archive by hand if the user wants.
- A bulk admin export of all uploads (DAT-1 relies on an ops archive instead).
- Linking checklist items to individual activities, which would let "Waiting for your mentor" track the actual missing deliverable (Q2 trade-off).
- Switching the existing inline audit writers (`AdminUserController`, `AdminAiSettingsController`, `AdminGoogleDriveController`, `BootstrapAdminCommand`) to `AdminAuditService`.
- Searchable participant/mentor pickers instead of selects for large user lists.
- ~~Deployment note: PHP `upload_max_filesize` / `post_max_size` must allow 100 MB resource uploads; record it in the release checklist when 8.6 ships.~~ No longer needed: resources are links only (8.9). The evidence limits still need PHP limits above them (see Open decisions); the weekly video no longer uploads (8.12).
- Checking from the app whether a Drive resource is shared correctly (needs a broader Google scope or a service account; the app has `drive.file`).
- Previews for links other than Google Drive, Docs, Slides and Sheets (e.g. embedded YouTube players, Drive folders, Google Forms); the spec gives them an "Open" link only.
- Editing a resource's link in place (the spec asks for add, rename and remove).
- Deleting `AdoptSquashedMigrationsCommand` once production, staging and every shared database have adopted the squashed migrations.
- Zoho Cliq and email integration for notifications (user request 2026-10-26). Today participant notifications are in-app only (`notifications:sync-participants`); the app has no `Mailable` or `Notification` classes, and mail is used only by the admin settings test email. Needs a spec first: which events notify whom, on which channel, and whether users can opt out.

## Open decisions

Problems found during implementation that need a decision. Starts empty.

- **Users created with only `name` after 1.1 have no first or last name** (found in 1.1) — **Resolved in 1.2:** the `User` `saving` hook fills `first_name` / `last_name` by splitting `name` at the first space when both are blank, so `BootstrapAdminCommand`, `DevelopmentAccountProvisioner` and any other `name`-only create are covered; the 1.1 migration keeps its own copy of the split because old migrations are not edited. ~~The backfill runs once, in the migration. `BootstrapAdminCommand` and `DevelopmentAccountProvisioner` still create users with `name` only, as do registration and admin create until 1.2 and 1.4, so those users have null `first_name` / `last_name` and the 1.3 profile form and 1.4 admin form would show them empty. 4.3 rewrites the demo accounts; `BootstrapAdminCommand` is in no batch. Decide whether 1.3/1.4 should fall back to splitting `name` when both are null, or whether `BootstrapAdminCommand` gets first/last names in a batch.~~
- **One-word-name users must add a last name to save their profile** (found in 1.3). The profile requires `first_name` and `last_name`, the same as registration, but the 1.1 backfill and the `User` `saving` hook leave the last name empty for a one-word `name` (e.g. "Prince"). Such a user sees an empty Last name field and "The last name field is required." until they fill it in. Decide whether the profile (and 1.4's admin form) should make `last_name` optional. 1.4 keeps it required on the admin form too; the same admin form error applies when an admin edits such a user. **Decided 2026-10-23:** first and last name stay required everywhere; one-word-name users add a last name the next time they save.
- **`UserIdentityTest` loads the 1.1 migration by file name** (found in 1.1). Its backfill case requires `database/migrations/2026_10_23_051317_add_identity_fields_to_users_table.php` and runs its `down()` and `up()`. 13.1 replaces that file, so 13.1 must delete that test case (the backfill only matters before the squash) or point it at the adoption command. **Resolved in 13.1:** the backfill case and the other two tests' `down()`/`up()` cases were deleted with their migrations; the schema checks (`url` not null and `added_by` null on delete; `drive_file_id` not null) stay in their files and now check the squashed tables. 8.9's `TurnTrainingSessionResourcesIntoLinksMigrationTest` likewise requires `database/migrations/2026_10_23_173403_turn_training_session_resources_into_links.php` and runs its `down()` and `up()`; 13.1 deletes that test with the migration (its schema checks, `url` not null and `added_by` null on delete, can move to a test of the squashed table). 8.12's `StoreWeeklyVideosAsGoogleDriveFilesMigrationTest` does the same with `database/migrations/2026_10_23_192547_store_weekly_videos_as_google_drive_files.php`; 13.1 deletes it with the migration (its `drive_file_id` not-null check can move to a test of the squashed table).
- **Admins can clear an existing employee ID** (found in 1.4). The admin edit form keeps `employee_id` nullable (so legacy users without one can still be saved), which also lets an admin blank out an ID a user already has; after 1.5 that user would be prompted for it at next login. Decide whether the edit form should require the ID once it is set. **Decided 2026-10-23:** required once set; scheduled as 1.7.
- **`AdminUserController` is 160 lines** (found in 1.4), over the ~150-line target. **Resolved in 5.2:** removing `mapAccessType()` brought it to 142 lines; the last-active-admin checks stay in the controller. ~~The last-active-admin checks and the access-type mapping still live in the controller; moving them to a service would bring it back under.~~
- **Seeded and bootstrapped accounts have no employee ID** (found in 1.5). `DatabaseSeeder` (`test@example.com`), `DevelopmentAccountProvisioner` (`coach.dev@example.test`, `admin.dev@example.test`) and `BootstrapAdminCommand` create users without one, so each stops at the employee ID prompt on first sign-in. 4.3 rewrites the demo accounts with IDs; decide whether `test@example.com` and `BootstrapAdminCommand` should set one too (then `DevelopmentAccountProvisioningTest` no longer needs its `seedAccountsWithEmployeeIds()` helper). **Decided 2026-10-23:** every seeded account gets the identity fields and two demo participants are added, scheduled as 1.8; `BootstrapAdminCommand` is unchanged (the real admin enters their own ID at the prompt).
- **The profile's "Not set" employee ID label is unreachable for its owner** (found in 1.5). A user without an employee ID is sent to the prompt before the profile, so `ProfilePageService::showData()`'s `employeeIdLabel` fallback never renders. Decide whether to drop it.
- **The at-risk CSV has no Employee ID column** (found in 1.6). `coach.reports.export?type=risk` (`at-risk.csv`, built in `CoachReportService::csvExport()`) lists participants with Participant, Path and Reason (Participant and Reason since 5.3), but 1.6 named only the progress, review and Gate types of that export. Decide whether it gets the column too. **Decided 2026-10-23:** left as is.
- **`DeleteQaTrainingDataMigrationTest` writes to tables that later batches drop** (found in 2.1). Its `seedTrainingRecords()` helper inserts one row into every training table, including ~~`validation_submissions` (dropped in 2.2)~~ (removed in 2.2, with the Lab evidence file and `submission_versions.validation_submission_id`), ~~`daily_logs` (2.4)~~ (removed in 2.4), ~~the Week 3 tables (2.5)~~ (removed in 2.5), ~~the Week 4 peer interview tables (2.6)~~ (removed in 2.6, with the helper's partner user), ~~the Week 4 mentor booking tables (2.7)~~ (removed in 2.7), and its `TRAINING_TABLES` list asserts they are empty. Each of those batches must remove the matching inserts and list entries (the migration already skips missing tables). ~~It also seeds `QaTrainingProgramSeeder`, so 4.2 must move it to a factory-built program named *AI-Assisted QA Level 3*,~~ (moved in 4.2) and like `UserIdentityTest` it loads its migration by file name, so 13.1 deletes it with the migration. **Resolved in 13.1:** deleted with the migration. 8.12 changed its weekly video insert to the Drive shape (`drive_file_id`, `name`) and dropped its video file assertions, since `localFiles()` now skips a table without a `path` column.
- **2.1 leaves orphaned files and empty folders on the local disk** (found in 2.1). The migration deletes only files that `submission_evidence`, `weekly_video_recordings` and `validation_submissions.evidence_files` reference. Files no row references (e.g. leftovers from earlier failed uploads) and the emptied `evidence/`, `weekly-video/` and `validation-evidence/` folders stay in `storage/app/private`. Decide whether the release checklist should remove those folders by hand after taking the storage archive.
- **Audit rows written by a participant are deleted with them** (found in 2.1). `admin_audit_logs.actor_id` cascades on delete, so if an admin was later demoted to participant, the rows they wrote as admin go when 2.1 deletes every participant. Today only admins write audit rows, so this only affects a demoted admin. Decide whether to check production for such users before running 2.1.
- **Phase 2 migrations have only run on SQLite** (found in 2.1, 2.2, 2.4, 2.5, 2.6, 2.7). The suite runs each one on SQLite; none has run on MySQL. `migrate --pretend` is no help for 2.1 (`Schema::hasTable` returns false in pretend mode), and the app's database user cannot create a scratch MySQL database. The drops use the standard `dropForeign` / `dropColumn` / `dropIfExists`, and the foreign keys involved match the dev MySQL schema. **Decided 2026-10-23:** no production database exists yet (the app has not been deployed), so there is no production copy to test against and production runs them on an empty schema; the dev MySQL database runs them at its next `migrate`. Later batches add their migrations to this entry instead of opening a new one. 5.4's `2026_10_23_121319_drop_training_path_columns` ran up, down and up again on a throwaway SQLite file only; on MySQL its SQL (`migrate --pretend --path=…`) and the dev schema's indexes and `activity_id` foreign key were checked, and it creates the replacement `activity_requirements` index before dropping the one that backs that foreign key. 4.2's `migrate:fresh --seed` acceptance (edited `2026_09_10_160000_add_week3_activity_fields.php`, `L4TrainingProgramSeeder` in `DatabaseSeeder`) also ran on a throwaway SQLite file only, for the same reason; the developer database was not reset. 8.1's `2026_10_23_140801_create_training_sessions_table` and `2026_10_23_140802_create_training_session_participants_table` ran up, down and up again on a throwaway SQLite file; their MySQL SQL was checked with `migrate --pretend --path=…` only. 8.6's `2026_10_23_143612_create_training_session_resources_table` likewise ran up, down and up again on a throwaway SQLite file, and its MySQL SQL was checked with `migrate --pretend --path=…` only (MySQL drops the foreign key's implicit `training_session_id` index once the composite (`training_session_id`, `week_number`) index exists). 8.9's `2026_10_23_173403_turn_training_session_resources_into_links` likewise ran up, down and up again on a throwaway SQLite file; its MySQL SQL (`migrate --pretend --path=…`) adds `added_by` with its foreign key, drops `training_session_resources_uploaded_by_foreign`, drops the columns (MySQL drops the `uploaded_by` index with its column) and makes `url` not null with `modify`. Its `down()` was not checked on MySQL. 8.12's `2026_10_23_192547_store_weekly_videos_as_google_drive_files` likewise ran up, down and up again on a throwaway SQLite file; its MySQL SQL (`migrate --pretend --path=…`) selects the old `disk` / `path`, drops the table, creates it with the Design columns and adds the two cascading foreign keys and the (`user_id`, `training_week_id`) unique key. Its `down()` was not checked on MySQL, and it has not run on the dev MySQL database. 8.16's `2026_10_23_200206_add_sharing_to_weekly_video_recordings_table` likewise ran up, down and up again on a throwaway SQLite file; its MySQL SQL (`migrate --pretend --path=…`) is two `alter table weekly_video_recordings add … null after …` statements (`drive_permission_id` varchar(255) after `drive_resource_key`, `shared_at` timestamp after `chosen_at`). Its `down()` was not checked on MySQL, and it has not run on the dev MySQL database. **2026-10-23:** the four Phase 8 migrations (8.1 ×2, 8.6, 8.9) then ran `up` on the dev MySQL database without errors; the resulting `training_session_resources` columns are `id, training_session_id, week_number, title, url, added_by, created_at, updated_at`. Their `down()` methods have still not run on MySQL. 9.4's `2026_10_23_211821_require_training_session_on_training_records` ran up, down and up in the suite on SQLite (`RequireTrainingSessionOnTrainingRecordsMigrationTest` checks the restrict foreign key and the per-session keys survive the table rebuild); its MySQL SQL (`migrate --pretend --path=…`) is the three NULL checks and three `alter table … modify training_session_id bigint unsigned not null`. It has not run on MySQL (nor has 9.1's, which it follows), and its `down()` was not checked there. **2026-10-24:** the eight then-pending migrations ran `up` on the dev MySQL database without errors: 8.12's `…192547_store_weekly_videos_as_google_drive_files`, 8.16's `…200206_add_sharing_to_weekly_video_recordings_table`, 9.1's `…202323_add_training_session_to_training_records`, 9.4's `…211821_require_training_session_on_training_records`, 9.11's `…223926_add_repository_url_to_training_session_participants_table`, 10.1's `…232343_create_training_session_mentors_table` and `…232344_create_mentor_assignments_table`, and 10.4's `…234934_drop_coach_participant_assignments_table`. 9.1's migration first refused, as designed, because the dev database held one pre-session `gate_evaluations` row (and its four `gate_criterion_evaluations` rows); those were deleted with the user's approval and the run completed. None of their `down()` methods has run on MySQL. **2026-10-24 (13.1):** the squash replaced all of these files with one create migration per table, whose schema was compared with the dev MySQL database (see 13.1), so this entry no longer concerns new installs; the old files and their `down()` methods are in Git history only.
- **Local `.env` files may still set `QA_APPLICATION_URL`** (found in 2.2). Nothing reads it any more; developers can delete the line. The local `.env` was not edited.
- **The QA seeder's Week 2 Gate still has a daily-log criterion** (found in 2.3). `QaTrainingProgramSeeder` seeds the required criterion "Daily-log entries for all five sessions." (`criterion_type` `daily_logs`) and the deprecated `daily_log_reference` field, so a locally seeded Week 2 Gate review still lists that checklist item, although nothing checks logs any more. 2.1 deleted the QA data and 4.2 deletes the seeder, so this only affects local and test seeds; it was left as is because about 25 tests seed the QA program. Decide whether to drop the criterion before 4.2. **Decided 2026-10-23:** left as is; 4.2 deletes the seeder. **Resolved in 4.2:** the seeder is deleted.
- **Missing Submissions status filter and count columns are now redundant** (found in 2.3). Without the daily-log rows, every row is "Not Submitted" (activities, and since 8.14 the video rows too, which said "Not Uploaded"), so the Status filter's only option matches every row, and Expected / Submitted / Missing are always 1 / 0 / 1. Decide whether to drop the filter and the three columns (page and CSV).
- **A week workbook without activities is a header-only sheet** (found in 2.3). The Week 1 Drive workbook used to hold only the General Training daily logs; it is now built by `forWeek()` like every week, and a week with no active activities (Week 1 of the QA program) gets one "Activities" sheet with the header row only, because a workbook needs at least one sheet. Decide whether such a week should get no workbook instead (`SyncTrainingDataJob` would skip the component); 9.10 reworks Drive sync per session. **Decided 2026-10-23:** keep the header-only sheet.
- **The QA seeder still seeds the Week 4 peer interview fields** (found in 2.6). `QaTrainingProgramSeeder::configureWeekFourInterviewFields()` keeps the required `partner_user_id` field typed `participant_selector` (now rendered and validated as a plain text field labelled "Interview partner"), the required `interview_date`, `about_you` and `question_1_answer` to `question_3_answer` fields and the partner-feedback fields, and `weekFourInterviewQuestionFields()` still reads the deleted `training.week_four_interview_questions` key (it now gets an empty list, so the 15 questionnaire fields are no longer seeded). So a locally seeded Week 4 Activity 2 asks for a typed partner and those answers before submit. Nothing breaks and 4.2 deletes the seeder, so it was left as is; decide whether to drop those fields before 4.2. **Decided 2026-10-23:** left as is; 4.2 deletes the seeder. **Resolved in 4.2:** the seeder is deleted.
- **The `flatpickr` npm package is unused** (found in 2.7). Only the mentor interview `scheduled_at` pickers used it; 2.7 removed their initializer, the imports and the CSS overrides, but left `flatpickr` in `package.json` because dependency changes need approval. Decide whether to remove it (`npm uninstall flatpickr`). **Decided 2026-10-23:** removed.
- **Coach-owned Mock Interview listings stay on the public page** (found in 2.7). The removed Mentor Booking Calendar page let coaches save their own `Mock` listing in `interview_schedulers` (with `user_id`). Those rows stay (2.1 kept `interview_schedulers`) and still show on `/interviews`; coaches can no longer change or remove them, only Admins can under Interview Scheduling. Decide whether that is enough, or whether mentors need a way to manage their own listing again.
- **Local `.env` files may still set `TRAINING_TIMEZONE`** (found in 2.7). Nothing reads it any more; the training settings page shows `app.timezone` (`APP_TIMEZONE`). Both default to Asia/Manila, so the page only changes where the two were set differently. The local `.env` does not set it.
- **`asset` evidence now uploads with the general evidence rules** (found in 2.8). 2.8 deleted `EvidenceFilePolicy::reusableAssetRules()` / `reusableAssetAcceptAttribute()`, so an `asset` requirement (only the QA seeder's Week 4 Activity 1 "Reusable AI Asset") validates like `file` evidence. Compared with the old asset rules it now also accepts images and videos, rejects the `.markdown` extension (`.md` still works), needs a `.zip` / `.tar` upload's detected MIME type to be in `training.evidence_mimes` (the old `PK` / `ustar` signature fallback is gone), and fails with the standard validation messages instead of "Please upload a supported Reusable AI Asset file.". The size limit (`training.evidence_max_kb`) is unchanged. Decide whether that is fine for the L4 program or whether the `asset` evidence type should go (no L4 activity is expected to use it).
- **Evidence URL overrides only the library used** (found in 2.8). `SubmissionEvidenceFileService::viewResponse()`'s optional `$downloadUrl` and the `view-url` / `download-url` / `preview-url` / `modal-id` props of `x-evidence-actions` (covered by `EvidenceActionsComponentTest::test_passed_urls_and_modal_id_replace_the_evidence_routes`) have no callers now. They were kept because they are generic; decide whether to drop them or keep them for the 8.x resources pages. (8.9 makes session resources links rendered by `x-session-resource`, so the resources pages will not use them.)
- **Admin training settings still label limits "Reusable AI Asset"** (found in 2.8). `AdminSettingsPageService::trainingData()` shows "Reusable AI Asset application limit:" (the same value as the general evidence limit) and "Effective Reusable Asset limit:" (`min(evidence_max_kb, PHP limit)`, the only effective evidence limit on the page), from `AdminSettingsStatusService::training()` `asset_limit` / `effective_asset_limit`. Decide whether to drop the first row and rename the second to "Effective evidence limit:" (Phase 3 or 6). **Decided 2026-10-23:** drop the first row and rename the second; scheduled in 6.2. **Resolved in 6.2.**
- **The week page and dashboard offer the weekly video for every week** (found in 2.10) — **Resolved in 3.4:** the week page, the dashboard week panels and the Weekly Video Recording page and upload follow `TrainingWeek::asksForWeeklyVideo()` (an active Gate with a `weekly_video` item), as do the Missing Submissions report and notifications. ~~`TrainingWeekPageService` shows the Video Recording card and the "Upload Video Recording" main action, and the participant dashboard week panels show "Video Recording · Not uploaded" with Upload Video, for every week, although since 2.10 only a week whose Gate has a `weekly_video` criterion requires the video (the Missing Submissions report, notifications and Gate pages already follow it). 2.10 named only the missing-submission and notification surfaces. Decide whether these two show the video only for such weeks (the week page's main action would then fall to the Gate or nothing).~~ **Decided 2026-10-23:** the weekly video follows the week's checklist everywhere and is never hardcoded; scheduled as 3.4.
- **`gate_evidence` requirements are a leftover of the Weekly Loom** (found in 2.10). Readiness, `ParticipantNotificationService::missingEvidence()` and `SubmissionWorkflowService` still skip `activity_requirements` of type `gate_evidence`; only the legacy Loom link used it and nothing seeds it now. Decide whether to drop the type and the exclusions (Phase 3).
- **The weekly video statuses are still plain strings** (found in 2.10). `GateReadinessService::weeklyVideosForParticipants()` returns `not_required` / `missing` / `awaiting_review` / `met`, compared as strings in `CoachGateBoardPageService`, `CoachGateReviewPageService`, `ParticipantGatePageService` and `GateReviewController` (the other half of the 8t open decision in `docs/CODE_QUALITY_PLAN.md`). Decide whether they become an enum.
- **The participant score summary CSV still has fixed Week 2–4 columns** (found in 3.1). `ScoringReportController::participantSummary()` writes "Week 2 Average", "Week 3 Average" and "Week 4 Average" from `$flat[2]`..`$flat[4]`. Since 3.1 the completed / scored totals and the overall average count every active week, so a Week 1 (or Week 5) score counts in the totals but has no column of its own. Decide whether the CSV gets one "Week N Average" column per active week of the program (the columns would then vary by program; `ScoringReportControllerTest` asserts fixed positions).
- **Drive sync still hardcodes Weeks 1 to 4** (found in 3.1). `SyncTrainingDataJob::handle()` loops `foreach ([1, 2, 3, 4] as $week)`, creates only the `Evidences` folder for Week 1 and syncs evidence and videos only for weeks `>= 2`. The confirmation grep does not match it and 3.1 did not name the job. Decide whether it follows the program's active weeks (and syncs Week 1 evidence) before 9.10, which rewrites the sync per session. **Resolved in 9.10:** the sync follows each session's weeks (`TrainingSession::includesWeek()`), and every week, Week 1 included, syncs its evidence; the always-created empty `Week 1/Evidences` folder is gone.
- **The participant dashboard says "Start at Week 2."** (found in 3.1) — **Resolved in 9.5:** the landing page header no longer has the line, and the session page header shows the session's dates instead. ~~`participant/dashboard` prints "{name} · Start at Week 2." under the heading (5.3 removed the "{path} QA" part); decide whether the "Start at Week 2." text goes too (L4 starts at Week 1).~~
- **Two notions of the active program** (found in 3.1). `TrainingWeek::inActiveProgram()` / `CoachReportService::activeProgramWeeks()` use the first active program by id, while `ScoringPageService`, `ScoringReportController`, `MissingSubmissionReportService`, `ParticipantNotificationService` and the dashboards use `Program::where('is_active', true)->first()` (no order), and the Gate board lists active Gates of every program. With more than one active program the filter options, the coach participant page's Gates and the board can disagree. Decide whether only one program may be active, or whether these move to a single helper (Phase 9 scopes them by session). **Updated in 9.5:** the participant dashboard no longer reads the active program (it lists the participant's sessions). **Updated in 9.9:** `ParticipantNotificationService` no longer reads it either (it covers the participant's not-completed sessions). **Updated in 10.8:** `ScoringPageService` and `ScoringReportController` no longer read it for scores (they summarize each session's weeks); the scoring week filters and the scoring criteria page still use `TrainingWeek::inActiveProgram()`. **Updated in 10.9:** the coach dashboard and `MissingSubmissionReportService` no longer read it either (the dashboard counts the viewer's memberships of any program; the report uses each session's own program); the missing submissions week filter still uses `TrainingWeek::inActiveProgram()`. **Resolved in 12.3:** the admin dashboard no longer reads it either, so the unordered `Program::where('is_active', true)->first()` has no reader left and the one remaining notion is `TrainingWeek::inActiveProgram()` (first active program by id), used only for the week and activity filter options of the review queue (`CoachReportService::activeProgramWeeks()`), Gate board, scoring overview and reports, missing submissions report and for the scoring criteria page. Whether those options should come from the sessions instead is the entry "The review queue's week and activity filters still come from the active program".
- **`TrainingWeekFactory` numbers weeks from 2** (found in 3.1). Its default `week_number` is `fake()->unique()->numberBetween(2, 99)`, a QA leftover against GEN-2 (weeks start at 1). Tests that need Week 1 set it explicitly. Decide whether the default starts at 1.
- **The activity scores CSV still has fixed D1–D5 columns** (found in 3.2). `ScoringReportController::scores()` writes "D1".."D5" from the score's dimension rows, so a D6–D9 score is missing from `activity-scores.csv`. Decide whether it writes one column per key used by the exported scores (D1 up to the highest key), or D1–D9 always (`ScoringReportControllerTest` asserts fixed positions).
- **Nothing checks an activity's scoring dimensions** (found in 3.2). **Resolved in admin-curriculum-editing 2.7:** admins now change dimensions only through the activity page's Scoring tab, whose `UpdateActivityScoringRequest` accepts 1 to 9 rows (label up to 60 characters, maximum 1 to 100) whose maxima add up to 100, and `CurriculumWeekService::updateScoringDimensions()` keys them `D1`..`Dn` in row order. A seeder-side check stays out of scope, because the seeder now only creates a missing program (admin-curriculum-editing 1.1). ~~`Activity::scoringDimensions()` reads `configuration.scoring_dimensions` as stored: keys outside `D1`..`D9` (the column is `string(2)`), duplicate keys or maxima that do not sum to 100 (the bands, the "/ 100" on the submission, activity and scoring participant pages, and the second-reader score's `max:100` assume 100) are not rejected. Decide whether `L4TrainingProgramSeeder` (4.1, 13.2) throws on such a definition, as it does for an empty checklist.~~
- **Changing an activity's dimensions leaves old dimension rows on rescoring** (found in 3.2). **Resolved in admin-curriculum-editing 2.7** for admin edits: the Scoring tab becomes read-only, and a save is rejected with "Scored submissions exist, so the dimensions can no longer change.", once any `activity_scores` row (draft or published) exists for a version of the activity (`Activity::hasScores()`), so no scored version can meet changed dimensions. `ScoringController::store()` is unchanged (out of scope). ~~`ScoringController::store()` `updateOrCreate`s the submitted dimensions and never deletes the score's other rows, so after the activity's dimensions change (e.g. 13.2 replacing the placeholders) a rescored version keeps rows for removed keys: they are not in the total but the submission page still lists them (after the current ones). Decide whether storing a score deletes the dimension rows it did not submit (the history keeps the previous points).~~
- **The admin dashboard's Training overview still shows Weeks 2 to 4 only** (found in 3.3). `AdminDashboardPageService::showData()` builds the `WEEK2` / `WEEK3` / `WEEK4` stats from `participantsWithWeek($program, 2..4)`; 3.1's grep pattern does not match it, so a Week 1 (or Week 5) of the L4 program is never counted. 12.3 removes these stats; decide whether they list every active week of the program until then. **Resolved in 12.3:** the stats are gone; per-week counts are per session on the Overview tab (12.1).
- **Two wordings for "no active program"** (found in 3.3). The coach dashboard says "**No active program.** Completion counts start once a training program is active." and the scoring criteria page "No activities to score yet." under the title "No active program", while the participant dashboard, scoring overview, participant scoring page and admin dashboard now say "No training program is active yet." Decide whether to use one sentence everywhere (6.2 could align it). **Updated in 9.5:** the participant dashboard no longer shows "No training program is active yet." (it shows empty training lists instead). **Updated in 10.8:** the scoring overview and the participant scoring page no longer show it either (the overview lists session memberships; the participant page says "This training session has no weeks to score yet." for a session without weeks). **Updated in 10.9:** the coach dashboard no longer shows its notice (its counts cover the viewer's memberships of any program). **Resolved in 12.3:** the admin dashboard's notice went with its Training overview card, so "No training program is active yet." is shown nowhere; the scoring criteria page's "No active program" title (and "No activities to score yet.") is the only wording left. `EmptyTrainingDataTest` (scoring overview and participant scoring cases) and `ParticipantDashboardControllerTest` still `assertDontSee` the old sentence, which no page prints now; they were left as they are (harmless).
- **A video week with the video uploaded has no main action** (found in 3.4). Since 3.4 a participant's week page offers "View Gate →" as its main action in a week that does not ask for the weekly video, but a week that asks for it keeps today's behavior: "Upload Video Recording →" (since 8.14 "Choose Weekly Video →") until the video is uploaded (chosen from Drive since 8.12), then no main action (the Gate link stays on the Gate card). Decide whether such a week should also fall to "View Gate →" once the video is chosen.
- **Gate-level video surfaces still read inactive Gates** (found in 3.4). The week rule counts only active Gates, but `ParticipantGatePageService` (via `GateReadinessService::weeklyVideo()`, which does not check `is_active`) shows the Weekly Video Recording card and an "Upload Video Recording" main action on the participant Gate page of an inactive Gate with a `weekly_video` item, and `GateReviewController::participant()` lets a participant open an inactive Gate's page. If no active Gate of that week has the item, that upload link answers 404. The week page also summarizes the first Gate by position whether active or not. Only reachable by URL or with an inactive first Gate; decide whether inactive Gates should 404 on the participant Gate page (and be skipped by the week page's summary).
- **An optional weekly video item still asks for the video** (found in 4.1). **Resolved in admin-curriculum-editing 3.1:** `GateCriterion::scopeWeeklyVideo()` now keeps only items on the checklist (`is_required = true`, through `scopeOnChecklist()`), so a retired weekly video item no longer makes the week ask for the video in `TrainingWeek::asksForWeeklyVideo()`, `GateReadinessService` (readiness and the video state), the week page, dashboard, Gate pages and reports. `isWeeklyVideo()` stays type-only, because its callers (`CoachGateReviewPageService`, `StoreGateReviewRequest`) already work on required items. The Gate board's "criteria met" also counts only ticks on items still on the checklist. ~~Checklist items have no `is_active` column, so `L4TrainingProgramSeeder` retires an item it no longer defines by making it optional. The participant and mentor checklists show only required items, but the weekly video rule (`TrainingGate::asksForWeeklyVideo()` via `GateCriterion::weeklyVideo()`, `GateReadinessService`, `CoachGateReviewPageService`, `GateReviewController`) ignores `is_required`, so a week whose `weekly_video` item was dropped from the definition keeps asking for the video. This matters when 13.2 drops the video for some weeks. Decide whether the weekly video rule counts only required items (one constraint in the scope and the readers that filter `isWeeklyVideo()`), or whether retired items need their own flag.~~
- **Checklist items are matched by position** (found in 4.1). **Resolved in admin-curriculum-editing 1.1:** the seeder creates the program only when none of that name exists and never updates or retires existing rows, so no re-run moves ticks; admin edits address items by id. ~~As the Design says, the seeder upserts `gate_criteria` by (`training_gate_id`, `position`). Removing an item from the middle of a week's list moves every later item onto the row before it, so mentor ticks already stored for those rows (`gate_criterion_evaluations`) then belong to a different item, and the last row becomes optional. Harmless while only placeholders exist; decide before 13.2 replaces the placeholders on a database with reviews whether items should be matched by type and activity instead.~~
- **The participant dashboard Overview tab still describes the QA program** (found in 4.2). `participant/dashboard` hardcodes the "TRAINING OVERVIEW" card: the title "AI-Assisted QA Level 3", the QA learning goals, a Week 2 / Week 3 / Week 4 "Training journey" ("AI-Output Validation", …, "Sustain, Reuse, Reassess") and "A Coach must review and pass the Gate…". With L4 seeded it describes the wrong program; `HomeNavigationTest` and `AttendanceTest` still assert parts of it. 6.1 rewords QA text but does not say to replace program-specific content; decide whether the card shows the active program's name, description and weeks (from the database) or is removed, and in which batch.
- **4.3's account count may be off** (found in 4.2) — **Resolved in 4.3:** the count is 7 (3 participants, 3 mentors, 1 admin); 4.3's acceptance now says so. ~~4.3's acceptance says provisioning creates or updates "the 8 accounts", but the config now holds 5 (test participant, coach, admin, two demo participants) and 4.3 adds two coaches, which makes 7. Check the count when 4.3 runs.~~
- **The development baseline command still refuses staging** (found in 4.3). Since 4.3 `app:ensure-development-accounts` provisions the demo users in staging, but `app:ensure-development-baseline` (which runs `L4TrainingProgramSeeder`) still allows only local and testing. `db:seed` has no environment check, so staging can get the L4 program that way. Decide whether the baseline command should also allow staging (e.g. before 10.10 enrolls the demo users in the first session there). Since 10.10, `app:ensure-development-accounts` in staging skips the demo enrollment with a warning until the first session exists there (`db:seed --class=L4TrainingProgramSeeder --force` and `--class=L4FirstSessionSeeder --force`, as in production), then enrolls on its next run.
- **Only the participant can start a Google Drive sync** (found in 4.4). DAT-1's checklist item had an admin run a Drive sync per participant, but `POST /google-drive/sync` is `role:participant` and `TrainingSyncController` syncs the signed-in user, so the checklist asks each participant to sync before the release. A participant who does not sync loses their Drive copy of the QA workbooks and evidence (the CSVs, the database dump and the `storage/app/private` archive still hold the data). Decide whether that is acceptable or an admin-triggered sync is needed before the release.
- **The atomic release script has no migration step** (found in 4.4) — **Resolved on 27 Oct 2026:** the document got the procedure, not the script. `docs/AWS_DEPLOYMENT.md` §7 runs `php artisan migrate --force` from the new release directory between `prepare` and `activate`, states the expand/migrate/contract rule, and the script's `activate`/`rollback` now run `queue:restart`. The destructive QA-data migrations no longer apply: the production database starts empty. ~~`scripts/atomic-release.sh` never migrates, and `prepare`'s health check dies on pending migrations, so `docs/ATOMIC_DEPLOYMENT.md` does not say where or when migrations run; it assumes expand/migrate/contract, which this release's destructive migrations cannot follow (the old release breaks once 2.1 to 2.7 run, and the generic "activate the previous release" rollback no longer works). The release checklist works around it (maintenance mode on the live release, `php artisan migrate --force` from the new release directory, then `prepare` and `activate`). Decide whether the script gets a migrate step or the document a standard migration procedure.~~
- **The activity page still titles its requirements by path** (found in 5.1) — **Resolved in 5.3:** the tab is headed "Requirements for this activity" and the workbook lost its "QA Path" column. ~~Since 5.1 every participant sees every requirement, but `activities/show` still heads the Requirements tab "Requirements for {{ $user->trainingPathLabel() }} QA", which reads "Requirements for Unassigned QA" for a participant without a path and names a path the list no longer depends on. It is a `trainingPathLabel()` caller, so 5.3 removes it; the weekly workbook's "QA Path" column (`TrainingWorkbookService`) is the same case.~~
- **Two page values lost their last reader in 5.3** (found in 5.3). `TrainingActivityPageService::showData()` still passes `user` to `activities/show`, which no longer reads it, and `ActivitySubmissionController::show()` still eager-loads `submission.participant`, which only fed the removed path label on the submission summary. Decide whether to drop them (harmless; one extra query on the submission page).
- **The submission form's artifact types are still the QA list** (found in 5.3). `config('training.artifact_types')` (passed by `ActivitySubmissionPageService::editData()`) offers Test Case, Test Scenario, ... Test Automation Script, Regression Test Plan and other QA artifacts. It is not a training path, so 5.3 left it, and the Phase 6 grep (`\bQA\b|tester`) does not match it. Decide whether it becomes a developer list (or goes) in Phase 6.
- **`CLAUDE.md` still names `TrainingPath` as an example enum** (found in 5.4). The "Status, role and path values" rule says "stored statuses, roles and training paths are string-backed enums … (e.g. `SubmissionStatus`, `UserRole`, `TrainingPath`)", but 5.4 deleted the enum and the columns. Agents do not edit `CLAUDE.md`; decide whether to reword the rule to "Status and role values" with e.g. `JobRole` as the third example (the same text is quoted in `docs/CODE_QUALITY_PLAN.md`, which can stay as history).
- **`google-sheets-integration-audit.md` describes the dropped path columns** (found in 5.4). The audit at the repo root (outside `docs/`) still lists `users.training_path` (`manual` or `automation`) and `activity_requirements.participant_path` among the exported columns. 5.4 left it alone as old documentation; decide whether to update it, move it under `docs/`, or delete it.
- **The legal pages' "Last updated" date did not move with the 6.1 rewording** (found in 6.1). Both pages still say "Last updated: September 11, 2026", although 6.1 changed their wording and the privacy policy's list of account details (employee ID and job role instead of the QA path). Agents should not set a legal effective date; the platform owner should confirm the new text and the date to publish (the terms' governing-law placeholder is still open too).
- **`JobRole::QaTester` shows "QA Tester" to users** (found in 6.1). The job role options on registration, profile and the admin user form come from `JobRole::label()`, so they are outside 6.1's `resources/views` grep, but 6.2's `app` grep will match the case and its label. It is a real employee job role, not QA program wording; decide in 6.2 whether it stays (and is listed as kept) or is renamed. **Kept in 6.2:** the case, its stored value `qa_tester` and the label "QA Tester" are the only remaining matches of the 6.2 grep in `app`; changing the job-role list (or renaming a stored value users already hold) is a product decision for the platform owner.
- **The `asset` evidence type still shows "Reusable AI Asset" wording** (found in 6.2). The 6.2 grep (`\bQA\b|tester`) does not match it, but it is QA Week 4 wording: `SubmissionWorkflowService::validateSubmissionRequirements()` fails an unmet `asset` requirement with "Please upload your Reusable AI Asset before submitting Activity 1." (every other requirement gets "{title} is required before submission."), and `submissions/edit` shows a "REUSABLE AI ASSET" note on an `asset` requirement (`ActivitySubmissionPageService` `is_reusable_asset`). No L4 activity is expected to use `asset`; decide together with the `asset` evidence-type decision above whether the type goes or these strings become generic.
- **The dashboard's training overview is static copy** (found in 6.1). After 6.1 the Overview tab describes how any training works rather than a program, because the program and session descriptions are placeholders until 13.2. Decide whether 9.x (the session page) or 13.2 should show the program's or session's own description there instead. **Updated in 9.5:** the Overview tab moved, unchanged, to the session page (`training-sessions/show`), next to its week tabs; the landing page has no overview. The decision now is whether 13.2 replaces it there with the program's or session's description.
- **The development Mentor account is named "Development Coach"** (found in 6.3). `config/development_accounts.php` provisions `DEV-0002` with last name `Coach`, email `coach.dev@example.test` and password `CoachDev!2026`, so that account shows "Development Coach" in the sidebar and on the user list. 6.3 left it alone because it is account data, not UI copy, and renaming changes existing dev logins. Decide whether the dev account becomes "Development Mentor" (and whether its email and password change too; `DevelopmentAccountProvisioningTest` asserts them).
- **The participant's submission summary does not link GitHub deliverables** (found in 7.1). 7.1 links `github_url` values on the mentor review and scoring pages only; the participant's read-only submission page (`submissions/show`, built by `ActivitySubmissionPageService::showData()` without `SubmissionAnswerPresenter`) still prints the value as Markdown text. Decide whether it uses `SubmissionAnswerPresenter::fieldLinks()` too.
- **`url` submission fields are not checked as URLs on the server** (found in 7.1). `StoreSubmissionDraftRequest` gives a `url` field only `nullable|string|max:2048`, so any text saves through the autosave or a direct post; only the browser's `type="url"` check stops it. 7.1 left `url` fields unchanged ("other field types are unaffected"); decide whether they get the `url` rule.
- **`GateCriterionFactory` defaults to the legacy type `activity_completion`** (found in 7.2). Its `criterion_type` is not one of the `GateCriterion::TYPE_*` constants, and `TrainingWeekPageTest` and the Gate page tests still create items of that type. Nothing reads it, so it behaves like a deliverable item; decide whether the factory default becomes `GateCriterion::TYPE_DELIVERABLE` (with a `weeklyVideo()` / `sessionLogs()` state).
- **Older `Failed` Gate reviews** (found in 7.3). — **Resolved in 11.1:** a `failed` review counts as incomplete (`GateEvaluationStatus::incompleteReviews()` = `NeedsRevision`, `Failed`), so it locks the later weeks until the week is marked completed; no data migration. Reverse it by dropping `Failed` from that list if the user prefers a migration that rewrites them to `needs_revision`. 7.3 stops offering `Failed` but keeps the case, so any stored `failed` review still shows "Failed" on the board and participant pages, and on the review page no decision is preselected (Completed shows first). 11.1 locks later weeks only after an *incomplete* (`NeedsRevision`) review, so a `failed` one would neither lock nor count as completed. DAT-2 deleted all QA training data, so there should be none; decide whether 11.1 treats `failed` as incomplete or a data migration rewrites any to `needs_revision`.
- **The "requirements incomplete" reminder follows readiness, not the checklist review** (found in 7.3). `ParticipantNotificationService` raises `GateIncomplete` ("Week N requirements incomplete") while the week has readiness reasons and no completed review, and an *incomplete* review raises no notification of its own (the participant learns of it only on the Gate page). Decide whether an incomplete review should notify the participant with its missing items (fits 11.3).
- **`AdminUserListPageTest` search case is flaky** (found in 7.3). `test_filters_keep_their_values_and_an_empty_result_shows_a_notice_instead_of_the_table` searches "Ava" and asserts "Max Manual" is absent, but the search also matches email and employee ID, so a Faker email containing "ava" (e.g. "java", "ava.") makes it fail now and then (seen once in the 7.3 full run, passed on rerun). Give the factory users fixed emails in that test.
- **AI scoring and submission checks run only when a mentor or admin starts them** (found in the hosting discussion, 2026-10-23). **Decided 2026-10-23:** no scheduled or automatic runs. The AI scoring suggestion already works this way (the mentor presses the button on the scoring page, `scoring.suggestion` → `ScoringController::generateSuggestion()`), but it calls the provider inside the web request (60s timeout per call). The future submission checks (the AI checker for GitHub deliverables, DEV-5, and any Google Drive check, e.g. that a week's video is in Drive) follow the same rule: a "Run check" action queues a job and the page shows its progress and result, so the request never waits on GitHub, Drive or the AI provider. Neither check has a spec yet; their specs start from this. Decide whether the scoring suggestion also moves to a queued job when the checks are built, so both behave the same.
- **Weekly videos live in Google Drive, but the app still accepts video uploads** (found in the hosting discussion, 2026-10-23). Participants upload their weekly videos to Google Drive, not to the app, yet DEV-3 keeps "weekly video recordings" and the app still takes uploads up to 350 MB onto the local disk (`WeeklyVideoRecordingController`, `EvidenceFilePolicy`, `training.weekly_video_max_kb`), with the `weekly_video` checklist item checked against those uploads (3.4, `GateReadinessService`). The app's Google permission (`drive.file`) cannot see files a participant uploaded to Drive themselves. **Decided 2026-10-23:** each participant uploads their weekly video to their **own** Google Drive; the app stores no video file (so production needs no file storage or 350 MB upload limits for it, and no shared folder or service account is involved). **Also decided 2026-10-23:** the participant chooses the video with the **Google Picker** (not a pasted link), the same Picker the profile already uses for the Drive folder (`initializeGoogleDrivePicker`, `GOOGLE_PICKER_API_KEY` and the project number as app ID), limited to video files. Choosing a file gives the app `drive.file` access to that one file through the participant's existing Drive connection, so the app stores its Drive file ID, reads its name, type, size and date to confirm it is a video, gives the participant's mentor viewer access, and lets the later manually started Drive check read it. This needs the participant's Google Drive connection (Drive enabled in production) and the OAuth consent screen set up for `drive.file`. Still to decide in the batch: whether the mentor gets access per email (and again when 10.3 changes the mentor) or the file is shared with the company domain; and what the page shows when the participant disconnects Drive or deletes the file. The in-app upload goes (`WeeklyVideoRecordingController` upload and preview, `EvidenceFilePolicy` video rules, `training.weekly_video_max_kb`, `initializeWeeklyVideoPreviews` / `initializeWeeklyVideoSizeLimit`, and the video part of `SyncTrainingDataJob`), the `weekly_video` checklist item and readiness read the chosen Drive file instead of an upload, and the DP spec (DEV-3) and the plan are updated to match. (8.9 adds `App\Support\GoogleDriveLink`, which a Drive-link video field could reuse to recognize and preview the link.) **Scheduled 2026-10-23 as 8.10 to 8.17** (Design and Assumptions → Weekly videos in Google Drive). The DP spec now has DEV-3 reworded and VID-1 to VID-6. **Answered 2026-10-23:** mentor access is a one-time view-only share with the company domain (Q5, 8.16), and video evidence becomes link-only (Q7, 8.17). Disconnect and deletion behavior is still open as Q6, which does not block anything: 8.12 and 8.14 apply the recommendation, no Drive reads on page views plus a help line. Existing rows and their files are deleted by 8.12's migration. Drive setup for production goes into the release checklist in 8.15.
- **Session weeks skip inactive program weeks** (found in 8.1). The Design defines `TrainingSession::weeks()` as the program's weeks with `week_number <= weeks_count`; 8.1 also requires `is_active` (in `weeks()` and `includesWeek()`), following the assumption that weeks count the program's *active* weeks and that the L4 seeder deactivates weeks it no longer defines. So a session whose program has an inactive week 3 shows weeks 1, 2 and 4 (when `weeks_count` is 4 or more), and the 9.x `session.week` middleware will 404 an inactive week. Decide whether that is right, or whether `weeks_count` should be capped at (and 8.2's form validated against) the number of active weeks.
- **`ends_on` is computed only in `TrainingSessionFactory`** (found in 8.1) — **Resolved in 8.2:** `TrainingSessionService::endsOn()` owns the rule; `TrainingSessionService::create()`, `StoreTrainingSessionRequest` and `TrainingSessionFactory` (`definition()` and `startingOn()`) all call it. ~~The rule `starts_on + weeks_count × 7 − 1 day` lives in the factory's `startingOn()` until 8.2's `TrainingSessionService` computes it on create and update; 8.2 should own the rule (e.g. a static helper the factory then calls) so the two cannot drift.~~
- **Older admin actions still write audit rows directly** (found in 8.2). `AdminAuditService::record()` is used by the session actions only. `AdminUserController::audit()`, `AdminGoogleDriveController::audit()`, `AdminAiSettingsController::update()` and `BootstrapAdminCommand` still call `AdminAuditLog::query()->create()` themselves. 8.2 left them alone. Decide whether to move them onto the service (behavior stays the same).
- **8.8 has no admin to pass to `TrainingSessionService::create()`** (found in 8.2). `create()` takes the creating admin, sets `created_by` and records `training_session_created` with that admin as `actor_id`, which is not nullable. `L4FirstSessionSeeder` (8.8) runs without a signed-in admin. Decide whether the seeder passes an existing admin (e.g. the bootstrapped one) or `create()` takes a nullable creator and skips the audit row when it is null. **Decided 2026-10-23:** `create()` takes a nullable creator (`?User $creator`). With no creator, `created_by` stays null and no audit row is written (seeding is not an admin action). The seeder passes null; admin-driven creation is unchanged and still audited. Applied in 8.8.
- **The session list rows do not link to the session yet** (found in 8.2) — **Resolved in 8.4:** each row's title links to `admin.sessions.show`, and `update` (and the edit form's Cancel) return to the session dashboard; `store` still returns to the list. ~~`admin/sessions/index` shows the title as plain text because `admin.sessions.show` arrives in 8.4. 8.4 should link each row's title to the session dashboard (8.7 already links the dashboard card's rows). Since 8.3 each row has an Edit button; 8.4 may also send `update` back to the dashboard instead of the list.~~
- **A session whose program was deactivated cannot be edited** (found in 8.3). The update request keeps the create rules, so `program_id` must be an *active* program: once a session's program is deactivated, saving any change (even the title) fails with "The selected program is invalid.", and with participants the program cannot be switched to an active one either. The form also lists active programs only, so the select shows no matching option for it (the locked, disabled select shows the first active program while the hidden input still submits the real one). Decide whether the update allows the session's current program even when inactive (and how its week maximum is counted), and whether the form adds it to the options.
- **The add participants select lists every candidate at once** (found in 8.5). The Participants tab's native `<select multiple>` offers every active participant not in the session, unpaginated and unsearchable, and choosing several needs Ctrl / Cmd. That is fine for the first session's size; decide whether a larger user base needs a searchable picker (would be a JS initializer in `resources/js/app.js`).
- **Participants can be added to and removed from completed sessions** (found in 8.5). Neither the spec nor the Design limits the admin's add and remove by session status (only `join` excludes completed sessions). 8.5 allows both at any status. Decide whether a completed session's membership should be frozen.
- **Two admins adding the same participant at once hit the unique key** (found in 8.5). The "already in this session" check runs before the insert, so a simultaneous second add fails on the (`training_session_id`, `user_id`) unique index with a server error instead of a validation message. Decide whether that is worth handling (e.g. `insertOrIgnore`-style attach and a softer message).
- **Upload-too-large messages are lost in a real request** (found in 8.6). **Resolved 2026-10-23 for session resources:** resources are links only (8.9), which removes their `PostTooLargeException` branch. **Resolved 2026-10-23 for the weekly video by 8.12 and 8.15:** videos are chosen from Drive (8.12), and 8.15 deleted the handler, so `bootstrap/app.php` has no `PostTooLargeException` handling left. The note below now applies only to evidence uploads over `post_max_size`, which never had a handler (the deleted one returned `null` for every other request, so their behavior is unchanged: Laravel's default 413 response). **Original note (weekly video upload):** `ValidatePostSize` is global middleware, so a body over PHP's `post_max_size` is rejected before the `web` group's `StartSession` runs. The `PostTooLargeException` handlers in `bootstrap/app.php` (the existing weekly video one and 8.6's session resources one) flash their message, but the session is neither started nor saved, so the admin (or participant) lands on the page without it; the video handler's `redirect()->back()` also depends on the Referer header for the same reason. `AdminTrainingSessionResourceTest` therefore asserts only the redirect to `#resources`. Decide whether to carry the message another way (e.g. a query flag the page reads) or to rely on the client-side size check (the video page has one; the resources form does not yet).
- **PHP's upload limits are below the 100 MB resource limit** (found in 8.6). **Resolved 2026-10-23:** resources are links only (8.9), so `training.session_resource_max_kb` goes. **Resolved 2026-10-23 for the weekly video by 8.12 and 8.15:** the weekly video no longer uploads (8.12), and 8.15's release checklist step says it needs no PHP upload limit. **It still applies to evidence uploads** (`training.evidence_max_kb`); the checklist step names it. **Resolved for video evidence by 8.17:** it is a link (Q7), and `training.weekly_video_max_kb` is gone. **Before 8.12 it applied to the weekly video (350 MB) and evidence uploads:** PHP's `upload_max_filesize` / `post_max_size` and the web server's body limit must exceed `training.weekly_video_max_kb` and `training.evidence_max_kb` on production; add them to the deploy checklist. Original note: The local CLI PHP (Herd Lite) has `post_max_size` 8M and `upload_max_filesize` 2M (the web server's PHP was not checked), so with those values a resource over 2 MB fails before validation (`file.uploaded`, "The file failed to upload.") and one over 8 MB hits the handler above. Production needs both raised above 100 MB (and the web server's body limit) for `training.session_resource_max_kb` to apply; add it to the deploy checklist with the existing evidence and video limits.
- **Office files may be rejected by MIME detection** (found in 8.6). **Resolved 2026-10-23:** resources are links only (8.9), so `session_resource_mimes` goes. ~~`session_resource_mimes` lists the standard Office MIME types, like `evidence_mimes`, but `fileinfo` can report older `.doc`/`.xls`/`.ppt` files as `application/CDFV2` or `application/x-ole-storage` and some `.docx`/`.xlsx`/`.pptx` files as `application/zip` or `application/octet-stream`; such uploads fail with the "must be a PDF, Word, …" message. Not checked with real files (the tests use fakes). Decide whether to accept those generic types for the Office extensions.~~
- **Shrinking a session leaves resources of weeks it no longer has** (found in 8.6). Editing a session to fewer weeks (8.3) does not touch its resources, so a Week 4 resource of a session cut to 3 weeks still lists under "Week 4" on the admin tab and, since 9.6, on the participant session page's Resources tab. Decide whether the update should refuse to drop weeks that have resources, move them to the whole session, or leave them. Since 11.6, such a resource follows the week rule by its date (`weekOpensOn(N)`), or opens early once week *N − 1* is completed when that is still a session week, and opens for everyone once the session ends.
- **Viewers without Drive access, and browsers that block third-party cookies** (found while planning 8.9). The app cannot check a Drive file's sharing (`drive.file` scope). When the viewer lacks access, the preview iframe shows Google's sign-in or "You need access" page, or the browser's "refused to connect" message, depending on the browser and Google's framing rules; 8.9 prints a fixed line under every preview ("Preview not loading? Open it in Drive. If Drive says you need access, ask an admin to share the file.") and the add form tells admins to share the file. Separately, browsers that block third-party cookies (Safari by default, Chrome with that setting) may not send the viewer's Google sign-in into the iframe, so a file shared only with the company domain may not preview there even for people who have access, while "Anyone with the link" files do; "Open in Drive" works in both cases. **Recommendation:** ship 8.9 as planned; have the add form's hint recommend "Anyone with the link" when an in-app preview matters, and revisit only if participants report blank previews. A sharing check (service account or a broader scope) is out of scope.
- **Evidence uploads still go to the local disk** (found while planning 8.10 to 8.17). Production runs on AWS Elastic Beanstalk with no persistent file storage, which is one reason weekly videos move to Drive. `SubmissionEvidenceController` / `SubmissionEvidenceService` still store activity evidence files (up to `training.evidence_max_kb`; video evidence is a link since 8.17) on the private `local` disk, which a redeploy or a new instance loses. The L4 placeholders have no evidence requirements, so nothing uses it today, but 13.2's real curriculum might. Decide before production whether evidence becomes link-only, like resources and videos, or moves to persistent storage (e.g. S3). The same applies to the workbooks `SyncTrainingDataJob` builds in temporary files (those are not kept, so they are fine).
- **The domain-shared video preview may not load in browsers that block third-party cookies** (found while planning 8.14). Like 8.9's resources (entry above), a video shared with the company domain (Q5) needs the viewer's Google sign-in inside the iframe, so the in-app preview can stay blank in Safari while "Open in Drive" works. 8.14 keeps "Open in Drive" next to every preview; revisit only if mentors report blank previews.
- **The activity page's "Connect Google Drive" link still lands on the profile** (found in 8.13). `activities/show` links to `profile.google-drive.connect` without `return_to`, although the activity page is under `/training/` and 8.13's `ConnectGoogleDriveRequest::returnTo()` would accept it. Decide whether it should pass its own relative URL too (one line in the view or its page data, plus a test).
- **The weekly video share domain is not on the admin Google Drive page** (found in 8.16). Every other Google Drive key (client ID, secret, redirect URI, enabled, Picker API key, project number) can be saved on the admin **Google Drive Integration** page, where a saved value wins over `.env`; `GOOGLE_DRIVE_SHARE_DOMAIN` is read from config only, as 8.16 said. Decide whether admins should be able to set (and see) it there too.
- **A shared weekly video does not record which domain it was shared with** (found in 8.16). `weekly_video_recordings` keeps `drive_permission_id` and `shared_at` only, so the mentor's "Shared with {domain}" names the domain configured now. If `GOOGLE_DRIVE_SHARE_DOMAIN` changes, earlier videos stay shared with the old domain while the page names the new one, and nothing re-shares them. Decide whether to store the domain with the share (a `shared_domain` column) or treat a domain change as a manual re-share.
- **9.1's required `training_session_id` breaks every production write path** (found in 9.1) — **Resolved 2026-10-23 with option (a)**, as decided by the user's coordinator: 9.1 adds the column nullable (foreign key, restrict on delete; the empty-table check, per-session keys and index unchanged) and 9.4 makes it not null in a new migration. NULLs are distinct in unique indexes, so the per-session keys do not cover NULL-session rows meanwhile; the per-user `updateOrCreate` / `firstOrNew` lookups still prevent duplicates. ~~With the column `not null`, every place that creates one of the three rows fails until 9.3 and 9.4 pass the session: `SubmissionWorkflowService::saveDraft()` (new attempt, `ActivitySubmission::create`), the retries in `SubmissionReviewService` and `RecoveryRequestController` (both `ActivitySubmission::create`), `GateReviewService::record()` (`GateEvaluation::updateOrCreate`) and `WeeklyVideoRecordingService::choose()` (`firstOrNew` + `save`). That is 29 failing tests: `ParticipantWorkflowTest` (6), `SubmissionFormPageTest` (2), `DraftFieldLimitTest`, `DevelopmentAccountProvisioningTest`, `ScoringTest` (2, call `saveDraft()` directly), `CoachOperationsTest` (2 retries), `CoachRecoveryRequestPageTest`, `CoachGateReviewPageTest` (4), `ParticipantNotificationTriggersTest`, `GateReadinessTest`, `WeeklyVideoRecordingTest` (7) and `WeeklyVideoRecordingPageTest`. Only the retries could copy the session from the earlier attempt; the other three have no session to use until 9.2 to 9.4 nest their routes under one, so 9.1 cannot ship green on its own. Options: (a) add the column nullable in 9.1 and make it `not null` in a new migration at the end of 9.4; (b) implement 9.1 to 9.4 as one deployable change (9.1 merged only with 9.4); (c) an interim "participant's session for this program" lookup in the three services, which 9.3 and 9.4 then replace (new behavior no batch asks for).~~ Still unverified: the migration has run only on SQLite (the suite, including its `down()` and `up()` in `SessionScopedRecordsTest`); on MySQL it relies on the single-column indexes it adds on `activity_id`, `training_gate_id` and `user_id` to back those foreign keys before the old keys are dropped.
- **Do not deploy 9.1 to 9.3 to production without 9.4** (found in 9.1) — **Resolved by 9.4:** Gate reviews and weekly videos are written with their session, and 9.4's migration makes `training_session_id` not null on all three tables (it refuses to run, naming the tables, while a row has no session). ~~Since 9.3, new submissions and retries carry their session; until 9.4, Gate reviews and weekly videos are still written with a NULL `training_session_id`, and 9.4's not-null migration refuses to run while such rows exist.~~
- **Interim week links through the participant's session** (found in 9.2). The week and activity pages now need a session, but the participant dashboard (until 9.5), the participant notifications (until 9.9) and the weekly video page's Back link (until 9.4) do not carry one. 9.2 adds `ParticipantWeekSessionService`, which picks the participant's session of the week's program that includes the week: not-completed sessions first, earliest start first (active before upcoming), then completed sessions, latest start first. Since 9.3 the dashboard also takes each week's activity progress from that session's attempts only. Without such a session the dashboard shows the week with no Open week or activity buttons, no activity progress and no message, notifications for that week's activities (including the submission page links since 9.3) and the "Week N completed" event have no link, and the video page's Back goes to the dashboard. A participant in two sessions of the program only reaches the preferred one from these pages. Delete the service once 9.4, 9.5 and 9.9 carry the session. **Updated in 9.4:** the weekly video page now carries its session (Back goes to the week in it).
